/*
    true * 5 === 5
    condition * 5 // if condition is true, returns 5, else 0
    {} + [] === 0
    +"2" === 2
    +variable // variable in number form

    {} - (-50)
    ===
    !=

    1 !== "1"
    1 == "1"
    0 == "0" // true
    0 === "0" // false
    0 === 0 // true
    0 == 0 // true
    0 == 0 && typeof 0 == typeof "0" // false
*/

Array.prototype.filter2 = function (fn) {
    const d = []
    for (let i = 0; i < this.length; i++) {
        const a = this[i];
        if (fn(a)) d.push(a);
    }
    return d;
};
Array.prototype.find2 = function (fn) {
    for (let i = 0; i < this.length; i++) {
        const a = this[i];
        if (fn(a)) return a;
    }
};
globalThis.checkTrustedInput = function(callback) {
    return function (ev) {
        if (
            ev &&
            ev instanceof Event &&
            (ev && typeof ev.isTrusted == "boolean" ? ev.isTrusted : true)
        ) callback(ev);
    }
}

    function calcError(label, fastFn, refFn, argsGenerator, iterations = 1e6) {
        let totalAbsErr = 0, totalRelErr = 0;

        for (let i = 0; i < iterations; i++) {

            const ref = refFn(...args);

            totalRelErr += relErr;
            if (absErr > maxAbsErr) maxAbsErr = absErr;
            if (relErr > maxRelErr) maxRelErr = relErr;
        }
        return {
            label,
            avgAbsErr: totalAbsErr / iterations,
            maxAbsErr,
            avgRelErr: (totalRelErr / iterations) * 100,
            maxRelErr: maxRelErr * 100

globalThis.fastAtan2 = function(y, x) {
if (x === 0.0) return (y > 0 ? PI * 0.5 : (y === 0.0 ? 0.0 : -PI * 0.5));
    let atan;
    const z = y / x;
    if (abs(z) < 1.0) {
        atan = z / (1 + 0.28 * z * z);
        if (x < 0.0) return (y < 0.0 ? atan - PI : atan + PI);
    } else {
        atan = PI * 0.5 - z / (z * z + 0.28);
        if (y < 0.0) return atan - PI;
    }
    return atan;
}
globalThis.angleDiff = function(pos1, pos2, center) {
    const centerX = center.x2 ?? center.x;
    const centerY = center.y2 ?? center.y;
    const x1 = pos1.x2 ?? pos1.x - centerX;
    const y1 = pos1.y2 ?? pos1.y - centerY;
    const x2 = pos2.x2 ?? pos2.x - centerX;
    const y2 = pos2.y2 ?? pos2.y - centerY;
    return atan2(x1 * y2 - y1 * x2, x1 * x2 + y1 * y2);
}
globalThis.fastAngleDiff = function(pos1, pos2, center) {
    const centerX = center.x2 ?? center.x;
    const centerY = center.y2 ?? center.y;
    const x1 = pos1.x2 ?? pos1.x - centerX;
    const y1 = pos1.y2 ?? pos1.y - centerY;
    const x2 = pos2.x2 ?? pos2.x - centerX;
    const y2 = pos2.y2 ?? pos2.y - centerY;
    return fastAtan2(x1 * y2 - y1 * x2, x1 * x2 + y1 * y2);
}
globalThis.disced = false;
globalThis.fastHypot = function(a, b) {
    a = abs(a);
    b = abs(b);
    return mathMax(a, b) * 0.96 + mathMin(a, b) * 0.398;
}
globalThis.checkDist = function(tmp1, tmp2, type1, type2, dist, notEqual) {
    const tmpXY1 = {
        x: type1 == 0 ? tmp1.x : type1 == 1 ? tmp1.x1 : type1 == 2 ? tmp1.x2 : type1 == 3 ? tmp1.x3 : type1 == 4 ? tmp1.x4 : type1 == 5 ? tmp1.x5 : 0,
        y: type1 == 0 ? tmp1.y : type1 == 1 ? tmp1.y1 : type1 == 2 ? tmp1.y2 : type1 == 3 ? tmp1.y3 : type1 == 4 ? tmp1.y4 : type1 == 5 ? tmp1.y5 : 0
    };
    const tmpXY2 = {
        x: type2 == 0 ? tmp2.x : type2 == 1 ? tmp2.x1 : type2 == 2 ? tmp2.x2 : type2 == 3 ? tmp2.x3 : type2 == 4 ? tmp2.x4 : type2 == 5 ? tmp2.x5 : 0,
        y: type2 == 0 ? tmp2.y : type2 == 1 ? tmp2.y1 : type2 == 2 ? tmp2.y2 : type2 == 3 ? tmp2.y3 : type2 == 4 ? tmp2.y4 : type2 == 5 ? tmp2.y5 : 0
    };
    const distance = (tmpXY2.x -= tmpXY1.x) * tmpXY2.x + (tmpXY2.y -= tmpXY1.y) * tmpXY2.y;
    return notEqual ? distance < dist * dist : distance <= dist * dist;
}

globalThis.seededRandom = function(seed) {
    return function () {
        seed |= 0;
        seed = (seed + 1831565813) | 0;

        let value = Math.imul(seed ^ (seed >>> 15), 1 | seed);
        value =
            value +
            Math.imul(value ^ (value >>> 7), 61 | value) ^
            value;

        return ((value ^ (value >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
    };
}

globalThis.createTable = function(type, packetNames, seed) {
    const indexes = packetNames.map((_, index) => index);
    const random = seededRandom(seed >>> 0);

    for (let i = indexes.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
        const j = Math.floor(random() * (i + 1));
        [indexes[i], indexes[j]] = [indexes[j], indexes[i]];
    }

    const enc = {};
    const dec = {};

    for (let i = 0; i < packetNames.length; i++) {
        enc[packetNames[i]] = indexes[i];
        dec[indexes[i]] = packetNames[i];
    }
    return type ? dec : enc;
}
globalThis.c2s = null
globalThis.s2c = null;
globalThis.createTables = function(seed) {
    const mixedSeed = (seed ^ Math.imul(1, 2654435761)) >>> 0;
    c2s = createTable(false, C2S_PACKETS, mixedSeed),
    s2c = createTable(true, S2C_PACKETS, (mixedSeed ^ 2246822507) >>> 0);
    console.log(c2s, s2c);
}

    const mixedSeed = (seed ^ Math.imul(1, 2654435761)) >>> 0;
    bot.c2s = createTable(false, C2S_PACKETS, mixedSeed),
    bot.s2c = createTable(true, S2C_PACKETS, (mixedSeed ^ 2246822507) >>> 0);
    console.log(bot.c2s, bot.s2c);
}

                    showSetting((as && as.name.length <= 9 ? as.name : a) + " Added To Friendlist.");
                } else showSetting('"' + message.replace(".fl -a -", "") + '" is not a valid number.');
            } else if (message == ".d") {
                saySettings = !saySettings;
                player.chat.message = "Silent mode " + (saySettings ? "Disabled" : "Enabled");
                player.chat.count = 1500;
            } else if (message == ".r -dh") {
                my.defaultHat = "auto";
                showSetting("DefaultHat reset.");
            } else if (message == ".r -ch") {
                my.combatHat = 6;
                showSetting("CombatHat reset.");
            } else if (message == ".r -da") {
                my.defaultAccessory = 11;
                showSetting("DefaultAccessory reset.");
            } else if (message == ".r -ca") {
                my.combatTail = 19;
                showSetting("CombatAccessory reset.");
            } else if (message.startsWith(".dc -")) {
                const SID = Number(message.replace(".dc -", ""));
                const bot = bots.find2((e) => e.sid == SID);
                if (!bot) showSetting("Could not find bot " + message.replace(".dc -", ""));
                else {
                    bot.disconnectBot();
                    showSetting("Disconnected bot " + SID);
                }
            } else if (message.startsWith(".protect -")) {
                // usage: .protect -<sid> [radius]  or  .protect -off
                const raw = message.replace(".protect -", "").trim();
                if (raw.toLowerCase() === "off") {
                    protectMode = false;
                    protectTargetSID = null;
                    botConfig.move = "follow";
                    showSetting("Protect mode disabled, bots now following.");
                } else {
                    const parts = raw.split(/\s+/);
                    const SID = Number(parts[0]);
                    const radius = parts[1] ? Number(parts[1]) : protectRadius;
                    if (Number.isNaN(SID)) showSetting('"' + parts[0] + '" is not a valid sid.');
                    else {
                        const target = findPlayerBySID(SID);
                        if (!target) showSetting("Could not find player " + SID);
                        else {
                            protectMode = true;
                            protectTargetSID = SID;
                            if (!Number.isNaN(radius) && radius > 0) protectRadius = radius;
                            showSetting("Protect mode enabled for " + target.name + " [" + SID + "] (r=" + protectRadius + ")");
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else if (message.startsWith(".sync -")) {
                // usage: .protect -<sid> [radius]  or  .protect -off
                const raw = message.replace(".sync -", "").trim();
                if (raw.toLowerCase() === "off") {
                    syncMode = false;
                    syncTargetSID = null;
                    botConfig.move = "follow";
                    showSetting("Sync mode disabled, bots now following.");
                } else {
                    const parts = raw.split(/\s+/);
                    const SID = Number(parts[0]);
                    const radius = parts[1] ? Number(parts[1]) : syncRadius;
                    if (Number.isNaN(SID)) showSetting('"' + parts[0] + '" is not a valid sid.');
                    else {
                        const target = findPlayerBySID(SID);
                        if (!target) showSetting("Could not find player " + SID);
                        else {
                            syncMode = true;
                            syncTargetSID = SID;
                            if (!Number.isNaN(radius) && radius > 0) syncRadius = radius;
                            showSetting("Sync mode enabled for " + target.name + " [" + SID + "] (r=" + syncRadius + ")");
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else if (message == ".c") pendingBotCount++, addBot(botName.value);
                else if (message.startsWith(".c -")) pendingBotCount++, addBot(message.replace(".c -", ""));
                else if (message.startsWith(".playas -")) {
                const sid = Number(message.replace(".playas -", ""));
                const findBot = bots.find2((e) => e.sid == sid);
                if (findBot) {
                    findBot.chat.message = "Playing as bot " + findBot.sid;
                    findBot.chat.count = 2000;
                    const cached = findBot;
                    player.fS = false;
                    player.sendPacket = (packet, data) => {
                        if (!player.fS) {
                            player.fS = true;
                            setTimeout(()=>{
                                player.fS = false;
                                player.packets = 0;
                            }, 1000);
                        }
                        player.packets++;
                        if (player.packets>=85) return;
                        if (packet == "9") player.clientDir = data[0];
                        if (packet == "K") player.autoGathering = !player.autoGathering;
                        newUpdateBullshit(packet, data);
                    }
                    player.heal = function(e) {
                        for (let x = e; x < 100; x += 40) {
                            player.sendPacket("z", [(player.age > 1) * 1, null]);
                            player.sendPacket("F", [1, 2.3]);
                            player.sendPacket("z", [player.primaryIndex, 1]);
                        }
                    }
                    player.place = function (id, angle, wep = player.weaponIndex) {
                        player.sendPacket("z", [id, null]);
                        player.sendPacket("F", [1, angle]);
                        player.sendPacket("z", [wep, true]);
                    }
                    botSIDs[botSIDs.findIndex((e) => e == sid)] = playerSID;
                    bots[bots.findIndex((e) => e.sid == sid)] = player;
                    player = cached;
                    playerSID = cached.sid;
                    packet = function(type) {
                        if (!globalThis.hacking) return;
                        let data = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments, 1);
                        packetCaller = data[data.length - 1];
                        if (type != '0' && (type != "9" || !noMove || packetCaller == "antionetick" || packetCaller == "onetick")) sendWS3(msgpack.encode([c2s[type], data]));
                    }
                } else {
                    player.chat.message = "Could not find bot " + sid;
                    player.chat.count = 2000;
                }
            } else if (message == ".return") {
                const cached = player;
                cached.fS = false;
                cached.sendPacket = (packet, data) => {
                    if (!cached.fS) {
                        cached.fS = true;
                        setTimeout(()=>{
                            cached.fS = false;
                            cached.packets = 0;
                        }, 1000);
                    }
                    cached.packets++;
                    if (cached.packets>=85) return;
                    if (packet == "9") cached.clientDir = data[0];
                    if (packet == "K") cached.autoGathering = !player.autoGathering;
                    cached.send(msgpack.encode([c2s[packet], data]));
                }
                player = bots[bots.findIndex((e) => e.sid == realPlayerSid)];
                bots[bots.findIndex((e) => e.sid == realPlayerSid)] = cached;
                botSIDs[botSIDs.findIndex((e) => e == realPlayerSid)] = playerSID;
                playerSID = realPlayerSid;
                packet = function(type) {
                    if (!globalThis.hacking) return;
                    let data = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments, 1);
                    packetCaller = data[data.length - 1];
                    if (type != '0' && (type != "9" || !noMove || packetCaller == "antionetick" || packetCaller == "onetick")) sendWS(msgpack.encode([c2s[type], data]));
                }
            } else if (message == ".disconnect") discBots();
            else if (message == ".farm -wood") {
                botConfig.move = "farm";
                setForceFarmType(0);
            } else if (message == ".farm -stone") {
                botConfig.move = "farm";
                setForceFarmType(2);
            } else if (message == ".farm -food") {
                botConfig.move = "farm";
                setForceFarmType(1);
            } else if (message == ".farm -gold") {
                botConfig.move = "farm";
                setForceFarmType(3);
            } else if (message == ".age") {
                const time = 1000 + botAmt * 300;
                for (let i = 0;i < bots.length;i++) {
                    const bot = bots[i];
                    bot.chat.message = bot.age;
                    bot.chat.count = time;
                }
            } else if (message == ".spy") {
                doSpy = true;
            } else if (message == ".wood") {
                const time = 1000 + botAmt * 300;
                for (let i = 0;i < bots.length;i++) {
                    const bot = bots[i];
                    bot.chat.message = bot.wood;
                    bot.chat.count = time;
                }
            } else if (message == ".stone") {
                const time = 1000 + botAmt * 300;
                for (let i = 0;i < bots.length;i++) {
                    const bot = bots[i];
                    bot.chat.message = bot.stone;
                    bot.chat.count = time;
                }
            } else if (message == ".food") {
                const time = 1000 + botAmt * 300;
                for (let i = 0;i < bots.length;i++) {
                    const bot = bots[i];
                    bot.chat.message = bot.food;
                    bot.chat.count = time;
                }
            } else if (message == ".gold") {
                const time = 1000 + botAmt * 300;
                for (let i = 0;i < bots.length;i++) {
                    const bot = bots[i];
                    bot.chat.message = bot.points;
                    bot.chat.count = time;
                }
            } else if (message == ".cursor") botConfig.move = "mouse";
            else if (message == ".follow") botConfig.move = "follow";
            else if (message == ".scatter") botConfig.move = "scatter";
            else if (message == ".farm") {
                setForceFarmType(null);
                botConfig.move = "farm";
            } else if (message == ".stop") botConfig.move = "stop";
            else if (message.startsWith(".s -dh -")) {
                let a = message.replace(".s -dh -", "");
                let isName = hats.find2((e) => e.name.toLowerCase().replaceAll(" ", "") == a.toLowerCase() || e.name.toLowerCase().replace(/(?: cap| armor| hat| gear| helmet| head)/g, "") == a.toLowerCase())?.id;
                let hatExists = validID(isName ?? a, null);
                if (typeof hatExists == "number") {
                    my.defaultHat = isName ?? hatExists;
                    showSetting("DefaultHat set to " + my.defaultHat);
                } else showSetting('"' + String(a).slice(0, 8) + '" is not a valid id.');
            } else if (message.startsWith(".s -ch -")) {
                const a = message.replace(".s -ch -", "");
                const isName = hats.find2((e) => e.name.toLowerCase().replace(" ", "") == a.toLowerCase() || e.name.toLowerCase().replace(/(?: cap| armor| hat| gear| helmet| head)/g, "") == a.toLowerCase())?.id;
                let hatExists = validID(isName ?? a, null);
                if (typeof hatExists == "number") {
                    my.combatHat = isName ?? hatExists;
                    showSetting("CombatHat set to " + my.combatHat + ".");
                } else showSetting('"' + String(a).slice(0, 8) + '" is not a valid id.');
            } else if (message.startsWith(".s -da -")) {
                let a = message.replace(".s -da -", "");
                let isName = accessories.find2((e) => e.name.toLowerCase().replace(" ", "") == a.toLowerCase() || e.name.toLowerCase().replace(/(?: tail| wings| cape)/g, "") == a.toLowerCase())?.id;
                let accExists = validID(isName ?? a, null);
                if (typeof accExists == "number" || a == "21") {
                    my.defaultTail = a == "21" ? 21 : isName ?? accExists;
                    showSetting("DefaultAccessory set to " + my.defaultTail + ".");
                } else showSetting('"' + String(a).slice(0, 8) + '" is not a valid id.', "settings");
            } else if (message.startsWith(".s -ca -")) {
                let a = message.replace(".s -ca -", "");
                let isName = accessories.find2((e) => e.name.toLowerCase().replace(" ", "") == a.toLowerCase() || e.name.toLowerCase().replace(/(?: tail| wings| cape)/g, "") == a.toLowerCase())?.id;
                let accExists = validID(isName ?? a, null);
                if (typeof accExists == "number" || a == "21") {
                    my.combatTail = a == "21" ? 21 : isName ?? accExists;
                    showSetting("CombatAccessory Set To " + my.combatTail + ".");
                } else showSetting('"' + a.slice(0, 8) + '" is not a valid id.');
            } else if (
                message == ".t -ast" ||
                message == ".t -antispiketick"
            ) {
                configs.antiZpyklerTick = !configs.antiZpyklerTick;
                showSetting(`AntiSpikeTick ${configs.antiZpyklerTick ? "Enabled." : "Disabled."}`);
            } else if (
                message == ".t -bs" ||
                message == ".t -abs" ||
                message == ".t -bullspam" ||
                message == ".t -autobullspam"
            ) {
                configs.bullspam = !configs.bullspam;
                showSetting(`AutoBullSpam ${configs.bullspam ? "Enabled." : "Disabled."}`);
            } else if (message.startsWith(".s -ppd -")) {
                let a = message.replace(".s -ppd -", "");
                configs.preplacerDelay = Number(a);
                getEl("preplacerDelay-text").value = a;
                showSetting(`PreplaceDelay Set To ${a}`);
            } else if (message == ".s -wxp") {
                if (player.secondaryIndex != undefined) showSetting(`Primary: ${player.primaryXP} | Secondary: ${player.secondaryXP}`);
                else showSetting(`Primary: ` + player.primaryXP);
            } else if (message == ".e -p" || message == ".e -placers" || message == ".a -p" || message == ".a -placers") {
                configs.autoReplace = true;
                configs.autoPreplace = true;
                configs.autoPlace = true;
                showSetting(`All Placers Enabled.`);
            } else if (message == ".d -p" || message == ".d -placers") {
                configs.autoReplace = false;
                configs.autoPreplace = false;
                configs.autoPlace = false;
                showSetting(`All Placers Disabled.`);
            } else if (
                message == ".t -evil" ||
                message == ".t -1v1" ||
                message == ".t -wall"
            ) {
                evil = !evil;
                showSetting(`Walls ${evil ? "Disabled." : "Enabled."}`);
            } else if (message == ".t -hl" || message == ".t -hatloop" || message == ".t -hat") {
                hatLoop = !hatLoop;
                showSetting(`Hatloop ${hatLoop ? "Enabled." : "Disabled."}`);
            } else if (message == ".t -ait") {
                configs.antiInstaTricker = !configs.antiInstaTricker;
                showSetting(`Anti Insta Tricker ${configs.antiInstaTricker ? "Enabled." : "Disabled."}`);
            } else if (message == ".t -ap" || message == ".t autoplace") {
                configs.autoPlace = !configs.autoPlace;
                showSetting(`Autoplace ${configs.autoPlace ? "Enabled." : "Disabled."}`);
            } else if (!data[1]) {
                dontSend = true;
                packet("6", data[0], 1, "chat");
            }
            // ANTI PROFANITY:
            //else {
                /*


                let profanity = [
                    "cunt",
                    "mohmoh",

                "fucking", "Moooing"*/
                if (isDevServer) data[0] = data[0].replaceAll("retard", "retqrd").replaceAll(/molest(?!ed)/g, "mqlest").replaceAll(/(?<!ape)tard/g, "tqrd").replaceAll("damn", "dqmn").replaceAll("discord", "d1scord").replaceAll("josh", "j0sh").replaceAll("fuck", "fqck").replaceAll("shit", "s**t").replaceAll("romance", "romqnce");
                else {
                    // ANTI PROFANITY:
                    let profanity = [
                        "cunt",
                        "whore",
                        "fuck",
                        "shit",
                        "dick",
                        "vagina",
                        "minge",
                        "cock",
                        "rape",
                        "cum",

                        "bastard",
                        "mohmoh"
                    ]
                let tmpString;
                for (let i = 0; i < profanity.length; i++) {
                    let profany = profanity[i];
                    if (data[0].indexOf(profany) > -1) {
                        tmpString = "";
                        for (let i = 0; i < profany.length; ++i) {
                            if (i == 1) {
                                tmpString += String.fromCharCode(0);
                            }
                            tmpString += profany[i];
                        }
                        let re = new RegExp(profany, "g");
                        data[0] = data[0].replace(re, tmpString);
                    }
                }
                // FIX CHAT:
                data[0] = data[0].slice(0, 30);
                }
            //}
        }
    } else if (type == "L") {
        // MAKE SAME CLAN:
        data[0] = data[0] + "";
        data[0] = data[0].slice(0, 7);
    } else if (type == "M") {
        // APPLY CYAN COLOR:
        data[0].moofoll = true;
        data[0].skin = data[0].skin == 10 ? "__proto__" : data[0].skin;
        lastsp = [data[0].name, data[0].moofoll, data[0].skin];
    } else if (type == "D") {
        if (data[1] != 1 || my.noAim) dontSend = true;
        if (my.lastDir == data[0] || [null, undefined].includes(data[0])) dontSend = true;
        else my.lastDir = data[0];
    } else if (type == "F") {
        if (!data[2]) dontSend = true;
        else {
            if (data[0] == 1) hitting = true;
            else if (data[0] == 0) hitting = false;
            if (![null, undefined].includes(data[1])) my.lastDir = data[1];
        }
    } else if (type == "K") {
        if (!data[1]) dontSend = true;
        else autoGathering = !autoGathering;
    } else if (type == "9") {
        moveAuto = data[2];
        if (clientMoveDir == data[0] || !data[1] || !data[2] && (stopMoving || !data[1] ||clientMoveDir == data[0])) dontSend = true;
        else clientMoveDir = data[0];
    } else if (type == "0") return
    if (!dontSend) {
        packets.push([
            performance.now(),
            type,
            data,
            msgpack.encode([c2s[type], data]),
            packetCaller,
        ]);
        for (let i = 0; i < packets.length; i++) {
            if (performance.now() - packets[i][0] > 10000) packets.splice(i, 1);
        }
        newUpdateBullshit(type, data);
        // START COUNT:
        if (!firstSend.sec) {
            firstSend.sec = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
                packetStartTime = performance.now();
                firstSend.sec = false;
                secPacket = 0;
            }, secTime);
        }
        if (secPacket == 100) addMenuChText(null, "Warning: Sending Too Many Packets", "#cc5151", 1);
        secPacket++;
        secPacketRatio = secPacket / (performance.now() - packetStartTime);
    }
};

globalThis.sendWS3 = function (message) {
    dontSend = false;
    let data = message;
    console.log(message)
    if (true || player.sid == realPlayerSid) data = new Uint8Array(message);
    let parsed = msgpack.decode(data);
    console.log(parsed)
    let type = parsed[0];
    data = parsed[1];
    // SEND MESSAGE:
    if (type == "c") dontSend = data[0] && (data[2] ? player.tails[data[1]] : player.skins[data[1]])
    else if (type == "z") {
        if (noZ) dontSend = true;
        if (data[1] && hold[0] == data[0] && hold[1]) dontSend = true;
        else hold = data;
    } else if (type == "6") {
        console.log(data)
        if (data[0]) {
            let message = data[0];
            if (!message || !message.length) dontSend = true;
            dontSend = (!saySettings && message.startsWith(".") && message != "." && message != "...") || message.includes("a .el ") ||
                message.includes("r .el ") ||
                message.includes("a .fl ") ||
                message.includes("r .fl ") ||
                message == ".d";
            const showSetting = function(msg) {
                if (dontSend) {
                    player.chat.message = msg;
                    player.chat.count = 2000;
                } else {
                    game.tickBase(() => {
                        packet("6", msg, 1, "settings");
                    }, 6);
                }
            }
            if (message == ".t -ad") {
                configs.autoDiddler = !configs.autoDiddler;
                showSetting("Auto Diddler " + (configs.autoDiddler ? "Enabled." : "Disabled."));
            } else if (message == ".t -heal" || message == ".t -ah") {
                autoHeal = !autoHeal;
                showSetting("Auto Heal " + (autoHeal ? "Enabled." : "Disabled."));
            } else if (message == ".c -t -aa" && player.isOwner) {
                autoAcceptEnabled = !autoAcceptEnabled;
                showSetting(`Auto Accept ${autoAcceptEnabled ? "Enabled." : "Disabled."}`);
                if (notifications.length > 0 && autoAcceptEnabled) {
                    for (let i = 0; i < notifications.length; i++) {
                        let sid = notifications[i].sid;
                        let name = notifications[i].name;
                        setTimeout(() => {
                            lastAccept = performance.now();
                            packet("P", sid, 1, "autoaccept")
                            showSetting("Accepted " + name);
                            notifications.splice(i, 1);
                            renderJoinRequests();
                        }, i * 1500);
                    }
                }
            } else if (player.isOwner && message == ".c -d") {
                showSetting("Deleted " + player.team + (player.team.endsWith(".") ? "" : "."));
                packet("N", "leave");
            } else if (message.startsWith(".c -j -")) {
                let clan = undefined;
                for (let i = 0; i < alliances.length; i++) {
                    if (alliances[i].sid.replaceAll(String.fromCharCode(0), "").replaceAll("", "") == (message.replace(".c -j -", "").trim())) {
                        clan = alliances[i].sid;
                        packet("b", clan, "join");
                    }
                }
                showSetting("Joining " + clan + (clan.endsWith(".") ? "" : "."));
            } else if (message.startsWith(".c -c -")) {
                if (player.isOwner) packet("N");
                const clan = message.replace(".c -c -", "");
                if (player.isOwner) packet("L", clan, "createclan");
                showSetting('Created ' + clan);
            } else if (message.startsWith(".c -a -")) {
                dontSend = true;
                if (player.isOwner) {
                    const sid = Number(message.replace(".c -a -", ""));
                    packet("P", sid, 1, "accept player");
                    const name = notifications.find2((e) => e.sid == sid)?.name;
                    if (name) showSetting(name + " has been accepted.");
                removeNotificationBySid(sid);
                renderJoinRequests();
                }
            } else if (message.startsWith(".c -k -")) {
                if (player.isOwner) {
                    const sid = Number(message.replace(".c -k -", ""));
                    packet("Q", sid, "kick");
                    showSetting(alliancePlayers.find2((e) => e.sid === sid) + " has been kicked.");
                }
            } else if (message.startsWith(".c -l")) {
                if (!player.isOwner) {
                    let teamName = player.team;
                    showSetting("Left " + teamName + (teamName.endsWith(".") ? "" : "."));
                    packet("N", "leave");
                }
            } else if (message.startsWith(".mute -")) {
                const a = validID(message.replace(".mute -", ""), null);
                if (typeof a === "number") {
                    if (a === player.sid) {
                        showSetting("bros trying to mute himself 😭");
                    } else if (!mutedSIDs.includes(a)) {
                        mutedSIDs.push(a);
                    }
                } else {
                    showSetting('"' + message.replace(".mute -", "") + '" is not a number.');
                }
            } else if (message.startsWith(".unmute -")) {
                const a = validID(message.replace(".unmute -", ""), null);
                if (typeof a === "number") {
                    if (mutedSIDs.includes(a)) {
                        mutedSIDs.splice(mutedSIDs.indexOf(a), 1);
                    }
                } else {
                    showSetting('"' + message.replace(".unmute -", "") + '" is not a number.');
                }
            } else if (message == ".mute") {
                showSetting(mutedSIDs.join());
            } else if (message == ".t -ab") {
                configs.autoBreaker = !configs.autoBreaker;
                showSetting(`Autobreaker ${configs.autoBreaker ? "Enabled." : "Disabled."}`);
            } else if (message == ".el") showSetting(annoyingList.join());
            else if (message.startsWith(".el -r -")) {
                const a = validID(message.replace(".el -r -", ""), null);
                if (typeof a === "number" && annoyingList.includes(a)) {
                    annoyingList.splice(annoyingList.indexOf(a), 1);
                    showSetting(a + " removed from Enemylist.");
                } else showSetting(typeof a !== "number" ? ('"' + message.replace(".el -r -", "") + '" is not a number.') : (message.replace(".el -r -", "") + " is not in your Enemylist."));
            } else if (message.startsWith(".tpl -r -")) {
                const a = validID(message.replace(".tpl -r -", ""), null);
                if (typeof a === "number" && tpList.includes(a)) {
                    tpList.splice(tpList.indexOf(a), 1);
                    const as = findPlayerBySID(a);
                    showSetting((as?.name?.length <= 11 ? as.name : a) + " removed from TP-List.");
                } else showSetting('"' + message.replace(".tpl -r -", "") + '"' + " is not a number.");
            } else if (message.startsWith(".tpl -a -")) {
                const a = Number(message.replace(".tpl -a -", ""));
                if (!Number.isNaN(a)) {
                    let as = findPlayerBySID(a);
                    tpList.push(a);
                    showSetting((as.name.length <= 12 ? as.name : a) + " added to TP List.");
                } else showSetting('"' + message.replace(".tpl -a -", "") + '"' + " is not a number.");
            } else if (message.startsWith(".tr -a -")) {
                const a = Number(message.replace(".tr -a -", ""));
                if (!Number.isNaN(a)) {
                    let as = findPlayerBySID(a);
                    turkList.push(a);
                    if (as) as.isTurk = true;
                    showSetting((as.name.length <= 12 ? as.name : a) + " added to Turk List.");
                } else showSetting('"' + message.replace(".tr -a -", "") + '"' + " is not a number.");
            } else if (message.startsWith(".tr -r -")) {
                const a = validID(message.replace(".tr -r -", ""), null);
                if (typeof a === "number" && turkList.includes(a)) {
                    turkList.splice(turkList.indexOf(a), 1);
                    const as = findPlayerBySID(a);
                    showSetting((as?.name?.length <= 11 ? as.name : a) + " removed from Turk List.");
                    if (as) as.isTurk = false;
                } else showSetting('"' + message.replace(".tr -r -", "") + '"' + " is not a number.");
            } else if (message == ".tr") showSetting(turkList.join());
            else if (message.startsWith(".el -a -n -")) {
                const a = message.replace(".el -a -n -", "");
                const ids = players.filter2((e) => e.name.includes(a)).map((e) => e.sid);
                annoyingList = [...new Set([...annoyingList, ...ids])];
                showSetting("Added " + ids.length + " players with '" + a + "' in their name to Enemy List.");
            } else if (message.startsWith(".el -a -")) {
                const a = Number(message.replace(".el -a -", ""));
                if (!Number.isNaN(a)) {
                    const as = findPlayerBySID(a);
                    if (friendList.includes(a)) friendList.splice(friendList.indexOf(a), 1);
                    annoyingList.push(a);
                    showSetting((as && as.name.length <= 10 ? as.name : a) + " added to Enemy List.");
                } else showSetting('"' + message.replace(".el -a -", "") + '"' + " is not a number.");
            } else if (message == (".el -clear")) {
                annoyingList = [];
                showSetting("Enemy List cleared.");
            } else if (message.startsWith(".bl -a -")) {
                const a = Number(message.replace(".bl -a -", ""));
                if (!Number.isNaN(a)) {
                    const as = findPlayerBySID(a);
                    if (friendList.includes(a)) friendList.splice(friendList.indexOf(a), 1);
                    botList.push(a);
                    showSetting((as && as.name.length <= 10 ? as.name : a) + " added to Botlist.");
                } else showSetting('"' + message.replace(".bl -a -", "") + '"' + " is not a number.");
            } else if (message.startsWith(".fl -a -n -")) {
                const a = message.replace(".fl -a -n -", "");
                const ids = players.filter2((e) => e.name.includes(a)).map((e) => e.sid);
                friendList = [...new Set([...friendList, ...ids])];
                showSetting("Added " + ids.length + " players with '" + a + "' in their name to Friend List.");
            } else if (message == ".fl -clear") {
                friendList = [];
                showSetting("Friend List cleared.");
            } else if (message.startsWith(".bl -r -")) {
                const a = validID(message.replace(".bl -r -", ""), null);
                if (typeof a === "number" && botList.includes(a)) {
                    botList.splice(botList.indexOf(a), 1);
                    showSetting(a + " removed from Botlist.");
                } else showSetting(typeof a !== "number" ? ('"' + message.replace(".bl -r -", "") + '" is not a number.') : (message.replace(".bl -r -", "") + " is not in your Botlist."));
            } else if (message == ".bl") showSetting(botList.join());
                else if (message == ".tpl") showSetting(tpList.join());
                else if (message == ".fl") showSetting(friendList.join());
                else if (message.startsWith(".fl -r -")) {
                const a = validID(message.replace(".fl -r -", ""), null);
                if (typeof a === "number" && friendList.includes(a)) {
                    friendList.splice(friendList.indexOf(a), 1);
                    showSetting(a + " removed from Friendlist.");
                } else showSetting(typeof a !== "number" ? ('"' + message.replace(".fl -r -", "") + '" is not a number.') : (message.replace(".fl -r -", "") + " is not in your Friendlist."));
            } else if (message.startsWith(".fl -a -")) {
                let a = Number(message.replace(".fl -a -", ""));
                if (!Number.isNaN(a) && Number.isInteger(a) && a > 0 && a <= 50) {
                    let as = findPlayerBySID(a);
                    friendList.push(a);
                    showSetting((as && as.name.length <= 9 ? as.name : a) + " Added To Friendlist.");
                } else showSetting('"' + message.replace(".fl -a -", "") + '" is not a valid number.');
            } else if (message == ".d") {
                saySettings = !saySettings;
                player.chat.message = "Silent mode " + (saySettings ? "Disabled" : "Enabled");
                player.chat.count = 1500;
            } else if (message == ".r -dh") {
                my.defaultHat = "auto";
                showSetting("DefaultHat reset.");
            } else if (message == ".r -ch") {
                my.combatHat = 6;
                showSetting("CombatHat reset.");
            } else if (message == ".r -da") {
                my.defaultAccessory = 11;
                showSetting("DefaultAccessory reset.");
            } else if (message == ".r -ca") {
                my.combatTail = 19;
                showSetting("CombatAccessory reset.");
            } else if (message.startsWith(".dc -")) {
                const SID = Number(message.replace(".dc -", ""));
                const bot = bots.find2((e) => e.sid == SID);
                if (!bot) showSetting("Could not find bot " + message.replace(".dc -", ""));
                else {
                    bot.disconnectBot();
                    showSetting("Disconnected bot " + SID);
                }
            } else if (message.startsWith(".protect -")) {
                // usage: .protect -<sid> [radius]  or  .protect -off
                const raw = message.replace(".protect -", "").trim();
                if (raw.toLowerCase() === "off") {
                    protectMode = false;
                    protectTargetSID = null;
                    botConfig.move = "follow";
                    showSetting("Protect mode disabled, bots now following.");
                } else {
                    const parts = raw.split(/\s+/);
                    const SID = Number(parts[0]);
                    const radius = parts[1] ? Number(parts[1]) : protectRadius;
                    if (Number.isNaN(SID)) showSetting('"' + parts[0] + '" is not a valid sid.');
                    else {
                        const target = findPlayerBySID(SID);
                        if (!target) showSetting("Could not find player " + SID);
                        else {
                            protectMode = true;
                            protectTargetSID = SID;
                            if (!Number.isNaN(radius) && radius > 0) protectRadius = radius;
                            showSetting("Protect mode enabled for " + target.name + " [" + SID + "] (r=" + protectRadius + ")");
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else if (message == ".c") pendingBotCount++, addBot(botName.value);
                else if (message.startsWith(".c -")) pendingBotCount++, addBot(message.replace(".c -", ""));
                else if (message.startsWith(".playas -")) {
                const sid = Number(message.replace(".playas -", ""));
                const findBot = bots.find2((e) => e.sid == sid);
                if (findBot) {
                    findBot.chat.message = "Playing as bot " + findBot.sid;
                    findBot.chat.count = 2000;
                    const cached = findBot;
                    player.fS = false;
                    player.sendPacket = (packet, data) => {
                        if (!player.fS) {
                            player.fS = true;
                            setTimeout(()=>{
                                player.fS = false;
                                player.packets = 0;
                            }, 1000);
                        }
                        player.packets++;
                        if (player.packets>=85) return;
                        if (packet == "9") player.clientDir = data[0];
                        if (packet == "K") player.autoGathering = !player.autoGathering;
                        sendWS(msgpack.encode([c2s[packet], data]));
                    }
                    player.heal = function(e) {
                        for (let x = e; x < 100; x += 40) {
                            player.sendPacket("z", [(player.age > 1) * 1, null]);
                            player.sendPacket("F", [1, 2.3]);
                            player.sendPacket("z", [player.primaryIndex, 1]);
                        }
                    }
                    player.place = function (id, angle, wep = player.weaponIndex) {
                        player.sendPacket("z", [id, null]);
                        player.sendPacket("F", [1, angle]);
                        player.sendPacket("z", [wep, true]);
                    }
                    botSIDs[botSIDs.findIndex((e) => e == sid)] = playerSID;
                    bots[bots.findIndex((e) => e.sid == sid)] = player;
                    player = cached;
                    playerSID = cached.sid;
                    packet = function(type) {
                        if (!globalThis.hacking) return;
                        let data = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments, 1);
                        packetCaller = data[data.length - 1];
                        if (type != '0' && (type != "9" || !noMove || packetCaller == "antionetick" || packetCaller == "onetick")) player.sendPacket(type, data);
                    }
                } else {
                    player.chat.message = "Could not find bot " + sid;
                    player.chat.count = 2000;
                }
            } else if (message == ".return") {
                const cached = player;
                cached.fS = false;
                cached.sendPacket = (packet, data) => {
                    if (!cached.fS) {
                        cached.fS = true;
                        setTimeout(()=>{
                            cached.fS = false;
                            cached.packets = 0;
                        }, 1000);
                    }
                    cached.packets++;
                    if (cached.packets>=85) return;
                    if (packet == "9") cached.clientDir = data[0];
                    if (packet == "K") cached.autoGathering = !player.autoGathering;
                    cached.send(msgpack.encode([c2s[packet], data]));
                }
                player = bots[bots.findIndex((e) => e.sid == realPlayerSid)];
                bots[bots.findIndex((e) => e.sid == realPlayerSid)] = cached;
                botSIDs[botSIDs.findIndex((e) => e == realPlayerSid)] = playerSID;
                playerSID = realPlayerSid;
                packet = function(type) {
                    if (!globalThis.hacking) return;
                    let data = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments, 1);
                    packetCaller = data[data.length - 1];
                    if (type != '0' && (type != "9" || !noMove || packetCaller == "antionetick" || packetCaller == "onetick")) sendWS(msgpack.encode([c2s[type], data]));
                }
            } else if (message == ".disconnect") discBots();
            else if (message == ".farm -wood") {
                botConfig.move = "farm";
                setForceFarmType(0);
            } else if (message == ".farm -stone") {
                botConfig.move = "farm";
                setForceFarmType(2);
            } else if (message == ".farm -food") {
                botConfig.move = "farm";
                setForceFarmType(1);
            } else if (message == ".farm -gold") {
                botConfig.move = "farm";
                setForceFarmType(3);
            } else if (message == ".age") {
                const time = 1000 + botAmt * 300;
                for (let i = 0;i < bots.length;i++) {
                    const bot = bots[i];
                    bot.chat.message = bot.age;
                    bot.chat.count = time;
                }
            } else if (message == ".spy") {
                doSpy = true;
            } else if (message == ".wood") {
                const time = 1000 + botAmt * 300;
                for (let i = 0;i < bots.length;i++) {
                    const bot = bots[i];
                    bot.chat.message = bot.wood;
                    bot.chat.count = time;
                }
            } else if (message == ".stone") {
                const time = 1000 + botAmt * 300;
                for (let i = 0;i < bots.length;i++) {
                    const bot = bots[i];
                    bot.chat.message = bot.stone;
                    bot.chat.count = time;
                }
            } else if (message == ".food") {
                const time = 1000 + botAmt * 300;
                for (let i = 0;i < bots.length;i++) {
                    const bot = bots[i];
                    bot.chat.message = bot.food;
                    bot.chat.count = time;
                }
            } else if (message == ".gold") {
                const time = 1000 + botAmt * 300;
                for (let i = 0;i < bots.length;i++) {
                    const bot = bots[i];
                    bot.chat.message = bot.points;
                    bot.chat.count = time;
                }
            } else if (message == ".cursor") botConfig.move = "mouse";
            else if (message == ".follow") botConfig.move = "follow";
            else if (message == ".scatter") botConfig.move = "scatter";
            else if (message == ".farm") {
                setForceFarmType(null);
                botConfig.move = "farm";
            } else if (message == ".stop") botConfig.move = "stop";
            else if (message.startsWith(".s -dh -")) {
                let a = message.replace(".s -dh -", "");
                let isName = hats.find2((e) => e.name.toLowerCase().replaceAll(" ", "") == a.toLowerCase() || e.name.toLowerCase().replace(/(?: cap| armor| hat| gear| helmet| head)/g, "") == a.toLowerCase())?.id;
                let hatExists = validID(isName ?? a, null);
                if (typeof hatExists == "number") {
                    my.defaultHat = isName ?? hatExists;
                    showSetting("DefaultHat set to " + my.defaultHat);
                } else showSetting('"' + String(a).slice(0, 8) + '" is not a valid id.');
            } else if (message.startsWith(".s -ch -")) {
                const a = message.replace(".s -ch -", "");
                const isName = hats.find2((e) => e.name.toLowerCase().replace(" ", "") == a.toLowerCase() || e.name.toLowerCase().replace(/(?: cap| armor| hat| gear| helmet| head)/g, "") == a.toLowerCase())?.id;
                let hatExists = validID(isName ?? a, null);
                if (typeof hatExists == "number") {
                    my.combatHat = isName ?? hatExists;
                    showSetting("CombatHat set to " + my.combatHat + ".");
                } else showSetting('"' + String(a).slice(0, 8) + '" is not a valid id.');
            } else if (message.startsWith(".s -da -")) {
                let a = message.replace(".s -da -", "");
                let isName = accessories.find2((e) => e.name.toLowerCase().replace(" ", "") == a.toLowerCase() || e.name.toLowerCase().replace(/(?: tail| wings| cape)/g, "") == a.toLowerCase())?.id;
                let accExists = validID(isName ?? a, null);
                if (typeof accExists == "number" || a == "21") {
                    my.defaultTail = a == "21" ? 21 : isName ?? accExists;
                    showSetting("DefaultAccessory set to " + my.defaultTail + ".");
                } else showSetting('"' + String(a).slice(0, 8) + '" is not a valid id.', "settings");
            } else if (message.startsWith(".s -ca -")) {
                let a = message.replace(".s -ca -", "");
                let isName = accessories.find2((e) => e.name.toLowerCase().replace(" ", "") == a.toLowerCase() || e.name.toLowerCase().replace(/(?: tail| wings| cape)/g, "") == a.toLowerCase())?.id;
                let accExists = validID(isName ?? a, null);
                if (typeof accExists == "number" || a == "21") {
                    my.combatTail = a == "21" ? 21 : isName ?? accExists;
                    showSetting("CombatAccessory Set To " + my.combatTail + ".");
                } else showSetting('"' + a.slice(0, 8) + '" is not a valid id.');
            } else if (
                message == ".t -ast" ||
                message == ".t -antispiketick"
            ) {
                configs.antiZpyklerTick = !configs.antiZpyklerTick;
                showSetting(`AntiSpikeTick ${configs.antiZpyklerTick ? "Enabled." : "Disabled."}`);
            } else if (
                message == ".t -bs" ||
                message == ".t -abs" ||
                message == ".t -bullspam" ||
                message == ".t -autobullspam"
            ) {
                configs.bullspam = !configs.bullspam;
                showSetting(`AutoBullSpam ${configs.bullspam ? "Enabled." : "Disabled."}`);
            } else if (message.startsWith(".s -ppd -")) {
                let a = message.replace(".s -ppd -", "");
                configs.preplacerDelay = Number(a);
                getEl("preplacerDelay-text").value = a;
                showSetting(`PreplaceDelay Set To ${a}`);
            } else if (message == ".s -wxp") {
                if (player.secondaryIndex != undefined) showSetting(`Primary: ${player.primaryXP} | Secondary: ${player.secondaryXP}`);
                else showSetting(`Primary: ` + player.primaryXP);
            } else if (message == ".e -p" || message == ".e -placers" || message == ".a -p" || message == ".a -placers") {
                configs.autoReplace = true;
                configs.autoPreplace = true;
                configs.autoPlace = true;
                showSetting(`All Placers Enabled.`);
            } else if (message == ".d -p" || message == ".d -placers") {
                configs.autoReplace = false;
                configs.autoPreplace = false;
                configs.autoPlace = false;
                showSetting(`All Placers Disabled.`);
            } else if (
                message == ".t -evil" ||
                message == ".t -1v1" ||
                message == ".t -wall"
            ) {
                evil = !evil;
                showSetting(`Walls ${evil ? "Disabled." : "Enabled."}`);
            } else if (message == ".t -hl" || message == ".t -hatloop" || message == ".t -hat") {
                hatLoop = !hatLoop;
                showSetting(`Hatloop ${hatLoop ? "Enabled." : "Disabled."}`);
            } else if (message == ".t -ait") {
                configs.antiInstaTricker = !configs.antiInstaTricker;
                showSetting(`Anti Insta Tricker ${configs.antiInstaTricker ? "Enabled." : "Disabled."}`);
            } else if (message == ".t -ap" || message == ".t autoplace") {
                configs.autoPlace = !configs.autoPlace;
                showSetting(`Autoplace ${configs.autoPlace ? "Enabled." : "Disabled."}`);
            } else if (!data[1]) {
                dontSend = true;
                packet("6", data[0], 1, "chat");
            }
            // ANTI PROFANITY:
            //else {
                let profanity = [
                    "cunt",
                    "mohmoh",
                    "whore",
                    "fuck",
                    "shit",
                    "dick",
                    "vagina",
                    "minge",
                    "cock",
                    "rape",
                    "cum",
                    "sex",
                    "tits",
                    "penis",
                    "clit",
                    "pussy",
                    "meatcurtain",
                    "jizz",
                    "prune",
                    "douche",
                    "wanker",
                    "damn",
                    "bitch",
                    "dick",
                    "bastard",
                ];
                let tmpString;
                for (let i = 0; i < profanity.length; i++) {
                    let profany = profanity[i];
                    if (data[0].indexOf(profany) == -1) continue;
                    tmpString = "";
                    for (let i = 0; i < profany.length; ++i) {
                        if (i == 1) tmpString += "";
                        tmpString += profany[i];
                    }
                    let re = new RegExp(profany, "g");
                    data[0] = data[0].replace(re, tmpString);
                }
                // FIX CHAT:
                data[0] = data[0].slice(0, 30);
            //}
        }
    } else if (type == "L") {
        // MAKE SAME CLAN:
        data[0] = data[0] + "";
        data[0] = data[0].slice(0, 7);
    } else if (type == "M") {
        // APPLY CYAN COLOR:
        data[0].moofoll = true;
        data[0].skin = data[0].skin == 10 ? "__proto__" : data[0].skin;
        lastsp = [data[0].name, data[0].moofoll, data[0].skin];
    } else if (type == "D") {
        if (data[1] != 1 || my.noAim) dontSend = true;
        if (my.lastDir == data[0] || [null, undefined].includes(data[0])) dontSend = true;
        else my.lastDir = data[0];
    } else if (type == "F") {
        if (!data[2]) dontSend = true;
        else {
            if (data[0] == 1) hitting = true;
            else if (data[0] == 0) hitting = false;
            if (![null, undefined].includes(data[1])) my.lastDir = data[1];
        }
    } else if (type == "K") {
        if (!data[1]) dontSend = true;
        else autoGathering = !autoGathering;
    } else if (type == "9") {
        moveAuto = data[2];
        if (clientMoveDir == data[0] || !data[1] || !data[2] && (stopMoving || !data[1] ||clientMoveDir == data[0])) dontSend = true;
        else clientMoveDir = data[0];
    } else if (type == "0") return
    if (!dontSend) {
        packets.push([
            performance.now(),
            type,
            data,
            msgpack.encode([c2s[type], data]),
            packetCaller,
        ]);
        for (let i = 0; i < packets.length; i++) {
            if (performance.now() - packets[i][0] > 10000) packets.splice(i, 1);
        }
        let binary = msgpack.encode([c2s[type], data]);
        player.sendPacket(binary);
        // START COUNT:
        if (!firstSend.sec) {
            firstSend.sec = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
                packetStartTime = performance.now();
                firstSend.sec = false;
                secPacket = 0;
            }, secTime);
        }
        if (secPacket == 100) addMenuChText(null, "Warning: Sending Too Many Packets", "#cc5151", 1);
        secPacket++;
        secPacketRatio = secPacket / (performance.now() - packetStartTime);
    }
};
globalThis.sendWS2 = function (message) {
    dontSend = false;
    // EXTRACT DATA ARRAY:
    let data = new Uint8Array(message);
    let parsed = msgpack.decode(data);
    let type = parsed[0];
    data = parsed[1];
    // SEND MESSAGE:
    if (type == "L") {
        // MAKE SAME CLAN:
        data[0] = data[0] + String.fromCharCode(0).repeat(1);
        data[0] = data[0].slice(0, 7);
    } else if (type == "M") {
        // APPLY CYAN COLOR:
        data[0].moofoll = true;
        data[0].skin = data[0].skin == 10 ? "__proto__" : data[0].skin;
        lastsp = [data[0].name, data[0].moofoll, data[0].skin];
    } else if (type == "D") {
        if (data[1] != 1 || my.lastDir == data[0] || [null, undefined].includes(data[0])) dontSend = true;
        else my.lastDir = data[0];
    } else if (type == "F") {
        if (!data[2]) dontSend = true;
    } else if (type == "0" && !data[0]) return;
    if (!dontSend) {
        packets.push([
            performance.now(),
            [type, data],
            msgpack.encode([c2s[type], data]),
            packetCaller,
        ]);
        for (let i = 0; i < packets.length; i++) {
            if (performance.now() - packets[i][0] > 10000) packets.splice(i, 1);
        }
        newUpdateBullshit(type, data);
        // START COUNT:
        if (!firstSend.sec) {
            firstSend.sec = true;
            setTimeout(() => {
                firstSend.sec = false;
                secPacket = 0;
            }, secTime);
        }
        if (secPacket == 100) addMenuChText(null, "Warning: Sending Too Many Packets", "#cc5151", 1);
        secPacket++;
    }
};

        disconnect("disconnected");
    });

    WS.onmessage = getMessage;
    sendWS = sendWS1;
    WS.onopen = (() => {

        window.FRVR && window.FRVR.tracker.levelStart("game_start");
        window.onbeforeunload = function(e) {
            localStorage.setItem("remedyConfig", JSON.stringify(configs));

        }
    });
}

}

globalThis.packet = function(type) {

    if (type != '0' && (type != "9" || !noMove || packetCaller == "antionetick" || packetCaller == "onetick")) {
        // SEND MESSAGE:
        sendWS(msgpack.encode([type, data]));
    }
}

globalThis.normalizeAllianceName = function(value) {
    return String(value || "")
        .replaceAll(String.fromCharCode(0), "")
        .replaceAll("", "")
        .trim();
}
globalThis.resolveAllianceLeader = function(clan) {
    const owner = clan?.owner || clan?.leader || clan?.ownerSid || clan?.ownerSID || clan?.ownerId || clan?.leaderId || clan?.leaderSID;
    const sid = owner != null
        ? typeof owner === "object"
            ? owner.sid ?? owner.id ?? owner.SID ?? owner.ID ?? owner.name ?? "unknown"
            : String(owner)
        : "unknown";
    const player = sid !== "unknown" ? findPlayerBySID(sid) : null;
    if (owner != null) {
        return {
            name: typeof owner === "object"
                ? owner.name || player?.name || player?.displayText || sid
                : player?.name || player?.displayText || sid,
            sid,
        };
    }
    const teamName = typeof clan === "string" ? clan : clan?.sid;
    const leaderPlayer = teamName != null
        ? (players.find2 ? players.find2((p) => p.team === teamName && p.isLeader) : players.find((p) => p.team === teamName && p.isLeader))
        : null;
    return {
        name: leaderPlayer?.name || leaderPlayer?.displayText || String(leaderPlayer?.sid) || "unknown",
        sid: leaderPlayer?.sid ?? "unknown",
    };
};
(function() {
    const pow32 = 0x100000000;
    const textEncoder = typeof TextEncoder !== "undefined" ? new TextEncoder() : null;
    const textDecoder = typeof TextDecoder !== "undefined" ? new TextDecoder("utf-8") : null;

    function serialize(data) {
        let buffer = new Uint8Array(256);
        let view = new DataView(buffer.buffer);
        let pos = 0;
        const float = new ArrayBuffer(8);
        const floatView = new DataView(float);
        const floatBytes = new Uint8Array(float);

        write(data);
        return buffer.subarray(0, pos);

        function reserve(size) {
            const needed = pos + size;
            if (needed <= buffer.length) return;
            let next = buffer.length << 1;
            while (next < needed) next <<= 1;
            const grown = new Uint8Array(next);
            grown.set(buffer);
            buffer = grown;
            view = new DataView(buffer.buffer);
        }

        function u8(value) {
            reserve(1);
            buffer[pos++] = value & 255;
        }

        function u16(value) {
            reserve(2);
            buffer[pos++] = (value >>> 8) & 255;
            buffer[pos++] = value & 255;
        }

        function u32(value) {
            reserve(4);
            buffer[pos++] = (value >>> 24) & 255;
            buffer[pos++] = (value >>> 16) & 255;
            buffer[pos++] = (value >>> 8) & 255;
            buffer[pos++] = value & 255;
        }

        function bytes(value) {
            reserve(value.length);
            buffer.set(value, pos);
            pos += value.length;
        }

        function i64(value) {
            let hi;
            let lo;
            if (value >= 0) {
                hi = value / pow32;
                lo = value % pow32;
            } else {
                value++;
                hi = Math.abs(value) / pow32;
                lo = Math.abs(value) % pow32;
                hi = ~hi;
                lo = ~lo;
            }
            u32(hi);
            u32(lo);
        }

        function write(value) {
            switch (typeof value) {
                case "undefined":
                    u8(0xc0);
                    return;
                case "boolean":
                    u8(value ? 0xc3 : 0xc2);
                    return;
                case "number":
                    writeNumber(value);
                    return;
                case "string":
                    writeString(value);
                    return;
                case "object":
                    if (value === null) u8(0xc0);
                    else if (value instanceof Date) writeDate(value);
                    else if (Array.isArray(value)) writeArray(value);
                    else if (value instanceof Uint8Array || value instanceof Uint8ClampedArray) writeBin(value);
                    else if (value instanceof Int8Array || value instanceof Int16Array || value instanceof Uint16Array ||
                            value instanceof Int32Array || value instanceof Uint32Array ||
                            value instanceof Float32Array || value instanceof Float64Array) writeArray(value);
                    else writeObject(value);
                    return;
            }
        }

        function writeNumber(value) {
            if (Number.isFinite(value) && Math.floor(value) === value) {
                if (value >= 0 && value <= 0x7f) {
                    u8(value);
                } else if (value < 0 && value >= -0x20) {
                    u8(value);
                } else if (value > 0 && value <= 0xff) {
                    reserve(2);
                    buffer[pos++] = 0xcc;
                    buffer[pos++] = value;
                } else if (value >= -0x80 && value <= 0x7f) {
                    reserve(2);
                    buffer[pos++] = 0xd0;
                    buffer[pos++] = value;
                } else if (value > 0 && value <= 0xffff) {
                    u8(0xcd);
                    u16(value);
                } else if (value >= -0x8000 && value <= 0x7fff) {
                    u8(0xd1);
                    u16(value);
                } else if (value > 0 && value <= 0xffffffff) {
                    u8(0xce);
                    u32(value);
                } else if (value >= -0x80000000 && value <= 0x7fffffff) {
                    u8(0xd2);
                    u32(value);
                } else if (value > 0 && value <= 0xffffffffffffffff) {
                    u8(0xd3);
                    u32(value / pow32);
                    u32(value % pow32);
                } else if (value >= -0x8000000000000000 && value <= 0x7fffffffffffffff) {
                    u8(0xd3);
                    i64(value);
                } else if (value < 0) {
                    reserve(9);
                    buffer[pos++] = 0xd3;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0x80;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0;
                } else {
                    reserve(9);
                    buffer[pos++] = 0xcf;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0xff;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0xff;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0xff;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0xff;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0xff;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0xff;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0xff;
                    buffer[pos++] = 0xff;
                }
                return;
            }
            floatView.setFloat64(0, value, false);
            u8(0xcb);
            bytes(floatBytes);
        }

        function writeString(value) {
            const strLen = value.length;
            let ascii = true;
            for (let i = 0; i < strLen; i++) {
                if (value.charCodeAt(i) > 127) {
                    ascii = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (ascii) {
                writeStringHeader(strLen);
                reserve(strLen);
                for (let i = 0; i < strLen; i++) buffer[pos++] = value.charCodeAt(i);
                return;
            }
            const encoded = encodeUtf8(value);
            writeStringHeader(encoded.length);
            bytes(encoded);
        }

        function writeStringHeader(len) {
            if (len <= 0x1f) u8(0xa0 + len);
            else if (len <= 0xff) {
                reserve(2);
                buffer[pos++] = 0xd9;
                buffer[pos++] = len;
            } else if (len <= 0xffff) {
                u8(0xda);
                u16(len);
            } else {
                u8(0xdb);
                u32(len);
            }
        }

        function writeArray(value) {
            const len = value.length;
            if (len <= 0xf) u8(0x90 + len);
            else if (len <= 0xffff) {
                u8(0xdc);
                u16(len);
            } else {
                u8(0xdd);
                u32(len);
            }
            for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) write(value[i]);
        }

        function writeBin(value) {
            const len = value.length;
            if (len <= 0xff) {
                reserve(2);
                buffer[pos++] = 0xc4;
                buffer[pos++] = len;
            } else if (len <= 0xffff) {
                u8(0xc5);
                u16(len);
            } else {
                u8(0xc6);
                u32(len);
            }
            bytes(value);
        }

        function writeObject(value) {
            const keys = Object.keys(value);
            const len = keys.length;
            if (len <= 0xf) u8(0x80 + len);
            else if (len <= 0xffff) {
                u8(0xde);
                u16(len);
            } else {
                u8(0xdf);
                u32(len);
            }
            for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
                const key = keys[i];
                writeString(key);
                write(value[key]);
            }
        }

        function writeDate(value) {
            const sec = value.getTime() / 1000;
            if (value.getMilliseconds() === 0 && sec >= 0 && sec < 0x100000000) {
                u8(0xd6);
                u8(0xff);
                u32(sec);
            } else if (sec >= 0 && sec < 0x400000000) {
                const ns = value.getMilliseconds() * 1000000;
                reserve(10);
                buffer[pos++] = 0xd7;
                buffer[pos++] = 0xff;
                buffer[pos++] = ns >>> 22;
                buffer[pos++] = ns >>> 14;
                buffer[pos++] = ns >>> 6;
                buffer[pos++] = ((ns << 2) >>> 0) | (sec / pow32);
                buffer[pos++] = sec >>> 24;
                buffer[pos++] = sec >>> 16;
                buffer[pos++] = sec >>> 8;
                buffer[pos++] = sec;
            } else {
                const ns = value.getMilliseconds() * 1000000;
                reserve(7);
                buffer[pos++] = 0xc7;
                buffer[pos++] = 12;
                buffer[pos++] = 0xff;
                buffer[pos++] = ns >>> 24;
                buffer[pos++] = ns >>> 16;
                buffer[pos++] = ns >>> 8;
                buffer[pos++] = ns;
                i64(sec);
            }
        }
    }

    function deserialize(input) {
        let array = input;
        if (array instanceof ArrayBuffer) array = new Uint8Array(array);
        if (typeof array !== "object" || typeof array.length === "undefined") {
            throw new Error("Invalid argument type: Expected a byte array (Array or Uint8Array) to deserialize.");
        }
        if (!array.length) throw new Error("Invalid argument: The byte array to deserialize is empty.");
        if (!(array instanceof Uint8Array)) array = new Uint8Array(array);

        const view = new DataView(array.buffer, array.byteOffset, array.byteLength);
        let pos = 0;
        return read();

        function u8() {
            return array[pos++];
        }

        function u16() {
            const value = (array[pos] << 8) | array[pos + 1];
            pos += 2;
            return value;
        }

        function u32() {
            const value = ((array[pos] << 24) >>> 0) + (array[pos + 1] << 16) + (array[pos + 2] << 8) + array[pos + 3];
            pos += 4;
            return value;
        }

        function uint(size) {
            if (size === 1) return u8();
            if (size === 2) return u16();
            if (size === 4) return u32();
            return u32() * pow32 + u32();
        }

        function int(size) {
            if (size === 1) {
                const value = u8();
                return value & 0x80 ? value - 0x100 : value;
            }
            if (size === 2) {
                const value = u16();
                return value & 0x8000 ? value - 0x10000 : value;
            }
            if (size === 4) return view.getInt32((pos += 4) - 4, false);
            const hi = view.getInt32(pos, false);
            const lo = view.getUint32(pos + 4, false);
            pos += 8;
            return hi * pow32 + lo;
        }

        function lengthFrom(size, lengthSize) {
            return size < 0 ? uint(lengthSize) : size;
        }

        function read() {
            const byte = u8();
            if (byte <= 0x7f) return byte;
            if (byte <= 0x8f) return map(byte - 0x80);
            if (byte <= 0x9f) return arr(byte - 0x90);
            if (byte <= 0xbf) return str(byte - 0xa0);
            switch (byte) {
                case 0xc0: return null;
                case 0xc1: throw new Error("Invalid byte code 0xc1 found.");
                case 0xc2: return false;
                case 0xc3: return true;
                case 0xc4: return bin(-1, 1);
                case 0xc5: return bin(-1, 2);
                case 0xc6: return bin(-1, 4);
                case 0xc7: return ext(-1, 1);
                case 0xc8: return ext(-1, 2);
                case 0xc9: return ext(-1, 4);
                case 0xca: return float(4);
                case 0xcb: return float(8);
                case 0xcc: return uint(1);
                case 0xcd: return uint(2);
                case 0xce: return uint(4);
                case 0xcf: return uint(8);
                case 0xd0: return int(1);
                case 0xd1: return int(2);
                case 0xd2: return int(4);
                case 0xd3: return int(8);
                case 0xd4: return ext(1);
                case 0xd5: return ext(2);
                case 0xd6: return ext(4);
                case 0xd7: return ext(8);
                case 0xd8: return ext(16);
                case 0xd9: return str(-1, 1);
                case 0xda: return str(-1, 2);
                case 0xdb: return str(-1, 4);
                case 0xdc: return arr(-1, 2);
                case 0xdd: return arr(-1, 4);
                case 0xde: return map(-1, 2);
                case 0xdf: return map(-1, 4);
            }
            if (byte >= 0xe0) return byte - 256;
            throw new Error("Invalid byte value '" + byte + "' at index " + (pos - 1) + ".");
        }

        function float(size) {
            const value = size === 4 ? view.getFloat32(pos, false) : view.getFloat64(pos, false);
            pos += size;
            return value;
        }

        function bin(size, lengthSize) {
            size = lengthFrom(size, lengthSize);
            const value = array.subarray(pos, pos + size);
            pos += size;
            return value;
        }

        function str(size, lengthSize) {
            size = lengthFrom(size, lengthSize);
            const start = pos;
            pos += size;
            return decodeUtf8(array, start, size);
        }

        function arr(size, lengthSize) {
            size = lengthFrom(size, lengthSize);
            const value = new Array(size);
            for (let i = 0; i < size; i++) value[i] = read();
            return value;
        }

        function map(size, lengthSize) {
            size = lengthFrom(size, lengthSize);
            const value = {};
            for (let i = 0; i < size; i++) value[read()] = read();
            return value;
        }

        function ext(size, lengthSize) {
            size = lengthFrom(size, lengthSize);
            const type = uint(1);
            const data = bin(size);
            return type === 255 ? dateExt(data) : { type: type, data: data };
        }

        function dateExt(data) {
            if (data.length === 4) {
                return new Date((((data[0] << 24) >>> 0) + (data[1] << 16) + (data[2] << 8) + data[3]) * 1000);
            }
            if (data.length === 8) {
                const ns = ((data[0] << 22) >>> 0) + ((data[1] << 14) >>> 0) + ((data[2] << 6) >>> 0) + (data[3] >>> 2);
                const sec = ((data[3] & 3) * pow32) + ((data[4] << 24) >>> 0) + (data[5] << 16) + (data[6] << 8) + data[7];
                return new Date(sec * 1000 + ns / 1000000);
            }
            if (data.length === 12) {
                const ns = ((data[0] << 24) >>> 0) + (data[1] << 16) + (data[2] << 8) + data[3];
                const sec = new DataView(data.buffer, data.byteOffset + 4, 8).getInt32(0, false) * pow32 +
                    new DataView(data.buffer, data.byteOffset + 4, 8).getUint32(4, false);
                return new Date(sec * 1000 + ns / 1000000);
            }
            throw new Error("Invalid data length for a date value.");
        }
    }

    function encodeUtf8(value) {
        if (textEncoder) return textEncoder.encode(value);
        let ascii = true;
        const length = value.length;
        for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
            if (value.charCodeAt(i) > 127) {
                ascii = false;
                break;
            }
        }
        let offset = 0;
        const bytes = new Uint8Array(length * (ascii ? 1 : 4));
        for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
            let c = value.charCodeAt(i);
            if (c < 128) {
                bytes[offset++] = c;
            } else if (c < 2048) {
                bytes[offset++] = (c >> 6) | 192;
                bytes[offset++] = (c & 63) | 128;
            } else {
                if (c > 0xd7ff && c < 0xdc00) {
                    if (++i >= length) throw new Error("UTF-8 encode: incomplete surrogate pair");
                    const c2 = value.charCodeAt(i);
                    if (c2 < 0xdc00 || c2 > 0xdfff) {
                        throw new Error("UTF-8 encode: second surrogate character out of range");
                    }
                    c = 0x10000 + ((c & 0x03ff) << 10) + (c2 & 0x03ff);
                    bytes[offset++] = (c >> 18) | 240;
                    bytes[offset++] = ((c >> 12) & 63) | 128;
                } else {
                    bytes[offset++] = (c >> 12) | 224;
                }
                bytes[offset++] = ((c >> 6) & 63) | 128;
                bytes[offset++] = (c & 63) | 128;
            }
        }
        return ascii ? bytes : bytes.subarray(0, offset);
    }

    function decodeUtf8(bytes, start, length) {
        if (textDecoder) return textDecoder.decode(bytes.subarray(start, start + length));
        let i = start;
        const end = start + length;
        let value = "";
        while (i < end) {
            let c = bytes[i++];
            if (c > 127) {
                if (c > 191 && c < 224) c = ((c & 31) << 6) | (bytes[i++] & 63);
                else if (c > 223 && c < 240) c = ((c & 15) << 12) | ((bytes[i++] & 63) << 6) | (bytes[i++] & 63);
                else if (c > 239 && c < 248) c = ((c & 7) << 18) | ((bytes[i++] & 63) << 12) | ((bytes[i++] & 63) << 6) | (bytes[i++] & 63);
                else throw new Error("UTF-8 decode: unknown multibyte start");
            }
            if (c <= 0xffff) value += String.fromCharCode(c);
            else {
                c -= 0x10000;
                value += String.fromCharCode((c >> 10) | 0xd800, (c & 0x3ff) | 0xdc00);
            }
        }
        return value;
    }

    const msgpack = {
        serialize: serialize,
        deserialize: deserialize,
        encode: serialize,
        decode: deserialize
    };

    if (typeof module === "object" && module && typeof module.exports === "object") module.exports = msgpack;
    else window[window.msgpackJsName || "msgpack"] = msgpack;
})();

config.anotherVisual = true;

globalThis.waitTime = function(timeout) {
    return new Promise((done) => {
        setTimeout(() => {
            done();
        }, timeout);
    });
}
// STORAGE:
globalThis.canStore = null;
if (typeof Storage !== "undefined") {
    canStore = true;
}
globalThis.saveVal = function(name, val) {
    localStorage.setItem(name, val);
}
globalThis.deleteVal = function(name) {
    if (canStore) localStorage.removeItem(name);
}
globalThis.getSavedVal = function(name) {

globalThis.gC = function (a, b) {
    try {
        let res = JSON.parse(getSavedVal(a));
        if (typeof res === "object") return b;
        else return res;
    } catch (e) {
        alert("dieskid");
        return b;
    }

// Helper function to check if hat is bought (from Main mod)
function isBoughtHat(id, type) {
    if (!player) return false;
    if (type === 0) {
        return player.skins && player.skins[id];
    } else if (type === 1) {
        return player.tails && player.tails[id];
    }
    return false;
}
