class HtmlAction {
    constructor(element) {
        this.element = element;
    }
    add(code) {
        if (!this.element) return undefined;
        this.element.innerHTML += code;
    }
    newLine(amount) {
        let result = `<br>`;
        if (amount > 0) {
            result = ``;
            for (let i = 0; i < amount; i++) {
                result += `<br>`;
            }
        }
        this.add(result);
    }
    checkBox(setting) {
        let newCheck = `<input type = "checkbox"`;
        setting.id && (newCheck += ` id = ${setting.id}`);
        setting.style && (newCheck += ` style = ${setting.style.replaceAll(" ", "")}`);
        setting.class && (newCheck += ` class = ${setting.class}`);
        setting.checked && (newCheck += ` checked`);
        setting.onclick && (newCheck += ` onclick = ${setting.onclick}`);
        newCheck += `>`;
        this.add(newCheck);
    }
    text(setting) {
        let newText = `<input type = "text"`;
        setting.id && (newText += ` id = ${setting.id}`);
        setting.style && (newText += ` style = ${setting.style.replaceAll(" ", "")}`);
        setting.class && (newText += ` class = ${setting.class}`);
        setting.size && (newText += ` size = ${setting.size}`);
        setting.maxLength && (newText += ` maxLength = ${setting.maxLength}`);
        setting.value && (newText += ` value = ${setting.value}`);
        setting.placeHolder && (newText += ` placeHolder = ${setting.placeHolder.replaceAll(" ", "&nbsp;")}`);
        newText += `>`;
        this.add(newText);
    }
    select(setting) {
        let newSelect = `<select`;
        setting.id && (newSelect += ` id = ${setting.id}`);
        setting.style && (newSelect += ` style = ${setting.style.replaceAll(" ", "")}`);
        setting.class && (newSelect += ` class = ${setting.class}`);
        newSelect += `>`;
        for (let options in setting.option) {
            newSelect += `<option value = ${setting.option[options].id}`;
            setting.option[options].selected && (newSelect += ` selected`);
            newSelect += `>${options}</option>`;
        }
        newSelect += `</select>`;
        this.add(newSelect);
    }
    button(setting) {
        let newButton = `<button`;
        setting.id && (newButton += ` id = ${setting.id}`);
        setting.style && (newButton += ` style = ${setting.style.replaceAll(" ", "")}`);
        setting.class && (newButton += ` class = ${setting.class}`);
        setting.onclick && (newButton += ` onclick = ${setting.onclick}`);
        newButton += `>`;
        setting.innerHTML && (newButton += setting.innerHTML);
        newButton += `</button>`;
        this.add(newButton);
    }
    selectMenu(setting) {
        let newSelect = `<select`;
        if (!setting.id) {
            alert("please put id skid");
            return;
        }
        window[setting.id + "Func"] = function () { };
        setting.id && (newSelect += ` id = ${setting.id}`);
        setting.style && (newSelect += ` style = ${setting.style.replaceAll(" ", "")}`);
        setting.class && (newSelect += ` class = ${setting.class}`);
        newSelect += ` onchange = window.${setting.id + "Func"}()`;
        newSelect += `>`;
        let last;
        let i = 0;
        for (let options in setting.menu) {
            newSelect += `<option value = ${"option_" + options} id = ${"O_" + options
                }`;
            setting.menu[options] && (newSelect += ` checked`);
            newSelect += ` style = "color: ${setting.menu[options] ? "#000" : "#fff"
                }; background: ${setting.menu[options] ? "#8ecc51" : "#cc5151"
                };">${options}</option>`;
            i++;
        }
        newSelect += `</select>`;
        this.add(newSelect);
        i = 0;
        for (let options in setting.menu) {
            window[options + "Func"] = function () {
                setting.menu[options] = getEl("check_" + options).checked
                    ? true
                    : false;
                saveVal(options, setting.menu[options]);
                getEl("O_" + options).style.color = setting.menu[options]
                    ? "#000"
                    : "#fff";
                getEl("O_" + options).style.background = setting.menu[options]
                    ? "#8ecc51"
                    : "#cc5151";
                //getEl(setting.id).style.color = setting.menu[options] ? "#8ecc51" : "#cc5151";
            };
            this.checkBox({
                id: "check_" + options,
                style: `display: ${i == 0 ? "inline-block" : "none"};`,
                class: "checkB",
                onclick: `window.${options + "Func"}()`,
                checked: setting.menu[options],
            });
            i++;
        }
        last = "check_" + getEl(setting.id).value.split("_")[1];
        window[setting.id + "Func"] = function () {
            getEl(last).style.display = "none";
            last = "check_" + getEl(setting.id).value.split("_")[1];
            getEl(last).style.display = "inline-block";
            //getEl(setting.id).style.color = setting.menu[last.split("_")[1]] ? "#8ecc51" : "#fff";
        };
    }
}
class Html {
    constructor() {
        this.element = null;
        this.action = null;
        this.startDiv = function (setting, func) {
            let newDiv = document.createElement("div");
            setting.id && (newDiv.id = setting.id);
            setting.style && (newDiv.style = setting.style);
            setting.class && (newDiv.className = setting.class);
            this.element.appendChild(newDiv);
            let addRes = new HtmlAction(newDiv);
            typeof func == "function" && func(addRes);
        };
        this.addDiv = function (setting, func) {
            let newDiv = document.createElement("div");
            setting.id && (newDiv.id = setting.id);
            setting.style && (newDiv.style = setting.style);
            setting.class && (newDiv.className = setting.class);
            setting.appendID && getEl(setting.appendID).appendChild(newDiv);
            let addRes = new HtmlAction(newDiv);
            typeof func == "function" && func(addRes);
        };
    }
    set(id) {
        this.element = getEl(id);
        this.action = new HtmlAction(this.element);
    }
    resetHTML(text) {
        if (text) this.element.innerHTML = ``;
        else this.element.innerHTML = ``;
    }
    setStyle(style) {
        this.element.style = style;
    }
    setCSS(style) {
        this.action.add(`<style>` + style + `</style>`);
    }
    addStyle(id, style) {
		let styleElement = document.getElementById(id);
		if (!styleElement) {
			styleElement = document.createElement("style");
			styleElement.id = id;
			document.head.appendChild(styleElement);
		}
		styleElement.textContent = style;
	}
}
globalThis.validID = function(id, Default, arr) {
    const num = +id;
    return (Number.isInteger(num) && String(num) === String(id) && (!arr || (arr.length + 1) >= num)) ? num : (Default === undefined ? "NaN" : Default);
}

globalThis.totalClientMessages = "";
window.clearChat = function () {
    window.collectChatInfo();
    const element = menuChats;
    for (let i = 0; i < element.children.length; i++) {
        const child = element.children[i];
        if (!child) break;
        if (!["rgb(247, 166, 91)", "rgb(184, 132, 90)"].includes(child.style.color)) {
            element.removeChild(child);
            i--;
        }
    }
}
window.collectChatInfo = function () {
    const chatElement = menuChats;
    for (let i = 0; i < chatElement.children.length; i++) {
        const elChild = chatElement.children[i];
        if (!elChild) break;
        totalClientMessages += elChild.textContent;
    }
}

        const theTime = document.createElement("span");
        theTime.style.color = "rgba(200, 200, 200, 0.35)";
        theTime.textContent = `${(hour < 10 ? "0" : "") + hour}:${(min < 10 ? "0" : "") + min}  `;
        x.appendChild(theTime);
    }
    if (name) {
        const theTime = document.createElement("span");
        theTime.style.color = isPlayer ? "rgb(93, 95, 255)" : "white";
        theTime.textContent = ` ${name}: `;
        x.appendChild(theTime);
    }
    if (message) {
        const theMessage = document.createElement("span");
        theMessage.style.color = color;
        let noNewLine = false;
        let translateData = null;
        theMessage.textContent = `${message}\n`;
        x.appendChild(theMessage);
    }
    menuChats.appendChild(x);
    const scrollHeight = menuChats.scrollHeight;
    if (scrollHeight - menuChats.scrollTop <= menuChats.clientHeight + 100) menuChats.scrollTop = scrollHeight;
    menuChCounts++;
}
globalThis.resetMenuChText = function() {
    menuChats.innerHTML = ``;
    menuChCounts = 0;
    addMenuChText(null, "", "white", 1); // chat history
}
globalThis.menuIndex = 0;
globalThis.menus = ["menuMain", "menuConfig", "menuOther"];
window.changeMenu = function () {
    getEl(menus[menuIndex % menus.length]).style.display = "none";
    menuIndex++;
    getEl(menus[menuIndex % menus.length]).style.display = "block";
};
globalThis.openMenu = false;
globalThis.autoAcceptEnabled = false;
globalThis.WS = undefined;
globalThis.socketID = undefined;
globalThis.stoppedGathering = true;

globalThis.secPacket = 0;
globalThis.secMax = 120;
globalThis.secTime = 1000;
globalThis.firstSend = {
    sec: false,
};
globalThis.game = {
    tick: 0,
    tickQueue: [],
    waitingActions: { actions: [], ticks: [] },
    tickBase: function (set, tick) {
        if (this.tickQueue[this.tick + tick]) this.tickQueue[this.tick + tick].push(set);
        else this.tickQueue[this.tick + tick] = [set];
    },
    tickWait: function (set, tick) {
        let stringedFuncs = this.waitingActions.actions.map((e) => JSON.stringify(e))
        if (stringedFuncs.includes(JSON.stringify(set))) {
            let ticks = this.waitingActions.ticks[stringedFuncs.indexOf(JSON.stringify(set))];
            if (ticks < game.tick + tick) this.waitingActions.ticks[stringedFuncs.indexOf(JSON.stringify(set))] = game.tick + tick;
        } else {
            this.waitingActions.actions.push(set);
            this.waitingActions.ticks.push(game.tick + tick);
        }
    },
    tickRate: 1000 / 9,
    tickSpeed: 0,
    lastTick: performance.now(),
};
globalThis.modConsole = [];
globalThis.dontSend = false;
globalThis.fpsTimer = {
    last: 0,
    time: 0,
    ltime: 0,
};
globalThis.noZ = false;
globalThis.lastMoveDir = undefined;
globalThis.lastsp = ["cc", 1, "__proto__"];
globalThis.packetCaller = null;
globalThis.realPlayerSid = null;
globalThis.bot0 = null;
globalThis.surroundEnemy = function(target, radius, fullCircle = false, givenAng = 0, Protectfullcircle = false) {
    const botsCount = botAmt;
    const positions = new Array(botsCount);
    let spread;
    let startAngle;
    if (fullCircle) {
        spread = PI2;
        startAngle = Protectfullcircle ? givenAng - spread / 2 : 0;
    } else {
        spread = Math.PI;
        startAngle = givenAng - spread / 2;
    }
    const step = spread / Math.max(1, botsCount - (fullCircle ? 0 : 1));
    for (let i = 0; i < botsCount; i++) {
        const angle = fullCircle
            ? startAngle + step * i
            : startAngle + step * i;
        positions[i] = {
            x: target.x2 + Math.cos(angle) * radius,
            y: target.y2 + Math.sin(angle) * radius,
            angle
        };
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < botsCount; i++) {
        const bot = bots[i];
        const pos = positions[i];
        if (!pos) continue;
        bot.targetPos = getMoveTarget(bot, pos);
    }
}
globalThis.pointBlocked = function(x, y, padding = 35) {
    const pad2 = (padding + 10) * (padding + 10);
    for (let i = 0; i < bot.botObjects.length; i++) {
        const obj = gameObjects[i];
        if (!obj) continue;
        const dx = x - obj.x;
        const dy = y - obj.y;
        const dist2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
        const r = obj.scale || 1;
        const r2 = r * r;
        if (dist2 < r2 + pad2) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}
globalThis.blockedCache = new Map();
globalThis.clearBlockCache = function() {
    blockedCache.clear();
}
globalThis.lineBlocked = function(x1, y1, x2, y2) {
    const samples = 8;
    for (let i = 0; i <= samples; i++) {
        const t = i / samples;
        const x = x1 + (x2 - x1) * t;
        const y = y1 + (y2 - y1) * t;
        if (pointBlocked(x, y)) return true;
    }
    return false;
}
globalThis.getMoveTarget = function(bot, targetPos) {
    if (!targetPos) {
        return { x: bot.x2, y: bot.y2 };
    }
    if (!lineBlocked(bot.x2, bot.y2, targetPos.x, targetPos.y)) {
        return targetPos;
    }
    const baseDir = fastAtan2(
        targetPos.y - bot.y2,
        targetPos.x - bot.x2
    );
    const angles = [
        0,
        Math.PI / 2,
        -Math.PI / 2,
        Math.PI / 4,
        -Math.PI / 4
    ];
    const distances = [120, 220];
    let best = null;
    let bestScore = 1e18;
    for (let d = 0; d < distances.length; d++) {
        for (let a = 0; a < angles.length; a++) {
            const dir = baseDir + angles[a];
            const x = bot.x2 + Math.cos(dir) * distances[d];
            const y = bot.y2 + Math.sin(dir) * distances[d];
            if (pointBlocked(x, y)) continue;
            if (lineBlocked(bot.x2, bot.y2, x, y)) continue;
            const dx = targetPos.x - x;
            const dy = targetPos.y - y;
            const score = dx * dx + dy * dy;
            if (score < bestScore) {
                bestScore = score;
                best = { x, y };
            }
        }
    }
    return best || targetPos;
}
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_REACHED_DIST_SQ = 35 * 35;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_WAYPOINT_DIST_SQ = 30 * 30;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_GOAL_REPATH_DIST_SQ = 26 * 26;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_ROUTE_REFRESH_TICKS = 6;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_ROUTE_FAIL_TICKS = 3;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_OBSTACLE_PADDING = 1150;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_MAX_ROUTE_DIST_SQ = 1040 * 1040;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_SIDESTEP_DIST = 145;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_LINE_CLEARANCE = 34;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_GOAL_CLEARANCE = 62;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_BUILD_CLEARANCE = 48;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_DANGER_CLEARANCE = 92;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_CACHE_MAX_AGE_TICKS = 20;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_ROUTE_CACHE_SIZE = 40;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_MAX_PENDING_ROUTES = 8;
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_MAX_ROUTE_LENGTH = 120; // max number of coordinates (x,y pairs)
globalThis.AUTOSURROUND_MAX_NEW_PATHS_PER_TICK = 1;
globalThis.autoSurroundRouteCache = new Map();
globalThis.autoSurroundRoutePending = new Map();
globalThis.autoSurroundRouteRequestsThisTick = 0;
globalThis.lastAutoSurroundRequestTick = -1;
globalThis.autoSurroundObjectSourceCache = { tick: -1, source: null };
globalThis.autoSurroundTick = function() {
    return (typeof game !== "undefined" && typeof game.tick === "number") ? game.tick : 0;
}
globalThis.resetAutoSurroundPath = function(bot) {
    bot.autoSurroundPath = [];
    bot.autoSurroundPathGoal = null;
    bot.autoSurroundTargetPos = null;
    bot.autoSurroundPathPending = false;
    bot.autoSurroundPathTick = 0;
    bot.autoSurroundPathFailTick = 0;
    bot.autoSurroundPathRequestedTick = 0;
    bot.autoSurroundPathKey = null;
    bot.autoSurroundPathIndex = 0;
}
globalThis.getAutoSurroundPathScale = function(obj) {
    if (typeof obj.pathScale === "number" && obj.pathScale > 0) return obj.pathScale;
    if (obj.isCactus) return obj.scale * 0.6 + 15;
    if (obj.type === 0) return obj.scale * 0.7 + 10;
    if (obj.dmg && !obj.inClan || obj.teleport || obj.boostSpeed) return obj.scale + 47;
    if (obj.name == "pit trap" && !obj.inClan) return 57;
    return (obj.scale || 0) + 5;
}
globalThis.getAutoSurroundClearance = function(obj) {
    if (obj.dmg && !obj.inClan || obj.isCactus) return AUTOSURROUND_DANGER_CLEARANCE;
    if (obj.trap && !obj.inClan) return AUTOSURROUND_DANGER_CLEARANCE;
    if (obj.teleport || obj.boostSpeed) return AUTOSURROUND_GOAL_CLEARANCE;
    if (obj.blocker) return AUTOSURROUND_GOAL_CLEARANCE;
    return AUTOSURROUND_BUILD_CLEARANCE;
}
globalThis.getAutoSurroundObjectSource = function() {
    const tick = autoSurroundTick();
    if (autoSurroundObjectSourceCache.tick === tick && Array.isArray(autoSurroundObjectSourceCache.source)) return autoSurroundObjectSourceCache.source;
    const source = [];
    const seenRefs = new Set();
    const seenKeys = new Set();
    const pools = [gameObjects, liztobj, nearObjects, renderObj, autoplacing, restrictPlace];
    for (let p = 0; p < pools.length; p++) {
        const pool = pools[p];
        if (!pool?.length) continue;
        for (let i = 0; i < pool.length; i++) {
            const obj = pool[i];
            if (!obj || seenRefs.has(obj)) continue;
            const key = obj.sid != undefined ? obj.sid : `${obj.x}:${obj.y}:${obj.scale}:${obj.id}`;
            if (seenKeys.has(key)) continue;
            seenRefs.add(obj);
            seenKeys.add(key);
            source.push(obj);
        }
    }
    autoSurroundObjectSourceCache.tick = tick;
    autoSurroundObjectSourceCache.source = source;
    return source;
}
globalThis.getAutoSurroundObstacles = function(bot, goal) {
    const obstacles = [];
    const source = getAutoSurroundObjectSource();
    const minX = mathMin(bot.x2, goal.x) - AUTOSURROUND_OBSTACLE_PADDING;
    const maxX = mathMax(bot.x2, goal.x) + AUTOSURROUND_OBSTACLE_PADDING;
    const minY = mathMin(bot.y2, goal.y) - AUTOSURROUND_OBSTACLE_PADDING;
    const maxY = mathMax(bot.y2, goal.y) + AUTOSURROUND_OBSTACLE_PADDING;
    for (let i = 0; i < source.length; i++) {
        const obj = source[i];
        if (!obj.active || obj.ignoreCollision && (!obj.trap || obj.inClan)) continue;
        const basePathScale = getAutoSurroundPathScale(obj);
        if (!(basePathScale > 0)) continue;
        const clearance = getAutoSurroundClearance(obj);
        const pathScale = basePathScale + clearance;
        if (obj.x + pathScale < minX || obj.x - pathScale > maxX || obj.y + pathScale < minY || obj.y - pathScale > maxY) continue;
        // keep potential obstacle
        obstacles.push({ x: obj.x, y: obj.y, pathScale, basePathScale, clearance });
    }
    // If there are many obstacles, cluster nearby ones into buckets and keep only the most relevant
    if (obstacles.length <= 80) return obstacles;
    const BUCKET = 90; // clustering granularity
    const buckets = new Map();
    for (let i = 0; i < obstacles.length; i++) {
        const o = obstacles[i];
        const bx = Math.floor(o.x / BUCKET);
        const by = Math.floor(o.y / BUCKET);
        const key = bx + ':' + by;
        if (!buckets.has(key)) buckets.set(key, { x: o.x, y: o.y, pathScale: o.pathScale, basePathScale: o.basePathScale, clearance: o.clearance });
        else {
            const p = buckets.get(key);
            const w1 = Math.max(1, p.pathScale);
            const w2 = Math.max(1, o.pathScale);
            const nx = (p.x * w1 + o.x * w2) / (w1 + w2);
            const ny = (p.y * w1 + o.y * w2) / (w1 + w2);
            const nscale = Math.max(p.pathScale, o.pathScale) + Math.min(12, Math.floor(Math.sqrt(p.pathScale * o.pathScale) / 6));
            p.x = nx; p.y = ny; p.pathScale = nscale; p.basePathScale = Math.max(p.basePathScale, o.basePathScale); p.clearance = Math.max(p.clearance, o.clearance);
        }
    }
    const merged = Array.from(buckets.values());
    // score by closeness to bot-goal corridor and size, prefer large/close obstacles
    for (let i = 0; i < merged.length; i++) {
        const m = merged[i];
        const distSq = autoSurroundSegmentDistSq(m.x, m.y, bot.x2, bot.y2, goal.x, goal.y);
        m._score = (m.pathScale * 2) / (Math.sqrt(distSq) + 16);
    }
    merged.sort((a, b) => b._score - a._score);
    const MAX_KEEP = 60;
    return merged.slice(0, Math.max(MAX_KEEP, Math.min(merged.length, 60)));
}
globalThis.getAutoSurroundObstacleFingerprint = function(obstacles) {
    let hash = obstacles.length;
    for (let i = 0; i < obstacles.length; i++) {
        const obj = obstacles[i];
        hash = (hash * 31 + ((obj.x | 0) ^ (obj.y << 16) ^ (obj.pathScale << 8))) >>> 0;
    }
    return hash;
}
globalThis.getAutoSurroundPathKey = function(bot, goal, obstacles) {
    return [
        Math.round(bot.x2 / 8),
        Math.round(bot.y2 / 8),
        Math.round(goal.x / 8),
        Math.round(goal.y / 8),
        getAutoSurroundObstacleFingerprint(obstacles)
    ].join(":");
}
globalThis.autoSurroundSegmentDistSq = function(px, py, ax, ay, bx, by) {
    const dx = bx - ax;
    const dy = by - ay;
    const lenSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
    if (lenSq <= 0.0001) return squareDist(px - ax, py - ay);
    const t = mathMax(0, mathMin(1, ((px - ax) * dx + (py - ay) * dy) / lenSq));
    return squareDist(px - (ax + dx * t), py - (ay + dy * t));
}
globalThis.getAutoSurroundSegmentInfo = function(px, py, ax, ay, bx, by) {
    const dx = bx - ax;
    const dy = by - ay;
    const lenSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
    if (lenSq <= 0.0001) return { distSq: squareDist(px - ax, py - ay), t: 0 };
    const t = mathMax(0, mathMin(1, ((px - ax) * dx + (py - ay) * dy) / lenSq));
    return {
        distSq: squareDist(px - (ax + dx * t), py - (ay + dy * t)),
        t
    };
}
globalThis.getAutoSurroundBlockingObject = function(from, to, obstacles) {
    const fromX = from.x2 ?? from.x;
    const fromY = from.y2 ?? from.y;
    const toX = to.x2 ?? to.x;
    const toY = to.y2 ?? to.y;
    let best = null;
    let bestT = Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < obstacles.length; i++) {
        const obj = obstacles[i];
        const scale = obj.pathScale + AUTOSURROUND_LINE_CLEARANCE;
        const hit = getAutoSurroundSegmentInfo(obj.x, obj.y, fromX, fromY, toX, toY);
        if (hit.distSq <= scale * scale && hit.t < bestT) {
            best = obj;
            bestT = hit.t;
        }
    }
    return best;
}
globalThis.autoSurroundLineBlocked = function(from, to, obstacles) {
    return !!getAutoSurroundBlockingObject(from, to, obstacles);
}
globalThis.getAutoSurroundUnsafeObject = function(point, obstacles, extra = 0) {
    const pointX = point.x2 ?? point.x;
    const pointY = point.y2 ?? point.y;
    for (let i = 0; i < obstacles.length; i++) {
        const obj = obstacles[i];
        const scale = obj.pathScale + extra;
        if (squareDist(pointX - obj.x, pointY - obj.y) <= scale * scale) return obj;
    }
    return null;
}
globalThis.getAutoSurroundSafeGoal = function(bot, goal, obstacles) {
    const safe = { x: goal.x, y: goal.y };
    for (let pass = 0; pass < 7; pass++) {
        let moved = false;
        for (let i = 0; i < obstacles.length; i++) {
            const obj = obstacles[i];
            const minDist = obj.pathScale + AUTOSURROUND_GOAL_CLEARANCE;
            let dx = safe.x - obj.x;
            let dy = safe.y - obj.y;
            let distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
            if (distSq >= minDist * minDist) continue;
            let dist = sqrt(distSq);
            if (dist < 0.001) {
                const fallback = fastAtan2(goal.y - bot.y2, goal.x - bot.x2);
                dx = cos(fallback);
                dy = sin(fallback);
                dist = 1;
            }
            safe.x += (dx / dist) * (minDist - dist + 8);
            safe.y += (dy / dist) * (minDist - dist + 8);
            safe.x = mathMax(0, mathMin(14400, safe.x));
            safe.y = mathMax(0, mathMin(14400, safe.y));
            moved = true;
        }
        if (!moved) break;
    }
    return safe;
}
globalThis.smoothAutoSurroundPath = function(path, obstacles) {
    if (!Array.isArray(path) || path.length <= 4) return path;
    const smoothed = [path[0], path[1]];
    let anchorX = path[0];
    let anchorY = path[1];
    for (let i = 2; i < path.length; i += 2) {
        const nextX = path[i];
        const nextY = path[i + 1];
        const probe = { x: nextX, y: nextY };
        if (!autoSurroundLineBlocked({ x: anchorX, y: anchorY }, probe, obstacles) && !getAutoSurroundUnsafeObject(probe, obstacles, AUTOSURROUND_LINE_CLEARANCE)) continue;
        smoothed.push(nextX, nextY);
        anchorX = nextX;
        anchorY = nextY;
    }
    if (smoothed[0] !== path[path.length - 2] || smoothed[1] !== path[path.length - 1]) smoothed.push(path[path.length - 2], path[path.length - 1]);
    return smoothed;
}
globalThis.canSyncTo = function(bot, target) {
    const targetX = target.x2 ?? target.x;
    const targetY = target.y2 ?? target.y;
    const obstacles = getAutoSurroundObstacles(bot, { x: targetX, y: targetY });
    if (getAutoSurroundUnsafeObject(bot, obstacles, AUTOSURROUND_LINE_CLEARANCE)) return false;
    const targetPoint = { x: targetX, y: targetY };
    if (!autoSurroundLineBlocked(bot, targetPoint, obstacles)) return true;
    const blocker = getAutoSurroundBlockingObject(bot, targetPoint, obstacles);
    if (!blocker) return false;
    const sidestep = getAutoSurroundAroundObjectDir(bot, targetPoint, blocker, obstacles);
    const probe = {
        x: bot.x2 + cos(sidestep) * AUTOSURROUND_SIDESTEP_DIST,
        y: bot.y2 + sin(sidestep) * AUTOSURROUND_SIDESTEP_DIST
    };
    return !getAutoSurroundUnsafeObject(probe, obstacles, AUTOSURROUND_LINE_CLEARANCE) && !autoSurroundLineBlocked(bot, probe, obstacles);
}
globalThis.isAutoSurroundPathPointReachable = function(bot, point, obstacles) {
    return !!point && !getAutoSurroundUnsafeObject(point, obstacles) && !autoSurroundLineBlocked(bot, point, obstacles);
}
globalThis.getAutoSurroundAroundObjectDir = function(bot, goal, blocker, obstacles) {
    if (!blocker) return fastAtan2(goal.y - bot.y2, goal.x - bot.x2);
    const toObj = fastAtan2(bot.y2 - blocker.y, bot.x2 - blocker.x);
    const goalDir = fastAtan2(goal.y - bot.y2, goal.x - bot.x2);
    const awayDir = fastAtan2(bot.y2 - blocker.y, bot.x2 - blocker.x);
    const candidates = [
        toObj + PI * 0.5,
        toObj - PI * 0.5,
        goalDir + PI * 0.5,
        goalDir - PI * 0.5,
        goalDir + PI * 0.32,
        goalDir - PI * 0.32,
        awayDir
    ];
    let bestDir = candidates[0];
    let bestScore = Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i++) {
        const dir = candidates[i];
        const probe = {
            x: bot.x2 + cos(dir) * AUTOSURROUND_SIDESTEP_DIST,
            y: bot.y2 + sin(dir) * AUTOSURROUND_SIDESTEP_DIST
        };
        const unsafe = getAutoSurroundUnsafeObject(probe, obstacles, AUTOSURROUND_LINE_CLEARANCE);
        const blockerClearance = squareDist(probe.x - blocker.x, probe.y - blocker.y) - (blocker.pathScale + AUTOSURROUND_LINE_CLEARANCE) ** 2;
        let score = squareDist(probe.x - goal.x, probe.y - goal.y) + dAng(dir, goalDir) * 4500;
        if (blockerClearance < 0) score += -blockerClearance * 12;
        if (unsafe) score += 180000;
        if (autoSurroundLineBlocked(bot, probe, obstacles)) score += 220000;
        if (score < bestScore) {
            bestScore = score;
            bestDir = dir;
        }
    }
    return bestDir;
}
globalThis.requestAutoSurroundPath = function(bot, goal, targetPos, obstacles) {
    const pathKey = getAutoSurroundPathKey(bot, goal, obstacles);
    bot.autoSurroundPathKey = pathKey;
    bot.autoSurroundPathPending = true;
    bot.autoSurroundPathGoal = { x: goal.x, y: goal.y };
    bot.autoSurroundTargetPos = { x: targetPos.x, y: targetPos.y };
    bot.autoSurroundPathRequestedTick = autoSurroundTick();
    const cached = autoSurroundRouteCache.get(pathKey);
    if (cached && autoSurroundTick() - cached.tick <= AUTOSURROUND_CACHE_MAX_AGE_TICKS) {
        bot.autoSurroundPathPending = false;
        bot.autoSurroundPath = cached.route.slice(0, AUTOSURROUND_MAX_ROUTE_LENGTH);
        bot.autoSurroundPathIndex = 0;
        bot.autoSurroundPathTick = autoSurroundTick();
        bot.autoSurroundPathFailTick = bot.autoSurroundPath.length ? 0 : bot.autoSurroundPathTick;
        return;
    }
    if (autoSurroundRoutePending.size >= AUTOSURROUND_MAX_PENDING_ROUTES) {
        // too many pending path requests; avoid memory spike
        bot.autoSurroundPathPending = false;
        bot.autoSurroundPath = [];
        bot.autoSurroundPathIndex = 0;
        bot.autoSurroundPathFailTick = autoSurroundTick();
        return;
    }
    const currentTick = autoSurroundTick();
    if (currentTick !== lastAutoSurroundRequestTick) {
        lastAutoSurroundRequestTick = currentTick;
        autoSurroundRouteRequestsThisTick = 0;
    }
    if (autoSurroundRouteRequestsThisTick >= AUTOSURROUND_MAX_NEW_PATHS_PER_TICK) {
        bot.autoSurroundPathPending = false;
        return;
    }
    autoSurroundRouteRequestsThisTick++;
    if (autoSurroundRoutePending.has(pathKey)) {
        autoSurroundRoutePending.get(pathKey).then(route => {
            bot.autoSurroundPathPending = false;
            if (!bot.autoSurroundPathGoal || !bot.autoSurroundTargetPos || !bot.targetPos || squareDist(bot.autoSurroundPathGoal.x - goal.x, bot.autoSurroundPathGoal.y - goal.y) > AUTOSURROUND_GOAL_REPATH_DIST_SQ || squareDist(bot.autoSurroundTargetPos.x - bot.targetPos.x, bot.autoSurroundTargetPos.y - bot.targetPos.y) > AUTOSURROUND_GOAL_REPATH_DIST_SQ) {
                bot.autoSurroundPath = [];
                return;
            }
            bot.autoSurroundPath = Array.isArray(route) ? smoothAutoSurroundPath(route, obstacles).slice(0, AUTOSURROUND_MAX_ROUTE_LENGTH) : [];
            bot.autoSurroundPathIndex = 0;
            bot.autoSurroundPathTick = autoSurroundTick();
            bot.autoSurroundPathFailTick = bot.autoSurroundPath.length ? 0 : bot.autoSurroundPathTick;
        }).catch(() => {
            bot.autoSurroundPathPending = false;
            bot.autoSurroundPath = [];
            bot.autoSurroundPathFailTick = autoSurroundTick();
        });
        return;
    }
    // adaptive tuning options based on obstacle count and distance
    const distToTarget = hypot(bot.x2, bot.y2, goal.x, goal.y);
    const obstacleCount = obstacles ? obstacles.length : 0;
    const coarseStep = Math.max(1, Math.min(6, Math.round(2 + obstacleCount / 30 + distToTarget / 2200)));
    const heuristicMult = Math.max(0.8, 0.994 + Math.min(0.6, obstacleCount / 150));
    const corridorRadiusMul = 1 + Math.min(2, obstacleCount / 80);
    const pending = pathfindRoute({
        player: { x2: bot.x2, y2: bot.y2, sid: bot.sid },
        target: { x: goal.x, y: goal.y },
        buildings: obstacles,
        clan: allianceSids,
        options: {
            coarseStep,
            heuristicMult,
            corridorRadiusMul
        }
    });
    autoSurroundRoutePending.set(pathKey, pending);
    pending.then(route => {
        autoSurroundRoutePending.delete(pathKey);
        const safeRoute = Array.isArray(route) ? smoothAutoSurroundPath(route, obstacles) : [];
        if (safeRoute.length) {
            const cachedRoute = safeRoute.slice(0, AUTOSURROUND_MAX_ROUTE_LENGTH);
            autoSurroundRouteCache.set(pathKey, { route: cachedRoute, tick: autoSurroundTick()});
            if (autoSurroundRouteCache.size > AUTOSURROUND_ROUTE_CACHE_SIZE) {
                const oldestKey = autoSurroundRouteCache.keys().next().value;
                autoSurroundRouteCache.delete(oldestKey);
            }
        }
        bot.autoSurroundPathPending = false;
        if (!bot.autoSurroundPathGoal || !bot.autoSurroundTargetPos || !bot.targetPos || squareDist(bot.autoSurroundPathGoal.x - goal.x, bot.autoSurroundPathGoal.y - goal.y) > AUTOSURROUND_GOAL_REPATH_DIST_SQ || squareDist(bot.autoSurroundTargetPos.x - bot.targetPos.x, bot.autoSurroundTargetPos.y - bot.targetPos.y) > AUTOSURROUND_GOAL_REPATH_DIST_SQ) {
            bot.autoSurroundPath = [];
            return;
        }
        bot.autoSurroundPath = safeRoute.slice(0, AUTOSURROUND_MAX_ROUTE_LENGTH);
        bot.autoSurroundPathIndex = 0;
        bot.autoSurroundPathTick = autoSurroundTick();
        bot.autoSurroundPathFailTick = bot.autoSurroundPath.length ? 0 : bot.autoSurroundPathTick;
    }).catch(() => {
        autoSurroundRoutePending.delete(pathKey);
        bot.autoSurroundPathPending = false;
        bot.autoSurroundPath = [];
        bot.autoSurroundPathFailTick = autoSurroundTick();
    });
}

globalThis.getAutoSurroundMoveDir = function(bot, goal) {
    if (!goal) return undefined;
    const rawGoal = goal;
    let obstacles = getAutoSurroundObstacles(bot, rawGoal);
    goal = getAutoSurroundSafeGoal(bot, rawGoal, obstacles);
    obstacles = getAutoSurroundObstacles(bot, goal);
    const goalDistSq = squareDist(goal.x - bot.x2, goal.y - bot.y2);
    const directDir = fastAtan2(goal.y - bot.y2, goal.x - bot.x2);
    if (goalDistSq <= AUTOSURROUND_REACHED_DIST_SQ) return undefined;
    const closeRisk = getAutoSurroundUnsafeObject(bot, obstacles, AUTOSURROUND_LINE_CLEARANCE);
    const blocker = getAutoSurroundBlockingObject(bot, goal, obstacles) || closeRisk;
    const tick = autoSurroundTick();
    const pathKey = getAutoSurroundPathKey(bot, goal, obstacles);
    const goalChanged = bot.autoSurroundPathKey !== pathKey;
    const stale = !bot.autoSurroundPathTick || tick - bot.autoSurroundPathTick > AUTOSURROUND_ROUTE_REFRESH_TICKS;
    const failedRecently = bot.autoSurroundPathFailTick && tick - bot.autoSurroundPathFailTick < AUTOSURROUND_ROUTE_FAIL_TICKS;
    const canRoute = goalDistSq <= AUTOSURROUND_MAX_ROUTE_DIST_SQ;
    if (canRoute && !bot.autoSurroundPathPending && !failedRecently && (goalChanged || stale || !bot.autoSurroundPath?.length)) requestAutoSurroundPath(bot, goal, rawGoal, obstacles);
    const path = bot.autoSurroundPath || [];
    let pathIndex = bot.autoSurroundPathIndex || 0;
    if (path.length >= 2 && pathIndex < path.length) {
        while (pathIndex + 1 < path.length && squareDist(bot.x2 - path[pathIndex], bot.y2 - path[pathIndex + 1]) <= AUTOSURROUND_WAYPOINT_DIST_SQ) {
            pathIndex += 2;
        }
        if (pathIndex >= path.length) {
            bot.autoSurroundPath = [];
            bot.autoSurroundPathIndex = 0;
        } else bot.autoSurroundPathIndex = pathIndex;
    }
    if (path.length >= 2 && pathIndex + 1 < path.length) {
        const currentPointX = path[pathIndex];
        const currentPointY = path[pathIndex + 1];
        const currentBlocked = !isAutoSurroundPathPointReachable(bot, { x: currentPointX, y: currentPointY }, obstacles);
        if (currentBlocked && canRoute && !bot.autoSurroundPathPending && !failedRecently) requestAutoSurroundPath(bot, goal, rawGoal, obstacles);
        let pointIndex = -1;
        let bestScore = Infinity;
        const maxCheckIndex = mathMin(path.length - 2, 40);
        for (let i = maxCheckIndex; i >= pathIndex; i -= 2) {
            const px = path[i];
            const py = path[i + 1];
            if (!isAutoSurroundPathPointReachable(bot, { x: px, y: py }, obstacles)) continue;
            const distToGoal = squareDist(px - goal.x, py - goal.y);
            const distToBot = squareDist(px - bot.x2, py - bot.y2);
            const anglePenalty = dAng(fastAtan2(py - bot.y2, px - bot.x2), directDir) * 3600;
            const score = distToGoal + distToBot * 0.22 + anglePenalty;
            if (score < bestScore) {
                bestScore = score;
                pointIndex = i;
            }
        }
        if (pointIndex >= 0) return fastAtan2(path[pointIndex + 1] - bot.y2, path[pointIndex] - bot.x2);
        for (let i = pathIndex; i <= maxCheckIndex; i += 2) {
            const px = path[i];
            const py = path[i + 1];
            if (isAutoSurroundPathPointReachable(bot, { x: px, y: py }, obstacles)) {
                return fastAtan2(py - bot.y2, px - bot.x2);
            }
        }
    }
    if (blocker) return getAutoSurroundAroundObjectDir(bot, goal, blocker, obstacles);
    return directDir;
}
globalThis.packet2 = function(type) {
    // EXTRACT DATA ARRAY:
    let data = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments, 1);
    packetCaller = data[data.length - 1];
    packets.push([
        performance.now(),
        [type, data],
        msgpack.encode([c2s[type], data]),
        packetCaller,
    ]);
    if (type != "9" || !noMove || packetCaller == "antionetick" || packetCaller == "onetick") {
        // SEND MESSAGE:
        newUpdateBullshit(type, data);
    }
}

globalThis.j = function(e, t) {
    return e >>> t | e << 32 - t
}
globalThis.Fo = new Uint32Array([1116352408, 1899447441, 3049323471, 3921009573, 961987163, 1508970993, 2453635748, 2870763221, 3624381080, 310598401, 607225278, 1426881987, 1925078388, 2162078206, 2614888103, 3248222580, 3835390401, 4022224774, 264347078, 604807628, 770255983, 1249150122, 1555081692, 1996064986, 2554220882, 2821834349, 2952996808, 3210313671, 3336571891, 3584528711, 113926993, 338241895, 666307205, 773529912, 1294757372, 1396182291, 1695183700, 1986661051, 2177026350, 2456956037, 2730485921, 2820302411, 3259730800, 3345764771, 3516065817, 3600352804, 4094571909, 275423344, 430227734, 506948616, 659060556, 883997877, 958139571, 1322822218, 1537002063, 1747873779, 1955562222, 2024104815, 2227730452, 2361852424, 2428436474, 2756734187, 3204031479, 3329325298]);
globalThis.Vt = function(e) {
    const t = new Uint32Array([1779033703, 3144134277, 1013904242, 2773480762, 1359893119, 2600822924, 528734635, 1541459225])
    , i = e.length
    , s = i * 8
    , n = i + 9
    , a = new Uint8Array(Math.ceil(n / 64) * 64);
    a.set(e),
    a[i] = 128;
    const o = new DataView(a.buffer);
    o.setUint32(a.length - 4, s >>> 0, !1),
    o.setUint32(a.length - 8, Math.floor(s / 4294967296), !1);
    const d = new Uint32Array(64);
    for (let m = 0; m < a.length; m += 64) {
        for (let w = 0; w < 16; w++)
            d[w] = o.getUint32(m + w * 4, !1);
        for (let w = 16; w < 64; w++) {
            const T = j(d[w - 15], 7) ^ j(d[w - 15], 18) ^ d[w - 15] >>> 3
            , A = j(d[w - 2], 17) ^ j(d[w - 2], 19) ^ d[w - 2] >>> 10;
            d[w] = d[w - 16] + T + d[w - 7] + A | 0
        }
        let g = t[0]
        , h = t[1]
        , u = t[2]
        , p = t[3]
        , x = t[4]
        , I = t[5]
        , P = t[6]
        , f = t[7];
        for (let w = 0; w < 64; w++) {
            const T = j(x, 6) ^ j(x, 11) ^ j(x, 25)
            , A = x & I ^ ~x & P
            , V = f + T + A + Fo[w] + d[w] | 0
            , W = j(g, 2) ^ j(g, 13) ^ j(g, 22)
            , S = g & h ^ g & u ^ h & u
            , H = W + S | 0;
            f = P,
            P = I,
            I = x,
            x = p + V | 0,
            p = u,
            u = h,
            h = g,
            g = V + H | 0
        }
        t[0] = t[0] + g | 0,
        t[1] = t[1] + h | 0,
        t[2] = t[2] + u | 0,
        t[3] = t[3] + p | 0,
        t[4] = t[4] + x | 0,
        t[5] = t[5] + I | 0,
        t[6] = t[6] + P | 0,
        t[7] = t[7] + f | 0
    }
    const l = new Uint8Array(32)
    , c = new DataView(l.buffer);
    for (let m = 0; m < 8; m++)
        c.setUint32(m * 4, t[m], !1);
    return l
}
globalThis.he = 64;
globalThis.Ao = function(e, t) {
    let i = e;
    i.length > he && (i = Vt(i));
    const s = new Uint8Array(he);
    s.set(i);
    const n = new Uint8Array(he + t.length)
    , a = new Uint8Array(he + 32);
    for (let o = 0; o < he; o++)
        n[o] = s[o] ^ 54,
        a[o] = s[o] ^ 92;
    return n.set(t, he),
    a.set(Vt(n), he),
    Vt(a)
}
globalThis.Eo = function(e, t) {
    return Ao(e, t).subarray(0, 6);
}
globalThis.initKey = function(e) {
    const t = new Uint8Array(e.length / 2);
    for (let i = 0; i < t.length; i++)
        t[i] = parseInt(e.substr(i * 2, 2), 16);
    return t
}

globalThis.getMessage = function(message) {
    let data = new Uint8Array(message.data);
    let parsed = window.msgpack.decode(data);
    let type = parsed[0];
    data = parsed[1];
    if (type == "io-init") {
        console.log("IO-INIT");
        if (data[3] === 1) {
            console.log("CREATING TABLES");
            socketMode = 1;
            socketKey = initKey(data[2]);
            createTables(data[1] >>> 0);
            socketID = data[0];
            spawnPlayer();
        }
        return;
    }
    if (typeof type == "number") type = s2c[type];
    let events = {
        A: setInitData, // id: setInitData,
        B: disconnect,
        C: setupGame, // 1: setupGame,
        D: addPlayer, // 2: addPlayer,
        E: removePlayer, // 4: removePlayer,
        a: updatePlayers, // 33: updatePlayers,
        G: updateLeaderboard, // 5: updateLeaderboard,here
        H: loadGameObject, // 6: loadGameObject,
        I: loadAI, // a: loadAI,
        J: animateAI, // aa: animateAI,
        K: gatherAnimation, // 7: gatherAnimation,
        L: wiggleGameObject, // 8: wiggleGameObject,
        M: shootTurret, // sp: shootTurret,
        N: updatePlayerValue, // 9: updatePlayerValue,
        O: updateHealth, // h: updateHealth,//here
        P: killPlayer, // 11: killPlayer,
        Q: killObject, // 12: killObject,
        R: killObjects, // 13: killObjects,
        S: updateItemCounts, // 14: updateItemCounts,
        T: updateAge, // 15: updateAge,
        U: updateUpgrades, // 16: updateUpgrades,
        V: updateItems, // 17: updateItems,
        X: addProjectile, // 18: addProjectile,
        Y: remProjectile, // 19: remProjectile,
        Z: serverShutdownNotice,
        g: addAlliance,
        1: deleteAlliance,
        2: allianceNotification, // an: allianceNotification,
        3: setPlayerTeam, // st: setPlayerTeam,
        4: setAlliancePlayers, // sa: setAlliancePlayers,
        5: updateStoreItems, // us: updateStoreItems,
        6: receiveChat, // ch: receiveChat,
        7: updateMinimap, // mm: updateMinimap,
        8: showText, // t: showText,
        9: pingMap, // p: pingMap,
        0: pingSocketResponse,
    };
    events[type].apply(undefined, data);
}

globalThis.ensureJoinRequestsPanel = function() {
    const joinPanel = panelElements["joinrequests"]
    if (!joinPanel || joinPanel.dataset.ready == "true") return joinPanel;
    joinPanel.dataset.ready = "true";
    joinPanel.style.color = "white";
    joinPanel.style.padding = "4px";
    const title = document.createElement("div");
    title.textContent = "Alliance join requests";
    title.style.fontSize = "18px";
    title.style.fontWeight = "700";
    title.style.marginBottom = "12px";
    const status = document.createElement("div");
    status.id = "join-requests-status";
    status.style.fontSize = "12px";
    status.style.opacity = "0.8";
    status.style.marginBottom = "12px";
    const list = document.createElement("div");
    list.id = "join-requests-list";
    list.style.display = "flex";
    list.style.flexDirection = "column";
    list.style.gap = "8px";
    joinPanel.appendChild(title);
    joinPanel.appendChild(status);
    joinPanel.appendChild(list);
    return joinPanel;
}
globalThis.removeNotificationBySid = function(sid) {
    for (let i = 0; i < notifications.length; i++) {
        if (notifications[i].sid != sid) continue;
        notifications.splice(i, 1);
        return;
    }
}
globalThis.renderJoinRequests = function() {
    const joinPanel = ensureJoinRequestsPanel();
    if (!joinPanel) return;
    const status = joinPanel.querySelector("#join-requests-status");
    const list = joinPanel.querySelector("#join-requests-list");
    if (!status || !list) return;
    const requestCount = notifications.length;
    status.textContent = requestCount ? `${requestCount} pending request${requestCount == 1 ? "" : "s"}` : "No pending join requests";
    list.innerHTML = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < notifications.length; i++) {
        const request = notifications[i];
        const row = document.createElement("div");
        row.style.display = "flex";
        row.style.alignItems = "center";
        row.style.justifyContent = "space-between";
        row.style.gap = "10px";
        row.style.padding = "10px 12px";
        row.style.borderRadius = "8px";
        row.style.background = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)";
        row.style.border = "1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12)";
        const label = document.createElement("div");
        label.textContent = `${(request.hour < 10 ? "0" : "") + request.hour}:${(request.min < 10 ? "0" : "") + request.min} - ${request.name} [${request.sid}] wants to join ${player?.team || "your alliance"}`;
        label.style.flex = "1";
        label.style.wordBreak = "break-word";
        row.appendChild(label);
        if (player?.isOwner) {
            const acceptButton = document.createElement("button");
            acceptButton.textContent = "Accept";
            acceptButton.style.padding = "6px 10px";
            acceptButton.style.border = "none";
            acceptButton.style.borderRadius = "6px";
            acceptButton.style.cursor = "pointer";
            acceptButton.style.color = "white";
            acceptButton.style.background = "rgb(38, 166, 91)";
            acceptButton.onclick = () => {
                packet("P", request.sid, 1, "accept join request");
                removeNotificationBySid(request.sid);
                renderJoinRequests();
                if (saySettings) packet("6", `${request.name} has been accepted.`, 1);
                else {
                    player.chat.message = `${request.name} has been accepted.`;
                    player.chat.count = 2000;
                }
            };
            row.appendChild(acceptButton);
        }
        list.appendChild(row);
    }
}
globalThis.accepts = 0;
globalThis.allianceNotification = function(sid, name) {
    let time = new Date();
    let min = time.getMinutes();
    let hour = time.getHours();
    notifications.push({ sid: sid, name: name, hour: hour, min: min });
    renderJoinRequests();
    if ((botSIDs.includes(sid) || bots.find2((e) => e.sid == sid) || autoAcceptEnabled) && player.isOwner) {
        accepts++;
        setTimeout(() => {
            accepts--;
            lastAccept = performance.now();
            packet2("P", sid, 1, "autoAccept");
            renderJoinRequests();
        }, accepts * 550/* + mathMax(0, 1000 - (performance.now() - lastAccept))*/);
    }
}

}

}
// MATHS:
globalThis.lerpAngle = function (value1, value2, amount) {
    let difference = abs(value2 - value1);
    if (difference > PI) {
        if (value1 > value2) value2 += PI2;
        else value1 += PI2;
    }
    let value = value2 + (value1 - value2) * amount;
    if (value >= 0 && value <= PI2) return value;
    return value % PI2;
};
// REOUNDED RECTANGLE:
CanvasRenderingContext2D.prototype.roundRect = function (x, y, w, h, r) {
    if (w < 2 * r) r = w * 0.5;
    if (h < 2 * r) r = h * 0.5;
    if (r < 0) r = 0;
    this.beginPath();
    this.moveTo(x + r, y);
    this.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r);
    this.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
    this.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r);
    this.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r);
    this.closePath();
    return this;
};

    avg: 0,
    max: 0,

globalThis.pinger = function() {
    pingTimeout && clearTimeout(pingTimeout),
        (sendTime = performance.now(), packet2("0")),
        pingTimeout = setTimeout(pinger, 400)
}
globalThis.pingSocketResponse = function() {
    const pingTime = performance.now() - sendTime;
    window.pingTime = pingTime;
    const pingDisplay = document.getElementById("pingDisplay");
    pingDisplay.innerText = "";
    if (pingTime > ms.max) ms.max = pingTime;
    if (pingTime < ms.min) ms.min = pingTime;
    fpsEl.textContent = `${Math.round(pingTime * 10) / 5}ms | Fps: ${lastFPS}`;
    if (pingTime < 90) return;
    player.chat.message = `High Ping (${Math.round(window.pingTime * 10) / 5})`;
    player.chat.count = 1000;
    // if (pingTime >= 90) {
    //     doAutoQ = true;
    // } else {
    //     doAutoQ = false;
    // }
}
globalThis.placeVisible = [];
/** CLASS CODES */
class uwutils {
    constructor() {
        // MATH UWUTILS:
        let mathABS = abs,
            mathSQRT = sqrt,
            mathATAN2 = fastAtan2,
            mathPI = PI;
        let _this = this;
        // GLOBAL UWUTILS:
        this.averageAngle = function (angles) {
            let sin2 = 0, cos2 = 0;
            for (let i = 0; i < angles.length; i++) {
                sin2 += sin(angles[i]);
                cos2 += cos(angles[i]);
            }
            return fastAtan2(sin2 / angles.length, cos2 / angles.length);
        }
        this.getMidAngle = function (a, b) {
            return fastAtan2((sin(a) + sin(b)) * 0.5, (cos(a) + cos(b)) * 0.5);
        };
        this.round = function (n, v) {
            return round(n * v) / v;
        };
        this.toRad = function (angle) {
            return angle / 180 * mathPI;
        };
        this.toAng = function (radian) {
            return radian / mathPI * 180;
        };
        this.randInt = function (min, max) {
            return ((random() * (max - min + 1)) | 0) + min;
        };
        this.randFloat = function (min, max) {
            return random() * (max - min + 1) + min;
        };
        this.lerp = function (value1, value2, amount) {
            return value1 + (value2 - value1) * amount;
        };
        this.decel = function (val, cel) {
            if (val > 0) val = mathMax(0, val - cel);
            else if (val < 0) val = mathMin(0, val + cel);
            return val;
        };
        this.getDistance = function (x1, y1, x2, y2) {
            return hypot(x2 - x1, y2 - y1);
        };
        this.getRealDistance = function (x1, y1, x2, y2) {
            return mathSQRT((x2 -= x1) * x2 + (y2 -= y1) * y2);
        };
        this.getDist = function (tmp1, tmp2, type1, type2) {
            const tmpXY1 = {
                x:
                    type1 == 0
                        ? tmp1.x
                        : type1 == 1
                            ? tmp1.x1
                            : type1 == 2
                                ? tmp1.x2
                                : type1 == 3 ? tmp1.x3
                                    : type1 == 4 ? tmp1.x4
                                        : type1 == 5 ? tmp1.x5
                                            : 0,
                y:
                    type1 == 0
                        ? tmp1.y
                        : type1 == 1
                            ? tmp1.y1
                            : type1 == 2
                                ? tmp1.y2
                                : type1 == 3 ? tmp1.y3
                                    : type1 == 4 ? tmp1.y4
                                        : type1 == 5 ? tmp1.y5
                                            : 0,
            };
            const tmpXY2 = {
                x:
                    type2 == 0
                        ? tmp2.x
                        : type2 == 1
                            ? tmp2.x1
                            : type2 == 2
                                ? tmp2.x2
                                : type2 == 3 ? tmp2.x3
                                    : type2 == 4 ? tmp2.x4
                                        : type2 == 5 ? tmp2.x5
                                            : 0,
                y:
                    type2 == 0
                        ? tmp2.y
                        : type2 == 1
                            ? tmp2.y1
                            : type2 == 2
                                ? tmp2.y2
                                : type2 == 3 ? tmp2.y3
                                    : type2 == 4 ? tmp2.y4
                                        : type2 == 5 ? tmp2.y5
                                            : 0,
            };
            return mathSQRT((tmpXY2.x -= tmpXY1.x) * tmpXY2.x + (tmpXY2.y -= tmpXY1.y) * tmpXY2.y);
        };
        this.getDirection = function (x1, y1, x2, y2) {
            return mathATAN2(y1 - y2, x1 - x2);
        };
        this.getDirect = function (tmp1, tmp2, type1, type2) {
            const tmpXY1 = {
                x:
                    type1 == 0
                        ? tmp1.x
                        : type1 == 1
                            ? tmp1.x1
                            : type1 == 2
                                ? tmp1.x2
                                : type1 == 3 ? tmp1.x3
                                    : type1 == 4 ? tmp1.x4
                                        : type1 == 5 ? tmp1.x5
                                            : 0,
                y:
                    type1 == 0
                        ? tmp1.y
                        : type1 == 1
                            ? tmp1.y1
                            : type1 == 2
                                ? tmp1.y2
                                : type1 == 3 ? tmp1.y3
                                    : type1 == 4 ? tmp1.y4
                                        : type1 == 5 ? tmp1.y5
                                            : 0,
            };
            const tmpXY2 = {
                x:
                    type2 == 0
                        ? tmp2.x
                        : type2 == 1
                            ? tmp2.x1
                            : type2 == 2
                                ? tmp2.x2
                                : type2 == 3 ? tmp2.x3
                                    : type2 == 4 ? tmp2.x4
                                        : type2 == 5 ? tmp2.x5
                                            : 0,
                y:
                    type2 == 0
                        ? tmp2.y
                        : type2 == 1
                            ? tmp2.y1
                            : type2 == 2
                                ? tmp2.y2
                                : type2 == 3 ? tmp2.y3
                                    : type2 == 4 ? tmp2.y4
                                        : type2 == 5 ? tmp2.y5
                                            : 0,
            };
            return fastAtan2(tmpXY1.y - tmpXY2.y, tmpXY1.x - tmpXY2.x);
        };
        this.getAngleDist = function (a, b) {
            let p = mathABS(b - a) % (PI2);
            return p > PI ? PI2 - p : p;
        };
        this.isNumber = function (n) {
            return typeof n == "number" && !isNaN(n) && isFinite(n);
        };
        this.isString = function (s) {
            return s && typeof s == "string";
        };
        this.kFormat = function (num) {
            return num > 999 ? (num / 1000).toFixed(1) + "k" : num;
        };
        this.sFormat = function (num) {
            let fixs = [
                {
                    num: 1e3,
                    string: "k",
                },
                {
                    num: 1e6,
                    string: "m",
                },
                {
                    num: 1e9,
                    string: "b",
                },
                {
                    num: 1e12,
                    string: "q",
                },
            ].reverse();
            let sp = fixs.find2((v) => num >= v.num);
            if (!sp) return num;
            return (num / sp.num).toFixed(1) + sp.string;
        };
        this.capitalizeFirst = function (string) {
            return string.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + string.slice(1);
        };
        this.fixTo = function (n, v) {
            return parseFloat(n.toFixed(v));
        };
        this.sortByPoints = function (a, b) {
            return parseFloat(b.points) - parseFloat(a.points);
        };
        this.angleWidth = function (x1, y1, x2, y2, rad) {
            const dist = hypot(x1 - x2, y1 - y2);
            return dist <= rad ? PI2 : atan(rad / dist);
        }
        this.lineInRect = function (recX, recY, recX2, recY2, x1, y1, x2, y2) {
            let minX = x1;
            let maxX = x2;
            if (x1 > x2) {
                minX = x2;
                maxX = x1;
            }
            if (maxX > recX2) maxX = recX2;
            if (minX < recX) minX = recX;
            if (minX > maxX) return false;
            let minY = y1;
            let maxY = y2;
            let dx = x2 - x1;
            if (abs(dx) > 0.0000001) {
                let a = (y2 - y1) / dx;
                let b = y1 - a * x1;
                minY = a * minX + b;
                maxY = a * maxX + b;
            }
            if (minY > maxY) {
                let tmp = maxY;
                maxY = minY;
                minY = tmp;
            }
            if (maxY > recY2) maxY = recY2;
            if (minY < recY) minY = recY;
            if (minY > maxY) return false;
            return true;
        };
        this.checkLineCollision = function (th, plyr, objs, r, pre, dist) {
            const newPos = {
                x: pre ? plyr.x3 : plyr.x2,
                y: pre ? plyr.y3 : plyr.y2
            }
            for (let i = 0; i < objs.length; i++) {
                const obj = objs[i];
                if (hypot(obj.x - newPos.x, obj.y - newPos.y) > dist || obj.ignoreCollision && (!obj.trap || obj.isTeamObject(plyr))) continue;
                const thCos = cos(th);
                const thSin = sin(th);
                const len = (obj.x - newPos.x) * thCos + (obj.y - newPos.y) * thSin;
                const closeX = newPos.x + thCos * len;
                const closeY = newPos.y + thSin * len;
                const dx = closeX - obj.x;
                const dy = closeY - obj.y;
                const rad = obj.scale + r;
                if ((dx * dx + dy * dy) < (rad * rad)) return obj;
            }
            return false;
        }
        this.containsPoint = function (element, x, y) {
            let bounds = element.getBoundingClientRect();
            let left = bounds.left + window.scrollX;
            let top = bounds.top + window.scrollY;
            let width = bounds.width;
            let height = bounds.height;
            let insideHorizontal = x > left && x < left + width;
            let insideVertical = y > top && y < top + height;
            return insideHorizontal && insideVertical;
        };
        this.mousifyTouchEvent = function (event) {
            let touch = event.changedTouches[0];
            event.screenX = touch.screenX;
            event.screenY = touch.screenY;
            event.clientX = touch.clientX;
            event.clientY = touch.clientY;
            event.pageX = touch.pageX;
            event.pageY = touch.pageY;
        };
        this.removeAllChildren = function (element) {
            while (element.hasChildNodes()) {
                element.removeChild(element.lastChild);
            }
        };
        this.generateElement = function (config) {
            let element = document.createElement(config.tag || "div");
            function bind(configValue, elementValue) {
                if (config[configValue]) element[elementValue] = config[configValue];
            }
            bind("text", "textContent");
            bind("html", "innerHTML");
            bind("class", "className");
            for (let key in config) {
                switch (key) {
                    case "tag":
                    case "text":
                    case "html":
                    case "class":
                    case "style":
                    case "parent":
                    case "children":
                        continue;
                    default:
                        break;
                }
                element[key] = config[key];
            }
            if (element.onclick) element.onclick = this.checkTrusted(element.onclick);
            if (element.onmouseover)
                element.onmouseover = this.checkTrusted(element.onmouseover);
            if (element.onmouseout)
                element.onmouseout = this.checkTrusted(element.onmouseout);
            if (config.style) element.style.cssText = config.style;
            if (config.parent) config.parent.appendChild(element);
            if (config.children) {
                for (let i = 0; i < config.children.length; i++) {
                    element.appendChild(config.children[i]);
                }
            }
            return element;
        };
        this.checkTrusted = function (callback) {
            return function (ev) {
                if (ev && ev instanceof Event && (ev && typeof ev.isTrusted == "boolean" ? ev.isTrusted : true)) callback(ev);
            }
        };
        this.hookTouchEvents = function(e, t) {
            const i = !t;
            let s = !1;
            const n = !1;
            e.addEventListener("touchstart", this.checkTrusted(r), n),
                e.addEventListener("touchmove", this.checkTrusted(o), n),
                e.addEventListener("touchend", this.checkTrusted(l), n),
                e.addEventListener("touchcancel", this.checkTrusted(l), n),
                e.addEventListener("touchleave", this.checkTrusted(l), n);
            function r(c) {
                hi(c),
                    window.setUsingTouch(!0),
                    i && (c.preventDefault(),
                        c.stopPropagation()),
                    e.onmouseover && e.onmouseover(c),
                    s = !0
            }
            function o(c) {
                hi(c),
                    window.setUsingTouch(!0),
                    i && (c.preventDefault(),
                        c.stopPropagation()),
                    Wr(e, c.pageX, c.pageY) ? s || (e.onmouseover && e.onmouseover(c),
                                                    s = !0) : s && (e.onmouseout && e.onmouseout(c),
                                                                    s = !1)
            }
            function l(c) {
                hi(c),
                    window.setUsingTouch(!0),
                    i && (c.preventDefault(),
                        c.stopPropagation()),
                    s && (e.onclick && e.onclick(c),
                        e.onmouseout && e.onmouseout(c),
                        s = !1)
            }
        }
        this.randomString = function (length) {
            let text = "";
            let possible =
                "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789";
            for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
                text += possible.charAt((random() * possible.length) | 0);
            }
            return text;
        };
        this.countInArray = function (array, val) {
            let count = 0;
            for (let i = 0; i < array.length; i++) {
                if (array[i] === val) count++;
            }
            return count;
        };
        this.hexToRgb = function (hex) {
            return hex
                .slice(1)
                .match(/.{1,2}/g)
                .map((g) => parseInt(g, 16));
        };
        this.getRgb = function (r, g, b) {
            return [r / 255, g / 255, b / 255].join(", ");
        };
    }
}
globalThis.animTextCache = new Map();
globalThis.getAnimTextKey = function(text, color, crit) {
    return text + "|" + color + "|" + (crit ? 1 : 0);
}
globalThis.BASE_FONT = 40;
globalThis.buildAnimTextSprite = function(t) {
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    const font = BASE_FONT + "px Lilita One";
    ctx.font = font;
    const w = ctx.measureText(t.text).width;
    const h = BASE_FONT * 1.5;
    canvas.width = w + 10;
    canvas.height = h;
    ctx.font = font;
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
    ctx.shadowBlur = (t.crit ? 5 : 6);
    ctx.shadowColor = t.color;
    ctx.fillStyle = t.color;
    ctx.strokeStyle = (t.crit ? critOutlineColor : darkOutlineColor);
    const x = canvas.width * 0.5;
    const y = canvas.height * 0.5;
    ctx.strokeText(t.text, x, y);
    ctx.fillText(t.text, x, y);
    return canvas;
}
globalThis.getAnimTextSprite = function(t) {
    const key = getAnimTextKey(t.text, t.color, t.crit);
    let sprite = animTextCache.get(key);
    if (!sprite) {
        sprite = buildAnimTextSprite(t);
        animTextCache.set(key, sprite);
    }
    return sprite;
}
class Animtext {
    init(x, y, scale, speed, text, crit, color) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        this.crit = crit;
        this.color = color;
        this.scale = 2;
        this.invScale = 1 / this.scale; // cache inverse
        this.startScale = scale;
        this.maxScale = 1.5 * scale;
        this.fadeInSpeed = 0.1 * scale;
        this.speed = speed;
        this.text = text;
        const dir = Math.random() * PI2 - PI;
        this.speedMult = Math.random() * 0.1;
        this.xMult = cos(dir);
        this.yMult = sin(dir);
        this.moveSpeed = 0;
        this.maxSpeed = 1;
        this.decel = 0;
    }
    update(dlta) {
        let moveSpeed = this.moveSpeed;
        let scale = this.scale;
        let invScale = this.invScale;
        const speed = this.speed;
        if (this.decel) {
            const f = 1 - 0.018 * speed;
            moveSpeed *= f;
            scale *= f;
            scale -= 2.5 * speed * invScale;
        } else if (moveSpeed > this.maxSpeed) {
            this.decel = 1;
            const f = 1 - 0.05 / speed;
            moveSpeed *= f;
            scale *= f;
        } else {
            if (dlta < 15) {
                const d = dlta > 10 ? dlta : 10;
                moveSpeed += (d * speed) * 0.0033333; // precomputed /300
                scale += this.fadeInSpeed * d * speed * invScale;
            } else {
                moveSpeed = 0;
                scale = 0;
            }
        }
        // update inverse once
        invScale = 1 / scale;
        this.moveSpeed = moveSpeed;
        this.scale = scale;
        this.invScale = invScale;
        const dist = moveSpeed * dlta * this.speedMult;
        this.x += this.xMult * dist;
        this.y += this.yMult * dist;
    }
    render(ctxt, xOff, yOff) {
        const sprite = getAnimTextSprite(this);
        const x = this.x - xOff;
        const y = this.y - yOff;
        const scaleFactor = this.scale / BASE_FONT;
        const w = sprite.width * scaleFactor;
        const h = sprite.height * scaleFactor;
        ctxt.shadowBlur = 0;
        ctxt.drawImage(
            sprite,
            x - w * 0.5,
            y - h * 0.5,
            w,
            h
        );
    }
}
class Textmanager {
    constructor() {
        this.stack = [];
        this.texts = [];
        this.pool = []; // object pool
    }
    update(dlta, ctxt, xOff, yOff) {
        const startTime = performance.now();
        const texts = this.texts;
        let lastColor = null;
        ctxt.globalAlpha = 1;
        ctxt.shadowOffsetX = 0;
        ctxt.shadowOffsetY = 0;
        ctxt.strokeStyle = darkOutlineColor;
        ctxt.textBaseline = "middle";
        ctxt.textAlign = "center";
        for (let i = texts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const t = texts[i];
            t.update(dlta);
            if (t.scale <= 0) {
                // recycle instead of delete
                this.pool.push(t);
                texts[i] = texts[texts.length - 1];
                texts.pop();
                continue;
            }
            // avoid redundant state changes
            if (t.color !== lastColor) {
                lastColor = t.color;
                ctxt.shadowColor = lastColor;
                ctxt.fillStyle = lastColor;
            }
            t.render(ctxt, xOff, yOff);
        }
        ctxt.shadowColor = null;
        ctxt.shadowBlur = 0;
        //textRenderTime = performance.now() - startTime;
    }
    showText(x, y, scale, speed, text, crit, color) {
        // reuse object if possible
        let t = this.pool.length ? this.pool.pop() : new Animtext();
        t.init(x, y, scale, speed, text, crit, color);
        this.texts.push(t);
    }
}
class GameObject {
    constructor(sid) {
        this.sid = sid;
        this.breakPointsMoofieMoostafaBullshit = 0;
        // INIT:
        this.init = function (x, y, dir, scale, type, data, owner, bot, fake, restricter, preplace) {
            data = data ?? {};
            if (data.trap) this.isVisible = false;
            this.prePlaced = false;
            this.preplaced = 0;
            this.breakPointsMoofieMoostafaBullshit = 0;
            this.trapPoints = {};
            this.connections = new Set();
            this.dists = new Map();
            this.placePoints = 0;
            this.active = true;
            this.render = true;
            this.doUpdate = data.doUpdate;
            this.syncHit = false;
            this.bot = bot;
            this.preplace = preplace;
            this.fake = fake;
            this.invincible = data.invincible;
            this.opacity2 = 1;
            this.restricter = restricter;
            this.x = x;
            this.y = y;
            this.dir = dir;
            this.alpha = (!owner && type == 0 && squareDist(x - player.x2, y - player.y2) <= 160000) ? 0.5 : 1;
            this.preplaceTimeout = undefined;
            this.aboutToBreak = false;
            this.lastDir = dir;
            this.xWiggle = 0;
            this.yWiggle = 0;
            this.visScale = scale;
            this.scale = scale;
            this.type = type;
            this.isCactus = type === 1 && y >= 12000;
            this.id = data.id;
            this.owner = owner;
            this.name = data.name;
            this.isItem = this.id != undefined;
            this.hitRange = scale + player.hammer.range;
            this.group = data.group;
            this.maxHealth = data.health;
            this.health = this.maxHealth;
            this.layer = 2;
            if (this.group != undefined) this.layer = this.group.layer;
            else if (this.type == 0) this.layer = 3;
            else if (this.type == 2) this.layer = 0;
            else if (this.type == 4) this.layer = -1;
            this.colDiv = data.colDiv || 1;
            this.blocker = data.blocker;
            this.ignoreCollision = data.ignoreCollision;
            this.dontGather = data.dontGather;
            this.hideFromEnemy = data.hideFromEnemy;
            this.friction = data.friction;
            this.projDmg = data.projDmg;
            this.dmg = data.dmg;
            this.pDmg = data.pDmg;
            this.pps = data.pps;
            this.zIndex = data.zIndex || 0;
            this.turnSpeed = data.turnSpeed;
            this.req = data.req;
            this.trap = data.trap;
            this.healCol = data.healCol;
            this.teleport = data.teleport;
            this.boostSpeed = data.boostSpeed;
            this.projectile = data.projectile;
            this.shootRange = data.shootRange;
            this.shootRate = data.shootRate;
            this.shootCount = this.shootRate;
            this.spawnPoint = data.spawnPoint;
            this.breakObj = false;
            this.alpha = data.alpha || 1;
            this.maxAlpha = data.alpha || 1;
            this.damaged = 0;
            this.realScale = (this.type <= 1 && this.type !== null ? this.scale * .6 : this.scale);
            this.escapeScale = (this.type <= 1 && this.type !== null ? this.scale * .6 : this.scale * this.colDiv);
            this.colScale = (this.escapeScale + 35);
            this.connectScale = (this.escapeScale + 30);
            this.pathScale = this.isCactus ? this.scale * .6 + 15
                : this.type === 0 ? this.scale * .7 + 10
                    : (this.dmg && !checkIsTeam(this?.owner?.sid) || this.teleport || this.boostSpeed) ? this.scale + 47
                        : this.isCactus ? this.scale * .55 + 47
                            : this.name == 'pit trap' && !checkIsTeam(this?.owner?.sid) ? 57
                                : this.ignoreCollision ? 0 : this.scale + 5
        };
        // GET HIT:
        this.changeHealth = function (amount, doer) {
            this.health += amount;
            return this.health <= 100;
        };
        // GET SCALE:
        this.getScale = function (sM, ig) {
            sM = sM || 1;
            return this.scale * ((this.isItem || this.type == 2 || this.type == 3 || this.type == 4) ? 1 : (0.6 * sM)) * (ig ? 1 : this.colDiv);
        };
        // VISIBLE TO PLAYER:
        this.visibleToPlayer = function (player) {
            return (!this.hideFromEnemy || (this.owner && (this.owner == player || (this.owner.team && player.team == this.owner.team))));
        };
        // UPDATE:
        this.update = function (dlta) {
            if (this.active) {
                if (this.xWiggle) this.xWiggle *= 0.99**dlta;
                if (this.yWiggle) this.yWiggle *= 0.99**dlta;
                if (!this.pps && this.turnSpeed) this.dir += this.turnSpeed * delta * 0.5;
                else {
                    let d2 = dAng(this.lastDir, this.dir);
                    if (d2 > 0.01) this.dir += d2 / 5;
                    else this.dir = this.lastDir;
                }
            } else {
                if (this.alive) {
                    this.alpha -= dlta / (200 / this.maxAlpha);
                    this.visScale += dlta / (this.scale * 0.4);
                    if (this.alpha <= 0) {
                        this.alpha = 0;
                        this.alive = false;
                    }
                }
            }
        };
        // CHECK TEAM:
        this.isTeamObject = function (tmpObj) {
            return !this.owner
                ? true
                : tmpObj.sid && ((this.owner && tmpObj.sid == this.owner.sid) ||
                    tmpObj.isTeam(findPlayerBySID(this.owner.sid)));
        };
    }
}
