                teleport: true,
                health: 200,
                colDiv: 0.7,
                scale: 45,
                holdOffset: 20,
                placeOffset: -5,
                itemID: 22,
                itemAID: 38,
            },
        ];
        // CHECK ITEM ID:
        this.checkItem = {
            index: function (id, myItems) {
                return [0, 1, 2].includes(id)
                    ? 0
                    : [3, 4, 5].includes(id)
                        ? 1
                        : [6, 7, 8, 9].includes(id)
                            ? 2
                            : [10, 11, 12].includes(id)
                                ? 3
                                : [13, 14].includes(id)
                                    ? 5
                                    : [15, 16].includes(id)
                                        ? 4
                                        : [17, 18, 19, 21, 22].includes(id)
                                            ? [13, 14].includes(myItems)
                                                ? 6
                                                : 5
                                            : id == 20
                                                ? [13, 14].includes(myItems)
                                                    ? 7
                                                    : 6
                                                : undefined;
            },
        };
        // ASSIGN IDS:
        for (let i = 0; i < this.list.length; ++i) {
            this.list[i].id = i;
            if (this.list[i].pre) this.list[i].pre = i - this.list[i].pre;
        }
        // TROLOLOLOL:
        if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
            function shuffle(a) {
                for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
                    const j = (random() * (i + 1)) | 0;
                    [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];
                }
                return a;
            }
            //shuffle(this.list);
        }
    }
}
class Objectmanager {
    constructor(GameObject, objects, UWUTILS, cfg, players, server) {
        this.disableObj = function (obj) {
            obj.active = false;
        };
        this.hitObj = [];
        let tmpObj;
        this.add = function (sid, x, y, dir, s, type, data, setSID, owner, bot, spectate, fake, restricter, preplace, restrictColor) {
            tmpObj = new GameObject(sid);
            tmpObj.placedTick = game.tick;
            tmpObj.sid = sid;
            tmpObj.init(x, y, dir, s, type, data, owner, bot, fake, restrictColor, preplace);
            if (!fake && !spectate) {
                liztobj.push(tmpObj);
                nearObjects.push(tmpObj);
                gameObjects.push(tmpObj);
            } else {
                //  visualPlace.push
                //thisTick.newBuilds.push(p);
                if (restricter) restrictPlace.push(tmpObj)
                autoplacing = nearObjects.concat(restrictPlace)
            }
            return tmpObj;
        };
        // DISABLE BY SID:
        this.disableBySid = function (sid) {
            let find = findObjectBySid(sid);
            if (find) this.disableObj(find);
        };
        // REMOVE ALL FROM PLAYER:
        this.removeAllItems = function (sid, server) {
            let asdks = gameObjects.filter2((tmp) => tmp.active && tmp.owner && tmp.owner.sid == sid);
            for (let i = 0; i < asdks.length; i++) {
                this.disableObj(asdks[i]);
            }
        };
        // CHECK IF PLACABLE:
        this.checkItemLocation = function (x, y, s, sM, indx, ignoreWater, bot) {
            let cantPlace = (bot ? bot.botObjects : nearObjects).find2((tmp) => tmp.active && squareDist(x - tmp.x, y - tmp.y) < (s + (tmp.blocker ? tmp.blocker : tmp.getScale(sM, tmp.isItem))) ** 2);
            if (cantPlace) return false;
            if (!ignoreWater && indx != 18 && y >= 7200 - config.riverWidth * 0.5 && y <= 7200 + config.riverWidth * 0.5) return false;
            return true;
        };
        this.checkItemLocationAutoPlacing = function (h, m, w, v, x, D, priority) {
            for (let S = 0; S < autoplacing.length; ++S) {
                const obj = autoplacing[S];
                const O = obj.blocker ? obj.blocker : obj.getScale(v, obj.isItem);
                if (obj.active && !(obj.assumeBreak && priority) && checkDist({x: h, y: m}, obj, 0, 0, w + O))
                    return !1
            }
            return !(!D && x != 18 && m >= 7200 - config.riverWidth * 0.5 && m <= 7200 + config.riverWidth * 0.5)
        }
        this.checkItemLocation0 = function (x, y, s, sM) {
            let cantPlace = renderObj.find2((tmp) => tmp.active && squareDist(x - tmp.x, y - tmp.y) < (s + (tmp.blocker ? tmp.blocker : tmp.getScale(sM, tmp.isItem)) - 6.76767) ** 2);
            if (cantPlace) return false;
            if (y >= 7200 - config.riverWidth * 0.5 && y <= 7200 + config.riverWidth * 0.5) return false;
            return true;
        };
        this.checkItemLocation3 = function (e, n, I, r, S, a, outplace, obj, breakSid) {
            const preplaceEnabled = configs.autoPreplace;
            for (var h = 0; h < nearObjects.length; ++h) {
                const asd = nearObjects[h];
                var u = asd.blocker ? asd.blocker : asd.getScale(r, asd.isItem);
                const dx = e - asd.x, dy = n - asd.y, scale = I+u;
                if (dx*dx+dy*dy < scale*scale) {
                    if (breakSid != asd.sid && (!preplaceEnabled || !asd.assumeBreak/* && (I != 52 || squareDist(e - nearHacker.x3, n - nearHacker.y3) > 7569)*/)) return false;
                    obj.overlap.push(asd);
                    obj.preplacer.push(asd.assumeBreak);
                    obj.sids.push(asd.sid)
                }
                //  if (nearObjects[h].active && distance && !(nearObjects[h].assumeBreak&&outplace)) return !1
            }
            return !(!a && 18 != S && n >= 7200 - config.riverWidth * 0.5 && n <= 7200 + config.riverWidth * 0.5)
        }
        this.checkItemLocation4 = function (e, n, I, r, S, a, outplace, obj) {
            const preplaceEnabled = configs.autoPreplace;
            for (var h = 0; h < nearObjects.length; ++h) {
                const asd = nearObjects[h];
                var u = asd.blocker ? asd.blocker : asd.getScale(r, asd.isItem);
                const dx = e - asd.x, dy = n - asd.y, scale = I+u
                if (dx*dx+dy*dy < scale*scale) {
                    if ((!preplaceEnabled || !asd.assumeBreak) && e.sid != nearHacker.inTrap?.sid && (I != 52 || squareDist(e - nearHacker.x3, n - nearHacker.y3) > 7569)) return false;
                    obj.overlap.push(asd)
                    obj.preplacer.push(asd.assumeBreak)
                    obj.sids.push(asd.sid)
                }
                //  if (nearObjects[h].active && distance && !(nearObjects[h].assumeBreak&&outplace)) return !1
            }
            return !(!a && 18 != S && n >= 7200 - config.riverWidth * 0.5 && n <= 7200 + config.riverWidth * 0.5)
        }
        this.checkItemLocation5 = function (e, n, I, r, S, a, outplace, obj) {
            for (var h = 0; h < nearObjects.length; ++h) {
                const asd = nearObjects[h];
                var u = asd.blocker ? asd.blocker : asd.getScale(r, asd.isItem);
                const dx = e - asd.x, dy = n - asd.y, scale = I+u
                if (dx*dx+dy*dy < scale*scale) {
                    if (!asd.assumeBreak) return false;
                    obj.overlap.push(asd);
                    obj.preplacer.push(true);
                    obj.sids.push(asd.sid)
                }
                //  if (nearObjects[h].active && distance && !(nearObjects[h].assumeBreak&&outplace)) return !1
            }
            return !(!a && 18 != S && n >= 7200 - config.riverWidth * 0.5 && n <= 7200 + config.riverWidth * 0.5)
        }
        this.checkItemLocation6 = function (e, n, I, r, S, a, outplace, obj) {
            for (var h = 0; h < nearObjects.length; ++h) {
                const asd = nearObjects[h];
                var u = asd.blocker ? asd.blocker : asd.getScale(r, asd.isItem);
                const dx = e - asd.x, dy = n - asd.y, scale = I+u
                if (dx*dx+dy*dy < scale*scale) {
                    if ((!preplaceEnabled || !asd.assumeBreak) && e.sid != nearHacker?.inTrap?.sid) return false;
                    obj.overlap.push(asd)
                    obj.preplacer.push(asd.assumeBreak)
                    obj.sids.push(asd.sid)
                }
                //  if (nearObjects[h].active && distance && !(nearObjects[h].assumeBreak&&outplace)) return !1
            }
            return !(!a && 18 != S && n >= 7200 - config.riverWidth * 0.5 && n <= 7200 + config.riverWidth * 0.5)
        }
        this.checkItemLocation7 = function (e, n, I, r, S, a, outplace, obj) {
            let count = 0;
            for (var h = 0; h < nearObjects.length; ++h) {
                const asd = nearObjects[h];
                var u = asd.blocker ? asd.blocker : asd.getScale(r, asd.isItem);
                const dx = e - asd.x, dy = n - asd.y, scale = I+u
                if (dx*dx+dy*dy < scale*scale) {
                    if (count > 0 || asd.health > 292.05 && asd.sid != traps.info.sid) return false;
                    count++;
                    obj.overlap = asd;
                    obj.sid = asd.sid;
                }
                //  if (nearObjects[h].active && distance && !(nearObjects[h].assumeBreak&&outplace)) return !1
            }
            return !(!a && 18 != S && n >= 7200 - config.riverWidth * 0.5 && n <= 7200 + config.riverWidth * 0.5)
        }
        this.checkItemLocation8 = function (e, n, I, r) {
            for (var h = 0; h < nearObjects.length; ++h) {
                const asd = nearObjects[h];
                var u = asd.blocker ? asd.blocker : asd.getScale(r, asd.isItem);
                const dx = e - asd.x, dy = n - asd.y, scale = I+u
                if (dx*dx+dy*dy < scale*scale && asd.sid != nearHacker?.inTrap?.sid) return false;
            }
            return true;
        }
        this.checkItemLocation9 = function (e, n, I, r, S, a, outplace, obj) {
            const preplaceEnabled = configs.autoPreplace;
            for (var h = 0; h < nearObjects.length; ++h) {
                const asd = nearObjects[h];
                var u = asd.blocker ? asd.blocker : asd.getScale(r, asd.isItem);
                const dx = e - asd.x, dy = n - asd.y, scale = I+u
                if (dx*dx+dy*dy < scale*scale) {
                    if ((!preplaceEnabled || !asd.assumeBreak) && (asd.sid != nearHacker.inTrap?.sid || S != 15 || squareDist(e - nearHacker.x3, n - nearHacker.y3) > 7569)) return false;
                    obj.overlap.push(asd)
                    obj.preplacer.push(asd.assumeBreak)
                    obj.sids.push(asd.sid)
                }
                //  if (nearObjects[h].active && distance && !(nearObjects[h].assumeBreak&&outplace)) return !1
            }
            return !(!a && 18 != S && n >= 7200 - config.riverWidth * 0.5 && n <= 7200 + config.riverWidth * 0.5)
        }
        /*
        this.checkItemLocation8 = function (e, n, I, r, S, a, outplace, obj) {
            for (var h = 0; h < nearObjects.length; ++h) {
                const asd = nearObjects[h];

        this.checkCanManualPlace = function (x, y, s, sM, indx, ignoreWater, placer) {
            const cantPlace = nearObjects.find2((tmp) => !tmp.assumeBreak && tmp.active && squareDist(x, y, tmp.x, tmp.y) < (s + (tmp.blocker ? tmp.blocker : tmp.getScale(sM, tmp.isItem))) ** 2);
            if (cantPlace) return false;
            if (
                !ignoreWater &&
                indx != 18 &&
                y >= cfg.mapScale * 0.5 - cfg.riverWidth * 0.5 &&
                y <= cfg.mapScale * 0.5 + cfg.riverWidth * 0.5
            )
                return false;
            return true;
        };
        this.checkBlockingItem = function (x, y, s, sM, indx, ignoreWater, placer) {
            return liztobj.find2((tmp) => tmp.active && UWUTILS.getDistance(x, y, tmp.x, tmp.y) < s + (tmp.blocker ? tmp.blocker : tmp.getScale(sM, tmp.isItem))
            );
        };
        this.checkItemLocationPrePlace = function (x, y, s, sM, indx, ignoreWater, objToIgnore) {
            let cantPlace = nearObjects.find2((tmp) => tmp.sid != objToIgnore.sid && tmp.active && UWUTILS.getDistance(x, y, tmp.x, tmp.y) < s + (tmp.blocker ? tmp.blocker : tmp.getScale(sM, tmp.isItem)));
            if (cantPlace) return false;
            if (!ignoreWater && indx != 18 && y >= cfg.mapScale * 0.5 - cfg.riverWidth * 0.5 && y <= cfg.mapScale * 0.5 + cfg.riverWidth * 0.5) return false;
            return true;
        };
        this.checkCollision2 = function (e, t, n) {
            n = n || 1;
            var l = e.x2 - t.x,
                h = e.y2 - t.y,
                u = t.colScale;
            if (abs(l) <= u || abs(h) <= u) {
                var f = sqrt(l * l + h * h) - u;
                if (f <= 0) return t.zIndex > t.zIndex && (t.zIndex = t.zIndex), !0
            }
            return !1
        }
    }
}
class Projectile {
    constructor(players, ais, objectManager, items, config, UWUTILS, server) {
        // INIT:
        this.init = function (indx, x, y, dir, spd, dmg, rng, scl, owner) {
            this.active = true;
            this.tickActive = true;
            this.indx = indx;
            this.x = x;
            this.y = y;
            this.x2 = x;
            this.y2 = y;
            this.dir = dir;
            this.skipMov = true;
            this.speed = spd;
            this.dmg = dmg;
            this.scale = scl;
            this.range = rng;
            this.r2 = rng;
            this.owner = owner;
        };
        // UPDATE:
        this.update = function (dlta) {
            if (this.active) {
                const tmpSpeed = this.speed * dlta;
                if (!this.skipMov) {
                    this.x += tmpSpeed * cos(this.dir);
                    this.y += tmpSpeed * sin(this.dir);
                    this.range -= tmpSpeed;
                    if (this.range <= 0) {
                        this.x += this.range * cos(this.dir);
                        this.y += this.range * sin(this.dir);
                        this.range = 0;
                        this.active = false;
                    }
                } else this.skipMov = false;
            }
        };
        this.tickUpdate = function (dlta) {
            if (this.tickActive) {
                let tmpSpeed = this.speed * dlta;
                if (!this.skipMov) {
                    this.x2 += tmpSpeed * cos(this.dir);
                    this.y2 += tmpSpeed * sin(this.dir);
                    this.r2 -= tmpSpeed;
                    if (this.r2 <= 0) {
                        this.x2 += this.r2 * cos(this.dir);
                        this.y2 += this.r2 * sin(this.dir);
                        tmpSpeed = 1;
                        this.r2 = 0;
                        this.tickActive = false;
                    }
                } else this.skipMov = false;
            }
        };
    }
}

class ProjectileManager {
    constructor(
        Projectile,
        projectiles,
        players,
        ais,
        objectManager,
        items,
        config,
        UWUTILS,
        server
    ) {
        this.projectiles = projectiles;
        this.addProjectile = function (
            x,
            y,
            dir,
            range,
            speed,
            indx,
            owner,
            ignoreObj,
            layer,
            inWindow
        ) {
            let tmpData = items.projectiles[indx];
            let tmpProj;
            for (let i = 0; i < projectiles.length; ++i) {
                if (!projectiles[i].active) {
                    tmpProj = projectiles[i];
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (!tmpProj) {
                tmpProj = new Projectile(
                    players,
                    ais,
                    objectManager,
                    items,
                    config,
                    UWUTILS,
                    server
                );
                tmpProj.sid = projectiles.length;
                this.projectiles.push(tmpProj);
            }
            tmpProj.init(
                indx,
                x,
                y,
                dir,
                speed,
                tmpData.dmg,
                range,
                tmpData.scale,
                owner
            );
            tmpProj.ignoreObj = ignoreObj;
            tmpProj.layer = layer || tmpData.layer;
            tmpProj.inWindow = inWindow;
            tmpProj.src = tmpData.src;
            return tmpProj;
        };
    }
}

class AI {
    constructor(
        sid,
        objectManager,
        players,
        items,
        UWUTILS,
        config,
        scoreCallback,
        server
    ) {
        this.lowHealth = false;
        this.sid = sid;
        this.isAI = true;
        this.nameIndex = UWUTILS.randInt(0, config.cowNames.length - 1);
        // INIT:
        this.init = function (x, y, dir, index, data) {
            this.x = x;
            this.y = y;
            this.displayText = data.name;
            this.startX = data.fixedSpawn ? x : null;
            this.startY = data.fixedSpawn ? y : null;
            this.xVel = 0;
            this.yVel = 0;
            this.lowHealth = false;
            this.zIndex = 0;
            this.dir = dir;
            this.dirPlus = 0;
            this.index = index;
            this.src = data.src;
            if (data.name) this.name = data.name;
            this.weightM = data.weightM;
            this.speed = data.speed;
            this.killScore = data.killScore;
            this.turnSpeed = data.turnSpeed;
            this.scale = data.scale;
            this.maxHealth = data.health;
            this.leapForce = data.leapForce;
            this.health = this.maxHealth;
            this.chargePlayer = data.chargePlayer;
            this.viewRange = data.viewRange;
            this.drop = data.drop;
            this.dmg = data.dmg;
            this.hostile = data.hostile;
            this.dontRun = data.dontRun;
            this.hitRange = data.hitRange;
            this.hitDelay = data.hitDelay;
            this.hitScare = data.hitScare;
            this.spriteMlt = data.spriteMlt;
            this.nameScale = data.nameScale;
            this.colDmg = data.colDmg;
            this.noTrap = data.noTrap;
            this.spawnDelay = data.spawnDelay;
            this.hitWait = 0;
            this.waitCount = 1000;
            this.moveCount = 0;
            this.targetDir = 0;
            this.active = true;
            this.alive = true;
            this.runFrom = null;
            this.chargeTarget = null;
            this.dmgOverTime = {};
        };
        let tmpRatio = 0;
        let animIndex = 0;
        this.animate = function (dlta) {
            if (this.animTime > 0) {
                this.animTime -= dlta;
                if (this.animTime <= 0) {
                    this.animTime = 0;
                    this.dirPlus = 0;
                    tmpRatio = 0;
                    animIndex = 0;
                } else {
                    if (animIndex == 0) {
                        tmpRatio += dlta / (this.animSpeed * config.hitReturnRatio);
                        this.dirPlus = UWUTILS.lerp(
                            0,
                            this.targetAngle,
                            mathMin(1, tmpRatio)
                        );
                        if (tmpRatio >= 1) {
                            tmpRatio = 1;
                            animIndex = 1;
                        }
                    } else {
                        tmpRatio -= dlta / (this.animSpeed * (1 - config.hitReturnRatio));
                        this.dirPlus = UWUTILS.lerp(
                            0,
                            this.targetAngle,
                            mathMax(0, tmpRatio)
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
        };
        // ANIMATION:
        this.startAnim = function () {
            this.animTime = this.animSpeed = 600;
            this.targetAngle = PI * 0.8;
            tmpRatio = 0;
            animIndex = 0;
        };
    }
}
class addCh {
    constructor(x, y, chat, tmpObj) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        this.alpha = 0;
        this.active = true;
        this.alive = false;
        this.chat = chat;
        this.owner = tmpObj;
    }
}
class DeadPlayer {
    constructor(
        x,
        y,
        dir,
        buildIndex,
        weaponIndex,
        weaponVariant,
        skinColor,
        scale,
        name,
        kbDIR
    ) {
        this.x = x;
        this.y = y;
        this.angle2 = kbDIR;
        this.lastDir = dir;
        this.dir = dir + PI;
        this.buildIndex = buildIndex;
        this.weaponIndex = weaponIndex;
        this.weaponVariant = weaponVariant;
        this.skinColor = skinColor;
        this.scale = scale;
        this.visScale = 0;
        this.name = name;
        this.alpha = 1;
        this.active = true;
        this.animate = function (dlta) {
            let d2 = dAng(this.lastDir, this.dir);
            if (d2 > 0.01) this.dir += d2 * 0.05;
            else this.dir = this.lastDir;
            if (this.visScale < this.scale) {
                this.visScale += dlta / (this.scale * 0.5);
                if (this.visScale >= this.scale) this.visScale = this.scale;
            }
            this.alpha -= dlta / 30000;
            if (this.alpha <= 0) {
                this.alpha = 0;
                this.active = false;
            }
        };
    }
}

            this.empAnti = false;

        };
        this.updateListTime = function () {
            this.listTime = toTime(((game.tick - this.guessTime) / 9) | 0);
            if (!this.listTime) return;
            this.listElement.textContent = this.logDetails + (this.listTime == "0s" ? "now" : (this.listTime + " ago"));
        }
        // RESET MOVE DIR:
        this.resetMoveDir = function () {
            this.moveDir = undefined;
        };
        // RESET RESOURCES:
        this.resetResources = function (moofoll) {
            for (let i = 0; i < config.resourceTypes.length; ++i) {
                this[config.resourceTypes[i]] = moofoll ? 100 : 0;
            }
        };
        // ADD ITEM:
        this.getItemType = function (id) {
            let findindx = this.items.findIndex((ids) => ids == id);
            if (findindx != -1) return findindx;
            else return items.checkItem.index(id, this.items);
        };
        // SET DATA:
        this.setData = function (data) {
            if (player && data[1] != player.sid && data[2] != this.name && this.name != undefined) { addMenuChText(null, `${this.name}[${this.sid}] has changed name to ${data[2]}`, 'rgb(247, 166, 91)') }
            this.id = data[0];
            this.sid = data[1];
            this.name = data[2];
            this.x = data[3];
            this.y = data[4];
            this.dir = data[5];
            this.health = data[6];
            this.maxHealth = data[7];
            this.scale = data[8];
            this.skinColor = typeof data[9] !== "number" ? 10 :data[9];
            this.realColor = config.skinColors[typeof data[9] !== "number" ? 10 :data[9]];
            this.displayText = `${this.name} [${this.sid}]`
            if (this.sid == playerSID) {
                const lowerCaseName = this.name.toLowerCase();
                EightySix86 = lowerCaseName == "86" || lowerCaseName.endsWith(" 86") || lowerCaseName.startsWith("86 ") || lowerCaseName.includes(" 86 ") ||
                lowerCaseName == "68" || lowerCaseName.endsWith(" 68") || lowerCaseName.startsWith("68 ") || lowerCaseName.includes(" 68 ") ||
                lowerCaseName == "99" || lowerCaseName.endsWith(" 99") || lowerCaseName.startsWith("99 ") || lowerCaseName.includes(" 99 ") ||
                lowerCaseName == "Eighty Six";
            }
        };
        // UPDATE POISON TICK:
        this.updateTimer = function () {
            this.poisonTimer -= 1;
            if (this.poisonTimer <= 0) {
                this.poisonTick = game.tick;
                this.dot = mathMax(0, this.dot - 1);
                this.poisonTimer = config.serverUpdateRate;
            }
        };
        this.changeHealth = function (amount, doer) {
            this.health += amount;
            return this.health <= 100;
        };
        this.update = function (updateSpeed) {
            if (this.sid == playerSID && !updateSpeed) this.cAngle += 0.1;
            if (this.active) {
                // MOVE:
                let gear = {
                    skin: findID(hats, this.skinIndex),
                    tail: findID(accessories, this.tailIndex),
                };
                let spdMult =
                    (this.buildIndex >= 0 ? 0.5 : 1) *
                    (this.weapon.spdMult || 1) *
                    (gear.skin ? gear.skin.spdMult || 1 : 1) *
                    (gear.tail ? gear.tail.spdMult || 1 : 1) *
                    (this.y2 <= config.snowBiomeTop ? gear.skin && gear.skin.coldM ? 1: config.snowSpeed : 1) * this.slowMult;
                this.maxSpeed = spdMult;
            }
        };
        let tmpRatio = 0;
        let animIndex = 0;
        this.animate = function (dlta) {
            if (this.animTime > 0) {
                this.animTime -= dlta;
                if (this.animTime <= 0) {
                    this.animTime = 0;
                    this.dirPlus = 0;
                    tmpRatio = 0;
                    animIndex = 0;
                } else {
                    if (animIndex == 0) {
                        tmpRatio += dlta / (this.animSpeed * config.hitReturnRatio);
                        this.dirPlus = UWUTILS.lerp(
                            0,
                            this.targetAngle,
                            mathMin(1, tmpRatio)
                        );
                        if (tmpRatio >= 1) {
                            tmpRatio = 1;
                            animIndex = 1;
                        }
                    } else {
                        tmpRatio -= dlta / (this.animSpeed * (1 - config.hitReturnRatio));
                        this.dirPlus = UWUTILS.lerp(
                            0,
                            this.targetAngle,
                            mathMax(0, tmpRatio)
                        );
                    }
                }
            }
        };
        // GATHER ANIMATION:
        this.startAnim = function (didHit, index) {
            this.animTime = this.animSpeed = items.weapons[index].speed;
            this.targetAngle = didHit ? -config.hitAngle : -PI;
            tmpRatio = 0;
            animIndex = 0;
        };
        // CAN SEE:
        this.canSee = function (other) {
            if (!other) return false;
            let dx = abs(other.x - this.x) - other.scale;
            let dy = abs(other.y - this.y) - other.scale;
            return (
                dx <= (config.maxScreenWidth * 0.65) &&
                dy <= (config.maxScreenHeight * 0.65)
            );
        };
        // SHAME SYSTEM:
        this.judgeShame = function () {
            this.lastShameCount = this.shameCount;
            if (this.oldHealth < this.health && !this.cheesed) {
                if (this.hitTime) {
                    this.healTime = game.tick - this.hitTime;
                    let oldHit = this.lastHit;
                    this.lastHit = game.tick;
                    this.hitTime = 0;
                    if (this.healTime < healTimeout) {
                        this.shameCount++;
                        if (game.tick - this.oldHitTime < 3 && (this.guessDangerShame < this.shameCount || this.guessDangerShame >= 7 || game.tick - this.lastGuess > 2700)) {
                            this.guessDangerShame = this.shameCount;
                            this.lastGuess = game.tick;
                        }
                        if (this.healTime == 1) this.hackerPoints.autoheal += 2;
                        if (this.buildIndex != 0) {
                            if (this.qHeld) this.macroPoints.qHold += 4;
                            else {
                                this.qHeld = true;
                                game.tickBase(() => {
                                    this.qHeld = false;
                                }, 10)
                                this.macroPoints.qHold++;
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        if (this.guessDangerShame == this.shameCount && this.oldHealth <= 35) this.dangerShame = this.shameCount;
                        else this.guessDangerShame = this.shameCount;
                        this.shameCount = mathMax(0, this.shameCount - 2);
                        this.macroPoints.qHold -= 0.3;
                        this.qHeld = false;
                        if (this.healTime == healTimeout) this.hackerPoints.autoheal += 3;
                        else if (this.healTime > healTimeout + 1) {
                            this.hackerPoints.autoheal -=
                                this.primaryIndex && this.primary.id == 0
                                    ? 10
                                    : 30;
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else if (this.oldHealth > this.health) {
                this.oldHitTime = this.hitTime;
                this.hitTime = game.tick;
            }
            if (this.cheesed) this.cheesed = false;
        };
        this.addShameTimer = function () {
            this.shameCount = 0;
            this.shameTimer = 30;
        };
        this.updateShameTimer = function () {
            this.shameTimer = mathMax(0, this.shameTimer - ((performance.now() - this.sD) / 1000));
            this.sD = performance.now();
        };
        // CHECK TEAM:
        this.isTeam = function (tmpObj) {
            if (typeof tmpObj == "number") return tmpObj == this.sid || allianceSids.includes(tmpObj.sid);
            else if (this.sid == tmpObj?.sid) return true
            else if (typeof tmpObj != "object" && this.sid != player.sid) {
                let tmp = findPlayerBySID(tmpObj);
                return tmp && (this == tmp || (this.team && tmp.team && this.team === tmp.team));
            } else return tmpObj && (this == tmpObj || (this.team && tmpObj.team && this.team === tmpObj.team));
        };
        // FOR THE PLAYER:
        this.findAllianceBySid = function (sid) {
            let plyrAllies = players.filter2((e) => e.team === this.team);
            return this.team ? plyrAllies.find2((THIS) => THIS === sid) : null;
        };
        this.checkCanInsta = function (nobull) {
            let totally = 0;
            if (inGame && nearHacker.sid) {
                let primary = {
                    weapon: this.primaryIndex,
                    variant: this.primaryVariant,
                    dmg: this.primaryIndex == undefined ? 0 : this.primary.dmg,
                };
                let secondary = {
                    weapon: this.secondaryIndex,
                    variant: this.secondaryVariant,
                    dmg: this.secondaryIndex == undefined ? 0 : this.secondary.Pdmg,
                };
                let bull = this.skins[7] ? 1.5 : 1;
                let pV = primary.variant != undefined ? config.weaponVariants[primary.variant].val : 1;
                if (primary.weapon != undefined && this.primaryReload == 0) totally += primary.dmg * pV * bull * (configs.rev || nearHacker.skinIndex == 6 ? 0.75 : 1);
                if (secondary.weapon != undefined && this.secondaryReload == 0) totally += secondary.dmg * (secondary.weapon == 10 ? config.weaponVariants[secondary.variant].val : 1) * (!configs.rev || nearHacker.skinIndex == 6 ? 0.75 : 1);
                if (this.skins[53] && this.turretReload <= (playerHasHammer ? 0 : game.tickRate) && nearHacker.skinIndex != 22) totally += (!configs.rev || nearHacker.skinIndex == 6 ? 18.75 : 25);
                return totally;
            }
            return 0;
        };
        // UPDATE WEAPON RELOAD:
        this.manageReload = function () {
            if (
                this.weaponReload <= game.tickRate &&
                this.weaponReload > 0
            ) {
                this.antiBull = 1;
            }
            if (this.shooting[53]) {
                this.shooting[53] = 0;
                //this.turretReload = 2500 - game.tickRate;
            } else if (this.turretReload > 0) this.turretReload = mathMax(0, this.turretReload - game.tickRate);
            if (this.gathering || this.shooting[1]) {
                if (this.gathering) {
                    this.gathering = 0;
                    this.weaponReload = this.gatherWeapon.speed * (this.skinIndex == 20 ? 0.78 : 1);
                    if (this.gatherIndex < 9) this.primaryReload = this.weaponReload;
                    else this.secondaryReload = this.weaponReload;
                    if (this.hammerIndex == this.weaponIndex && this.sid == playerSID) this.hammerReload = this.weaponReload;
                    this.attacked = true;
                }
                if (this.shooting[1]) {
                    this.shooting[1] = 0;
                    this.weaponReload = items.weapons[this.shootIndex].speed * (this.skinIndex == 20 ? 0.78 : 1);
                    this.secondaryReload = this.weaponReload;
                    this.attacked = true;
                }
            } else {
                this.attacked = false;
                if (this.buildIndex < 0) {
                    if (this.weaponReload > 0) {
                        // mathMax(0, this.weaponReload - game.tickRate)
                        this.weaponReload = mathMax(0, this.weaponReload - 110);
                        if (this.weaponIndex < 9) this.primaryReload = mathMax(0, this.primaryReload - 110);
                        else this.secondaryReload = mathMax(0, this.secondaryReload - 110);
                        if (this.hammerIndex == this.weaponIndex && this.sid == playerSID) this.hammerReload = this.weaponReload;
                        if (this.sid == player.sid) {
                            if (configs.autoGrind) {
                                checkPlace(player.getItemType(22), (visDir + PI / 4));
                                checkPlace(player.getItemType(22), (visDir - PI / 4));
                            }
                        } else this.clicking = this.weaponReload === 0 && (this.skinIndex == 40 || this.skinIndex == 7);
                    }
                }
            }
        };
    }
}

globalThis.spikeCollision = false;
globalThis.updatePredictPos = function(weaponId, hatId, accId) {
    if (!player.weapon) return;
    const maxSpeed = ((weaponId != null ? weaponId < 9 ? player.primary.spdMult : player.secondary?.spdMult : player.weapon.spdMult) || 1) *
    (hatSpeedMults[hatId != null ? hatId : player.skinIndex]) * (accessorySpeedMults[accId != null ? accId : player.tailIndex]) *
    (1 - (player.y2 <= config.snowBiomeTop && (hatId == null ? player.skinIndex != 15 : hatId != 15)) * 0.25) * player.slowMult * player.riverMult;
    player.predSpeedMult = maxSpeed;
    if (weaponId != null) player.predWeapon = weaponId;
    if (hatId != null) player.predHat = hatId;
    if (accId != null) player.predTail = accId;
    player.velocity = getVelocity(player.x2, player.y2, player.xVel, player.yVel, (player.moveDirection != null && player.moveDirection != undefined), player.moveCos, player.moveSin, maxSpeed);
    player.x3 = player.velocity.x;
    player.y3 = player.velocity.y;
    player.newXVel = player.x3 - player.x2;
    player.newYVel = player.y3 - player.y2;
    player.nextVelocity = getVelocity(player.x3, player.y3, player.newXVel, player.newYVel, (player.moveDirection != null && player.moveDirection != undefined), player.moveCos, player.moveSin, maxSpeed);
    player.x4 = player.nextVelocity.x;
    player.y4 = player.nextVelocity.y
    player.accel = getVelocity(player.x2, player.y2, player.xVel, player.yVel, true, player.moveCos, player.moveSin, maxSpeed);
    if (spikeCollDist <= (hypot(player.x2 - player.accel.x, player.y2 - player.accel.y) * DECELDISTANCEMULT) + scaleFailSafe) {
        lastMoveDir = undefined;
        if (closestCollObj.type === 1 || nearHacker.hitSpike && closestCollObj.type === "plyr") {
            scaleFailSafe = 2;
            breakSpike = false;
            spikeCollision = true;
            player.moveCos = 0;
            player.moveSin = 0;
            player.moveDirection = null;
            player.x5 = player.x4 = player.x3 = player.x2;
            player.y5 = player.y4 = player.y3 = player.y2;
        } else {
            const ranger = 75;
            const canBreak = closestCollObj.dist2 <= (75 + closestCollObj.scale) && closestCollObj.type !== 1;
            const aimer = bestAim(closestCollObj.aim2, ranger);
            if (canBreak) {
            // if (!configs.rubyShame || nearHacker.dist2 > 200 || nearHacker.shameCount < 4) {
                scaleFailSafe = 5;
                breakSpike = true;
                spikeAim = aimer;
                    /*
                    const opposite = obj.aim2 + PI;
                    if (checkDist(calcAccel(plyr, opposite, { maxSpeed: plyr.predictSpeedMult }), obj, 0, 0, ranger + obj.scale)) {
                        packet("9", opposite, 1, "safewalk");
                        lastMove = game.tick;
                        lastMoveDir = opposite
                        return true;
                    }*/
            }
            if (closestCollObj.dmg || closestCollObj.type === 1 || player.hammerReload > 0) {
                player.moveCos = 0;
                player.moveSin = 0;
                player.moveDirection = null;
                spikeCollision = true;
                player.x5 = player.x4 = player.x3 = player.x2;
                player.y5 = player.y4 = player.y3 = player.y2;
            }
        }
    }
}
globalThis.getPredictPos = function(weaponId, hatId, accId) {
    if (!player.weapon) return {x3: Infinity, y3: Infinity};
    const maxSpeed = ((weaponId != null ? weaponId < 9 ? player.primary.spdMult : player.secondary?.spdMult : player.weapon.spdMult) || 1) *
    (hatSpeedMults[hatId != null ? hatId : player.skinIndex]) * (accessorySpeedMults[accId != null ? accId : player.tailIndex]) *
    (player.y2 <= config.snowBiomeTop && (hatId == null ? player.skinIndex != 15 : hatId != 15) ? config.snowSpeed : 1) * player.slowMult * player.riverMult,
    velocity = getVelocity(player.x2, player.y2, player.xVel, player.yVel, (player.moveDirection != null && player.moveDirection != undefined), player.moveCos, player.moveSin, maxSpeed),
    x3 = velocity.x,
    y3 = velocity.y,
    newXVel = x3 - player.x2,
    newYVel = y3 - player.y2,
    nextVelocity = getVelocity(x3, y3, newXVel, newYVel, (player.moveDirection != null && player.moveDirection != undefined), player.moveCos, player.moveSin, maxSpeed),
    x4 = nextVelocity.x,
    y4 = nextVelocity.y;
    return { velocity: velocity, x3: x3, y3: y3, newXVel: newXVel, newYVel: newYVel, nextVelocity: nextVelocity, x4: x4, y4: y4 }
}
globalThis.setPredictPos = function(vel) {
    player.velocity = vel.velocity;
    player.x3 = vel.x3;
    player.y3 = vel.y3;
    player.newXVel = vel.newXVel;
    player.newYVel = vel.newYVel;
    player.nextVelocity = vel.nextVelocity;
    player.x4 = vel.x4;
    player.y4 = vel.y4;
}
if (isDevServer) {
    globalThis.buyEquip = function(id, index, force) {
        if (game.tick - lastHatEquip <= 1) return;
        if (player.alive && inGame) {
            if (player.health <= 5 && id == 7 && index == 0) return;
            if (index == 0) {
                if (id == 0 || player.skins[id]) {
                    if (player.predictHat != id || force) {
                        player.predictHat = id;
                        lastHatEquip = game.tick;
                        packet("c", 0, id, 0, "buyEquip");
                    }
                } else {
                    if (hatLoop && !loopHats.includes(id)) packet("c", 0, loopHats[loopIndex++], 0, "buyËquip");
                    else {
                        let hat = soldDist ? my.combatHat : my.defaultHat;
                        if (!player.skins[hat]) return;
                        player.predictHat = hat;
                        lastHatEquip = game.tick;
                        packet("c", 0, hat, 0, "buyËquip");
                    }
                }
            } else if (index == 1) {
                if (player.tails[id]) {
                    player.predictTail = id;
                    lastHatEquip = game.tick;
                    packet("c", 0, id, 1, "buyËquip");
                } else {
                    const equipTail = player.tails[my.combatTail] ? my.combatTail : 0;
                    player.predictTail = equipTail
                    lastHatEquip = game.tick;
                    packet("c", 0, equipTail, 1, "buyËquip");
                }
            }
        }
    }
} else {
    globalThis.buyEquip = function(id, index, force) {
        if (player.alive && inGame) {
            if (player.health <= 5 && id == 7 && index == 0) return;
            if (index == 0) {
                if (id == 0 || player.skins[id]) {
                    if (player.predictHat != id || force) {
                        player.predictHat = id;
                        packet("c", 0, id, 0, "buyEquip");
                    }
                } else {
                    if (hatLoop && !loopHats.includes(id)) packet("c", 0, loopHats[loopIndex++], 0, "buyËquip");
                    else {
                        let hat = soldDist ? my.combatHat : my.defaultHat;
                        if (!player.skins[hat]) return;
                        player.predictHat = hat;
                        packet("c", 0, hat, 0, "buyËquip");
                    }
                }
            } else if (index == 1) {
                if (player.tails[id]) {
                    player.predictTail = id;
                    packet("c", 0, id, 1, "buyËquip");
                } else {
                    const equipTail = player.tails[my.combatTail] ? my.combatTail : 0;
                    player.predictTail = equipTail
                    packet("c", 0, equipTail, 1, "buyËquip");
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

globalThis.selectWeapon = function(index, isPlace, force) {
    if (((!forcePrimary || index < 9) && (!forceHammer || index == 10) && !noWep || force) && (blocking == 0 || index === 11)) {
        if (!isPlace) player.weaponCode = index;
        packet("z", index, 1, 1, "selectWeapon");
    }
}
globalThis.selectWeapon2 = function(index) {
    if (!globalThis.hacking || (!forcePrimary || index < 9) && (!forceHammer || index == 10) && !noWep && (blocking == 0 || index === 11)) {
        if (globalThis.hacking) packet("z", index, 1, 1, "selectWeapon");
        else {
            packet2("z", index, 1, 1, "selectWeapon");
            hold = [index, 1, 1];
        }
    }
}
globalThis.sendAutoGather = function(ticks) {
    if (ticks === "e") {
        if (!globalThis.hacking) packet2("K", 1, 1, "sendAutoGather");
        else packet("K", 1, 1, "sendAutoGather");
    } else {
        if (my.waitHit < ticks) my.waitHit = ticks;
        if (!autoGathering) packet("K", 1, 1, "sendAutoGather");
    }
}

}
// PLACER:
globalThis.place = function(id, rad, rmd, returnBool) {
    if (my.noAim) rad = blockAim ?? player.d2;
        try {
            if (!config.isSandbox) {
                if (id == 0 && Number(foodEl.textContent) < items.list[player.items[id]].req[1]
                )
                    return false;
                if (
                    id != 0 &&
                    Number(stoneEl.textContent) <
                    items.list[player.items[id]].req[
                    items.list[player.items[id]].req.indexOf("stone") + 1
                    ]
                )
                    return false;
                if (
                    id != 0 &&
                    Number(woodEl.textContent) <
                    items.list[player.items[id]].req[
                    items.list[player.items[id]].req.indexOf("wood") + 1
                    ]
                )
                    return false;
            }
            let wall = id == 2 && !evil ? 1 : id;
            if (wall == undefined) return false;
            let item = items.list[player.items[wall]];
            if (
                inGame/* &&
                (wall == 0 || (wall == 2 ? spikeCount < 15 : wall == 4 ? trapCount < 6 : player.items[wall] && (player.itemCounts[item.group.id] == undefined ? true
                    : player.itemCounts[item.group.id]) <
                    (config.isSandbox
                        ? 299
                        : item.group.limit
                            ? item.group.limit
                            : 99)))*/
            ) {
                if (returnBool) return true;
                    selectToBuild(player.items[wall]);
                    sendAtck2(1, rad);
                    selectWeapon(player.weaponCode, 1);
                    if (id != 0) {
                        const item = items.list[player.items[wall]];
                        const tmpS = 35 + item.scale + (item.placeOffset || 0);
                        const tmpX = player.x2 + tmpS * cos(rad);
                        const tmpY = player.y2 + tmpS * sin(rad);
                        placeVisible.push({
                            id: item.id,
                            x: tmpX,
                            y: tmpY,
                            name: item.name,
                            scale: item.scale,
                            dir: rad + PI * 0.5,
                            owner: { sid: player.sid }
                        });
                }
            }
        } catch (e) {
        }
    if (returnBool) return false;
}
globalThis.placerCheck = function(id, rad) {
    try {
        let wall = id == 2 && !evil ? 1 : id;
        if (wall == undefined) return;
        let item = items.list[player.items[id]];
        let tmpS = 35 + item.scale + (item.placeOffset || 0);
        let tmpX = player.x2 + tmpS * cos(rad);
        let tmpY = player.y2 + tmpS * sin(rad);
        if (
            objectManager.checkCanManualPlace(
                tmpX,
                tmpY,
                item.scale,
                0.6,
                item.id,
                false,
                player
            )
        ) {
            place(wall, rad);
        }
        placeVisible.push({
            id: item.id,
            x: tmpX,
            y: tmpY,
            name: item.name,
            scale: item.scale,
            dir: rad + PI * 0.5,
            owner: { sid: player.sid }
        });
    } catch (e) { }
}
globalThis.wallPlacer = function(rad) {
    let item = items.list[player.items[1]];
    let tmpS = 30 + item.scale;
    let tmpX = player.x2 + tmpS * cos(rad);
    let tmpY = player.y2 + tmpS * sin(rad);
    if (
        objectManager.checkCanManualPlace(
            tmpX,
            tmpY,
            item.scale,
            0.6,
            item.id,
            false,
            player
        )
    ) {
        place(1, rad);
    }
    placeVisible.push({
        id: item.id,
        x: tmpX,
        y: tmpY,
        name: item.name,
        scale: item.scale,
        dir: rad + PI * 0.5,
        owner: { sid: player.sid }
    });
}
globalThis.checkPreplace = function(id, rad, obj, pre) {
    try {
        let wall = id == 2 && !evil ? 1 : id;
        if (wall == undefined) return;
        let item = items.list[player.items[wall]];
        let tmpS = 35 + item.scale + (item.placeOffset || 0);
        let tmpX = player[pre ? "x3" : "x2"] + tmpS * cos(rad);
        let tmpY = player[pre ? "y3" : "y2"] + tmpS * sin(rad);
        if (
            objectManager.checkItemLocationPrePlace(
                tmpX,
                tmpY,
                item.scale,
                0.6,
                item.id,
                false,
                obj
            )
        ) {
            place(wall, rad, 1);
            return true;
        } else {
            placeVisible.push({
                id: item.id,
                x: tmpX,
                y: tmpY,
                name: item.name,
                scale: item.scale,
                dir: rad + PI * 0.5,
                owner: { sid: player.sid }
            });
        }
        return false;
    } catch (e) { }
    return false;
}
globalThis.checkPlace = function(id, rad) {
    try {
        if (id == undefined) return;
        let item = items.list[player.items[id]];
        let tmpS = player.scale + item.scale + (item.placeOffset || 0);
        let tmpX = player.x2 + tmpS * cos(rad);
        let tmpY = player.y2 + tmpS * sin(rad);
        if (objectManager.checkItemLocation(tmpX, tmpY, item.scale, 0.6, item.id, false)) {
            placeObj(item.id, rad);
            return true;
        }
            placeVisible.push({
                id: item.id,
                x: tmpX,
                y: tmpY,
                name: item.name,
                scale: item.scale,
                dir: rad + PI * 0.5,
                owner: { sid: player.sid }
            });
            return false;
    } catch (e) {
    }
    return false;
}
globalThis.checkCanPlace = function(id, rad) {
    try {
        if (id == undefined) return;
        let item = items.list[player.items[id]];
        let tmpS = player.scale + item.scale + (item.placeOffset || 0);
        let tmpX = player.x2 + tmpS * cos(rad);
        let tmpY = player.y2 + tmpS * sin(rad);
        placeVisible.push({
            id: item.id,
            x: tmpX,
            y: tmpY,
            name: item.name,
            scale: item.scale,
            dir: rad + PI * 0.5,
            owner: { sid: player.sid }
        });
        if (objectManager.checkItemLocation(tmpX, tmpY, item.scale, 0.6, item.id, false)) return true;
        else return false;/*{
                let tmpLen = placeVisible.length;
                placeVisible.push({
                    x: tmpX,
                    y: tmpY,
                    name: item.name,
                    scale: item.scale,
                    dir: rad,
                    owner: {sid: player.sid}
                });
                setTimeout(() => {
                    placeVisible.splice(tmpLen, 1);
                }, game.tickRate)
        }*/
    } catch (e) { }
}
globalThis.checkBotPlacement = function(bot, id, rad) {
    try {
        if (id == undefined) return;
        let item = items.list[id];
        let tmpS = 35 + item.scale + (item.placeOffset || 0);
        let tmpX = bot.x2 + tmpS * cos(rad);
        let tmpY = bot.y2 + tmpS * sin(rad);
        return objectManager.checkItemLocation(tmpX, tmpY, item.scale, 0.6, item.id, id == 18, bot);
    } catch (e) { }
}
globalThis.checkPlayerPlacement = function(individual, rad) {
    try {
        return objectManager.checkItemLocation0(individual.x2 + 85 * cos(rad), individual.y2 + 85 * sin(rad), 45, 0.6);
    } catch (e) { }
}
globalThis.checkBlockingItem = function(id, rad) {
    try {
        let wall = id == 2 && !evil ? 1 : id;
        if (wall == undefined) return false;
        let item = items.list[player.items[wall]];
        let tmpS = 35 + item.scale + (item.placeOffset || 0);
        let tmpX = player.x2 + tmpS * cos(rad);
        let tmpY = player.y2 + tmpS * sin(rad);
        return objectManager.checkBlockingItem(
            tmpX,
            tmpY,
            item.scale,
            0.6,
            item.id,
            false,
            player
        );
    } catch (e) { }
}
globalThis.checkCanPrePlace = function(id, rad, obj, loose, pre = true) {
    try {
        let wall = id == 2 && !evil ? 1 : id;
        if (wall == undefined) return false;
        let item = items.list[player.items[wall]];
        let tmpS = 35 + item.scale + (item.placeOffset || 0);
        let tmpX = player[pre ? "x3" : "x2"] + tmpS * cos(rad);
        let tmpY = player[pre ? "y3" : "y2"] + tmpS * sin(rad);
        if (
            objectManager.checkItemLocationPrePlace(
                tmpX,
                tmpY,
                item.scale,
                loose ? 0.5 : 0.6,
                item.id,
                false,
                obj
            )
        ) {
            return true;
        }
    } catch (e) { }
}
globalThis.toTime = function(number) {
    if (number >= 3600) {
        const hours = number % 3600;
        const minutes = (number % 3600) % 60;
        return `${hours}h ${minutes}m ${number % 60}s`;
    } else if (number >= 60) {
        const minutes = (number / 60) | 0;
        return `${minutes}m ${number % 60}s`;
    } else return `${number}s`;
}

}

globalThis.ensureAllianceLogSection = function() {
    const logsPanel = panelElements.clans;
    if (!(logsPanel instanceof HTMLElement)) return null;
    let section = document.getElementById("alliance-log-section");
    if (section instanceof HTMLElement) return section;
    section = document.createElement("div");
    section.id = "alliance-log-section";
    section.style.marginBottom = "18px";
    section.style.padding = "10px";
    section.style.borderRadius = "8px";
    section.style.background = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.06)";
    section.style.border = "1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12)";
    const title = document.createElement("div");
    title.id = "alliance-log-title";
    title.textContent = "Existing Clans";
    title.style.fontSize = "18px";
    title.style.fontWeight = "700";
    title.style.color = "white";
    title.style.marginBottom = "10px";
    section.appendChild(title);
    const list = document.createElement("div");
    list.id = "alliance-log-list";
    list.style.display = "flex";
    list.style.flexDirection = "column";
    list.style.gap = "6px";
    section.appendChild(list);
    logsPanel.insertBefore(section, logsPanel.firstChild);
    return section;
}
globalThis.renderAllianceListInLogs = function() {
    const section = ensureAllianceLogSection();
    if (!(section instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    const list = section.querySelector("#alliance-log-list");
    const title = section.querySelector("#alliance-log-title");
    if (!(list instanceof HTMLElement) || !(title instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
    list.innerHTML = "";
    if (player?.team != null) {
        title.textContent = `Your Clan (${normalizeAllianceName(player.team) || "unknown"})`;
        const leaveBtn = document.createElement("button");
        leaveBtn.textContent = "Leave Clan";
        leaveBtn.style.padding = "8px";
        leaveBtn.style.borderRadius = "6px";
        leaveBtn.style.border = "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2)";
        leaveBtn.style.background = "rgba(255, 80, 80, 0.25)";

        leaveBtn.style.cursor = "pointer";

        };

        leaveBtn.onclick = () => {
            packet("N", "leave");
        };

        list.appendChild(leaveBtn);
        const myTeam = String(player.team);
        const members = (typeof players !== "undefined")
            ? players.filter(p => p && p.team != null && String(p.team) === myTeam)
            : [];
        if (!members.length) {
            const empty = document.createElement("div");
            empty.textContent = "No players in clan.";
            empty.style.color = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.75)";
            empty.style.fontSize = "13px";
            empty.style.marginTop = "8px";
            list.appendChild(empty);
        } else {
            for (let i = 0; i < members.length; i++) {
                const member = members[i];
                const row = document.createElement("div");
                row.style.display = "flex";
                row.style.justifyContent = "space-between";
                row.style.alignItems = "center";
                row.style.padding = "6px 8px";
                row.style.borderRadius = "6px";
                row.style.background = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04)";
                const name = document.createElement("div");
                name.style.color = "white";
                name.style.fontSize = "14px";
                name.textContent = `${i + 1}. ${member.name || member.sid || "unknown"}`;
                row.appendChild(name);
                if (player.isOwner && member.sid !== player.sid) {
                    const kickBtn = document.createElement("button");
                    kickBtn.textContent = "Kick";
                    kickBtn.style.padding = "4px 8px";
                    kickBtn.style.fontSize = "12px";
                    kickBtn.style.borderRadius = "5px";
                    kickBtn.style.border = "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2)";
                    kickBtn.style.background = "rgba(255, 80, 80, 0.2)";
                    kickBtn.style.color = "white";
                    kickBtn.style.cursor = "pointer";
                    kickBtn.onmouseenter = () => {
                        kickBtn.style.background = "rgba(255, 80, 80, 0.35)";
                    };
                    kickBtn.onmouseleave = () => {
                        kickBtn.style.background = "rgba(255, 80, 80, 0.2)";
                    };
                    kickBtn.onclick = () => {
                        const targetId = member.sid;
                        if (!targetId || targetId === player.sid) return;
                        packet("Q", targetId, "kick");
                        for (let i = players.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                            if (players[i].sid === targetId) {
                                players[i].team = null;
                            }
                        }
                        renderAllianceListInLogs();
                    };
                    row.appendChild(kickBtn)
                }
                list.appendChild(row);
            }
        }
        const divider = document.createElement("div");
        divider.style.height = "1px";
        divider.style.margin = "14px 0";
        divider.style.background = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.08)";
        list.appendChild(divider);
        const existingTitle = document.createElement("div");
        existingTitle.textContent = "Existing Clans";
        existingTitle.style.color = "white";
        existingTitle.style.fontSize = "15px";
        existingTitle.style.fontWeight = "700";
        existingTitle.style.marginBottom = "8px";
        list.appendChild(existingTitle);
        if (!alliances.length) {
            const empty = document.createElement("div");
            empty.textContent = "No clans found.";
            empty.style.color = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.75)";
            empty.style.fontSize = "13px";
            list.appendChild(empty);
            return;
        }
        for (let i = 0; i < alliances.length; i++) {
            const clan = alliances[i];
            const row = document.createElement("div");
            row.style.display = "flex";
            row.style.justifyContent = "space-between";
            row.style.alignItems = "center";
            row.style.padding = "6px 8px";
            row.style.borderRadius = "6px";
            row.style.background = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04)";

            name.style.color = "white";

            const { name: leaderName, sid: leaderSid } = resolveAllianceLeader(clan);
            const leaderLabel = document.createElement("div");
            leaderLabel.textContent = `Leader: ${leaderName}${leaderSid && leaderSid !== leaderName ? ` (${leaderSid})` : ""}`;
            leaderLabel.style.color = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.75)";
            leaderLabel.style.fontSize = "13px";
            leaderLabel.style.whiteSpace = "nowrap";

            row.appendChild(name);
            row.appendChild(leaderLabel);
            list.appendChild(row);
        }
        return;
    }
    title.textContent = "Existing Clans";
    const createWrap = document.createElement("div");
    createWrap.style.display = "flex";
    createWrap.style.gap = "6px";
    createWrap.style.marginBottom = "10px";

    input.style.color = "white";
    input.style.fontSize = "13px";
    input.style.outline = "none";
    input.id = "clan-name-input";
    textAreas.push(input.id);
    const createBtn = document.createElement("button");
    createBtn.textContent = "Create";
    createBtn.style.padding = "6px 10px";
    createBtn.style.borderRadius = "6px";
    createBtn.style.border = "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2)";
    createBtn.style.background = "rgba(0, 150, 255, 0.25)";
    createBtn.style.color = "white";
    createBtn.style.cursor = "pointer";
    createBtn.style.fontSize = "13px";
    createBtn.onmouseenter = () => {
        createBtn.style.background = "rgba(0, 150, 255, 0.4)";
    };
    createBtn.onmouseleave = () => {
        createBtn.style.background = "rgba(0, 150, 255, 0.25)";
    };

    createWrap.appendChild(input);

    if (!alliances.length) {
        const empty = document.createElement("div");
        empty.textContent = "No clans found.";
        empty.style.color = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.75)";
        empty.style.fontSize = "13px";
        list.appendChild(empty);

    }
    for (let i = 0; i < alliances.length; i++) {

        row.style.alignItems = "center";
        row.style.gap = "10px";

        const joinBtn = document.createElement("button");

        joinBtn.style.padding = "4px 10px";
        joinBtn.style.fontSize = "12px";
        joinBtn.style.borderRadius = "5px";
        joinBtn.style.border = "1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.2)";
        joinBtn.style.background = "rgba(0, 200, 120, 0.2)";
        joinBtn.style.color = "white";
        joinBtn.style.cursor = "pointer";
        joinBtn.onmouseenter = () => {
            joinBtn.style.background = "rgba(0, 200, 120, 0.35)";
        };
        joinBtn.onmouseleave = () => {
            joinBtn.style.background = "rgba(0, 200, 120, 0.2)";
        };
        joinBtn.onclick = () => {
            const clanId = clan?.sid;
            if (!clanId) return;
            packet2("b", clanId, "join");
        };
        row.appendChild(name);

        const { name: leaderName, sid: leaderSid } = resolveAllianceLeader(clan);
        const leaderLabel = document.createElement("div");
        leaderLabel.textContent = `Leader: ${leaderName}${leaderSid && leaderSid !== leaderName ? ` (${leaderSid})` : ""}`;
        leaderLabel.style.color = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.75)";
        leaderLabel.style.fontSize = "13px";
        leaderLabel.style.whiteSpace = "nowrap";
        row.appendChild(leaderLabel);

        row.appendChild(joinBtn);
        list.appendChild(row);
    }
}
globalThis.drawPlayer = function(plyr, parent) {
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    canvas.height = 130;
    canvas.width = 90;
    parent.appendChild(canvas);
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d")
    ctx.strokeStyle = outlineColor;
    ctx.translate(40, 40)
    ctx.save()
    renderPlayer(plyr, ctx, 1);
    ctx.restore()
    ctx.font = "15px Hammersmith One";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    ctx.lineJoin = "round";
    ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
    ctx.strokeStyle = outlineColor;
    ctx.strokeText(plyr.name, 0, -30)
    ctx.fillText(plyr.name, 0, -30)
}

    plyr.logDetails = `Team: ${player.team ? `"${plyr.team}"` : "none"}, SID: ${plyr.sid}, Estimated Location: ${plyr.guessLocation ?? "unknown"}, Move Direction: ${plyr.movDir}, Estimated `;
    log.style.color = "white";
    log.style.fontSize = "20px";
    log.style.display = "flex";
    log.style.left = "20%";

}

// HEALING:
globalThis.soldierMult = function() {
    return player.predictHat == 6 ? 0.75 : 1;
}
//checkKB(player,getDir(_.np.real,player.np.real),dist(player,_.kbpot.fpriTKB),true,0)
// objs: trap,spike (binary, 1 = 01 = only spike, 2 = 10 = only trap, 3 = 11 = both)
globalThis.checkKB = function(target, angle, distance, objs = 1, z = nearObjects, estimate, x, y) {
    const isPlayer = target.sid == player.sid;
    let dmg = 0, closestDist = 9999;//closestObj, closestScale;
    for (let i = 0;i < z.length;i++) {
        const e = z[i];
        e.isCandidate = !!((objs & 1) > 0 && (e.isCactus || e.dmg && isPlayer != e.inClan) || e.trap && objs & 2 && isPlayer != e.inClan);
        //if (e.dmg) consolelog(e.isCandidate, isPlayer, (objs & 1) > 0, e.dmg ? (e.owner.sid == player.sid) : "not a spike", e.dmg ? isPlayer != (e.owner.sid == player.sid) : "not a spike");
    }
    if (!z.find2((e) => e.isCandidate)) return false
    x = target.x2
    y = target.y2;
    /*
    if (vel) {
        x = target.x3
        y = target.y3
    } else {
        x = target.x2
        y = target.y2;
    }*/
    const Z = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < z.length; i++) {
        const obj = z[i];
        const scale = (estimate ? 60 : (isPlayer ? 40 : 35)) + (obj.isCactus ? obj.scale * .55 : obj.scale);
        const dist1 = isPlayer ? obj.dist2 : cdf(target, obj)//getDistance(nEnemy.x2, nEnemy.y2, obj.x, obj.y);
        if (dist1 - closestDist > 10 || dist1 - scale > (isPlayer ? distance - 10 - estimate * 5 : distance)) continue;
        const point1 = simulate(target, x, y, target.newXVel + cos(angle) * dist1, target.newYVel + sin(angle) * dist1);
        //const dist2 = getDistance(point1.x, point1.y, obj.x, obj.y);
        if (point1.collides.find2((e) => e.sid == obj.sid) || squareCdf(point1, obj) < scale*scale) {
            /*
            closestObj = obj;
            closestObjScale = scale;*/
            if (abs(dist1 - closestDist) <= 10) {
                if (!obj.isCandidate) continue;
                Z.unshift(obj);
                dmg += obj.dmg ?? 35;
            } else {
                if (!obj.isCandidate) {
                    Z.length = 0;
                    dmg = 0;
                    continue;
                }
                Z.length = 0;
                Z.unshift(obj);
                dmg = obj.dmg ?? 35;
            }
            if (dist1 < closestDist) closestDist = dist1;
        }
    }
    //const p00 = calcPoint(x, y, angle, closestDist - closestObjScale);
    if (Z.length) return { /*x: p00.x, y: p00.y, */objs: Z, spikeCount: Z.length, dmg: dmg };
    return false;
}
globalThis.checkKB2 = function(target, angle, distance, objs = 1, z = nearObjects, estimate, x, y) {
    let dmg = 0, closestDist = 9999;//closestObj, closestScale;
    for (let i = 0;i < z.length;i++) {
        const e = z[i];
        e.isCandidate = !!((objs & 1) > 0 && (e.isCactus || e.dmg && e.inClan));
        //if (e.dmg) consolelog(e.isCandidate, isPlayer, (objs & 1) > 0, e.dmg ? (e.owner.sid == player.sid) : "not a spike", e.dmg ? isPlayer != (e.owner.sid == player.sid) : "not a spike");
    }
    if (!z.find2((e) => e.isCandidate)) return false
    x = target.x3
    y = target.y3;
    /*
    if (vel) {
        x = target.x3
        y = target.y3
    } else {
        x = target.x2
        y = target.y2;
    }*/
    const Z = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < z.length; i++) {
        const obj = z[i];
        const scale = (estimate ? 50 : 35) + (obj.isCactus ? obj.scale * .55 : obj.scale);
        const dist1 = cdf(target, obj)//getDistance(nEnemy.x2, nEnemy.y2, obj.x, obj.y);
        if (dist1 - closestDist > 6.7 || dist1 - scale > distance) continue;
        const point1 = simulate(null, target.x3, target.y3, target.x4 - target.x3 + cos(angle) * dist1, target.y4 - target.y3 + sin(angle) * dist1);
        //const dist2 = getDistance(point1.x, point1.y, obj.x, obj.y);
        if (point1.collides.find2((e) => e.sid == obj.sid) || squareCdf(point1, obj) < scale*scale) {
            /*
            closestObj = obj;
            closestObjScale = scale;*/
            if (abs(dist1 - closestDist) <= 6.7) {
                if (!obj.isCandidate) continue;
                Z.unshift(obj);
                dmg += obj.dmg ?? 35;
            } else {
                if (!obj.isCandidate) {
                    Z.length = 0;
                    dmg = 0;
                    continue;
                }
                Z.length = 0;
                Z.unshift(obj);
                dmg = obj.dmg ?? 35;
            }
            if (dist1 < closestDist) closestDist = dist1;
        }
    }
    //const p00 = calcPoint(x, y, angle, closestDist - closestObjScale);
    return dmg;
}
globalThis.healthBased = function() {
    if (player.health == 100) return 0;
    if (player.skinIndex != 45) {
        return ceil((100 - player.health) / foodType.healing);
    }
    return 0;
}
globalThis.getAttacker = function(damaged, tmpObj) {
    if (damaged <= 0) return;
    let enemies = nearPlayers.filter2((e) => tmpObj.team == undefined ? tmpObj.sid != e.sid : tmpObj.team != e.team);
    let attackers = enemies.filter2((tmp) => {
        const wpn = tmp.weapon;
        let inRange = false;
        let tmpDist = UWUTILS.getDist(tmpObj, tmp, 2, 2);
        let rangedWeapon = [9, 12, 13, 15].includes(tmp.weaponIndex);
        if (rangedWeapon || tmpDist <= wpn.range + 120 || UWUTILS.getDist(tmpObj, tmp, 3, 3) <= wpn.range + 120)
            inRange = true;
        let damagePossible = false;
        let dmg2 = rangedWeapon ? wpn.Pdmg : (wpn.dmg * tmp.weaponVariant);
        let damage = dmg2;
        if (tmpObj.skinIndex == 6) damage *= 0.75;
        if (!rangedWeapon) {
            if (tmp.skinIndex == 7) damage *= 1.5;
            else if (tmp.skinIndex == 55) damage *= 1.2;
            if (tmp.tailIndex == 11) damage *= 0.2;
        }
        if (abs(damage - damaged) < 0.001) damagePossible = true;
        let RuLe = {
            one: inRange,
            two: damagePossible || tmpObj.skinIndex == 45,
            three: abs(tmp.d2 - UWUTILS.getDirect(tmpObj, tmp, 2, 2)) <= (rangedWeapon ? PI / 1.5 / ((UWUTILS.getDist(tmpObj, tmp, 2, 2) + 50) / 100) : (config.gatherAngle * 2)),
            four: tmp.attackingWeapon != null
        };
        if ((tmp.weaponIndex != 0 || tmp.dist2 > 140) && (damaged == 18.75 || damaged == 25)) return tmp.turretGear && tmp.dist2 <= 300;
        return RuLe.one && RuLe.two && RuLe.three && RuLe.four;
    });
    if (attackers.length == 0 && nears.length && tmpObj == player) {
        attackers = enemies.filter2((tmp) => {
            let inRange = false;
            let tmpDist = UWUTILS.getDist(tmpObj, tmp, 2, 2);
            let rangedWeapon =
                tmp.weaponIndex > 8 && tmp.weapon.Pdmg > 20;
            if (
                rangedWeapon ||
                tmpDist <= tmp.weapon.range + 120 ||
                UWUTILS.getDist(tmpObj, tmp, 3, 3) <=
                tmp.weapon.range + 120
            )
                inRange = true;
            let damagePossible = false;
            let dmg2;
            if (rangedWeapon) dmg2 = tmp.weapon.Pdmg;
            else dmg2 = tmp.weapon.dmg * (tmp.weaponVariantDmg);
            let damage = dmg2;
            if (tmpObj.skinIndex == 6) damage *= 0.75;
            if (tmp.skinIndex == 7) damage *= 1.5;
            else if (tmp.skinIndex == 55) damage *= 1.2;
            if (tmp.tailIndex == 11) damage *= 0.2;
            if (abs(damage - damaged) < 0.001) damagePossible = true;
            let RuLe = {
                one: inRange,
                two: damagePossible,
                three:
                    abs(tmp.d2 - UWUTILS.getDirect(tmpObj, tmp, 2, 2)) <= (rangedWeapon ? PI / 1.5 / ((UWUTILS.getDist(tmpObj, tmp, 2, 2) + 50) / 100) : (config.gatherAngle * 2)),
                four: tmp.attacked,
            };
            if ((tmp.weaponIndex != 0 || tmp.dist2 > 140) && (damaged == 18.75 || damaged == 25)) return tmp.lastSkinIndexes.slice(-3).includes(53) && tmp.dist2 <= 300;
            return RuLe.one && RuLe.two && RuLe.four;
        });
        return attackers.length > 0 ? attackers : undefined;
    } else return attackers.length > 0 ? attackers : undefined;
}
globalThis.healer = function(force) {
    if (player.food < foodTypeReq || !autoHeal && !force) return;
    if (player.skinIndex == 56 && player.health < 100) buyEquip(6, 0);
    for (let i = 0; i < healthBased(); i++) {
        selectToBuild(foodIndex);
        sendAtck2(1, lastDir);
    }
}

globalThis.findAllianceBySid = function(sid) {
    return player.team ? allianceSids.find2((THIS) => THIS === sid) : null;
}
globalThis.allianceSids = [];
globalThis.checkIsTeam = function(sid) {
    return botSIDs.includes(sid) || sid === playerSID || allianceSids.find2((THIS) => THIS === sid) != undefined;
}
globalThis.applCxC = function(value) {
    if (player.health == 100) return 0;
    if (player.skinIndex != 45 && player.skinIndex != 56) return ceil(value / foodType.healing);
    return 0;
}
globalThis.calcDmg = function(value) {
    return value * player.skinIndex == 6 ? 0.75 : 1;
}
globalThis.matchedArray = [];
globalThis.restArray = [];
globalThis.partitionzion = function(arr, predicate) {
    matchedArray.length = 0;
    restArray.length = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
        const item = arr[i];
        (predicate(item) ? matchedArray : restArray).push(item);
    }
    return [matchedArray, restArray]; // or { matched, rest }
}
globalThis.biomeGear = function(ignoreStandStill) {
    let asda = getMoveDir();
    let antiBullTrue = configs.antiBull;
    if (player.tailIndex != (!ignoreStandStill && near && near.dist3 <= 280 ? player.tails[antiBullTrue ? 21 : 19] ? antiBullTrue ? 21 : 19 : 0
            : (ignoreStandStill || near.dist3 >= 320) && player.tails[11] ? 11 : 0)) {
        buyEquip(
            ignoreStandStill && (near.dist3 <= 280 || noTail)
                ? antiBullTrue
                    ? 21
                    : 19
                : ignoreStandStill || near.dist3 >= 320
                    ? 11
                    : antiBullTrue
                        ? 21
                        : 19,
            1
        );
    } else if (asda == undefined && !ignoreStandStill) buyEquip(hatLoop ? loopHats[loopIndex++] : my.combatHat, 0);
    else {
        if (
            player.y2 >= 7200 - config.riverWidth * 0.5 &&
            player.y2 <= 7200 + config.riverWidth * 0.5
        ) {
            buyEquip(
                player.skins[31] ? 31 : hatLoop ? loopHats[loopIndex++] : my.combatHat,
                0
            );
        } else {
            if (player.y2 <= config.snowBiomeTop) buyEquip(hatLoop ? loopHats[loopIndex++] : 15, 0);
            else {
                buyEquip(
                    player.skins[6] && enemy && !ignoreStandStill && soldDist
                        ? 6
                        : hatLoop
                            ? loopHats[loopIndex++]
                            : player.skins[12]
                                ? 12
                                : my.combatHat,
                    0
                );
            }
        }
    }
}
globalThis.biomeGearHat = function(ignoreStandStill) {
    let asda = getMoveDir();
    if (asda == undefined) return hatLoop ? loopHats[loopIndex++] : 6;
    else {
        if (
            player.y2 >= 7200 - config.riverWidth * 0.5 &&
            player.y2 <= 7200 + config.riverWidth * 0.5
        ) {
            return player.skins[31]
                ? 31
                : hatLoop
                    ? loopHats[loopIndex++]
                    : 0;
        } else {
            if (player.y2 <= config.snowBiomeTop) {
                return player.skins[15] ? 15 : hatLoop
                        ? loopHats[loopIndex++]
                        : 0;
            } else {
                return hatLoop
                    ? loopHats[loopIndex++]
                    : player.skins[12]
                        ? 12
                        : 0;
            }
        }
    }
}
