// === MAIN MOD HAT FUNCTION ===
function hatFc() {
                // HAT
                currentHat = 6;
                if (isBoughtHat(48, 0)) {
                    currentHat = 48;
                }

                if (isBoughtHat(12, 0)) {
                    currentHat = 12;
                }

                if(isBoughtHat(6,0) && nearestEnemy && UTILS.getDistance(nearestEnemy.x2, nearestEnemy.y2, myPlayer.x2, myPlayer.y2) < 400 && window.vars.safeSoldier) {
                    currentHat = 6;
                }

                if (myPlayer.y < 2400 && isBoughtHat(15, 0)) {
                    currentHat = 15;
                }

                if ((myPlayer.y > 6838 && myPlayer.y < 7562) && isBoughtHat(31, 0)) {
                    currentHat = 31;
                }

                if (autogathering && isBoughtHat(6, 0)) {
                    currentHat = currentHat;
                }

                if (isBoughtHat(7, 0)) {
                    if (shouldResetShame) {
                        currentHat = 7;
                    }
                }

                if (((imTrapped && spikeDmgCount > 0) || spikeTickAnti) && isBoughtHat(6, 0)) {
                    currentHat = 6;
                }

                if (autogathering) {
                    if (predictWeapon < 9) {
                        if (primaryReload[myPlayer.sid] == 1) {
                            if (autoBreak) {
                                if (antiPush) {
                                    if (isBoughtHat(7, 0)) {
                                        currentHat = 7;
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    if (isBoughtHat(40, 0)) {
                                        currentHat = 40;
                                    }
                                }
                            } else {
                                if (predictWeapon < 8 && nearestEnemy && UTILS.getDistance(myPlayer.xVel, myPlayer.yVel, nearestEnemy.xVel, nearestEnemy.yVel) <= (35 * 1.8 + items.weapons[predictWeapon].range)) {
                                    if (isBoughtHat(7, 0)) {
                                        currentHat = 7;
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    if (isBoughtHat(40, 0)) {
                                        currentHat = 40;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    } else if (predictWeapon != 11) {
                        if (secondaryReload[myPlayer.sid] == 1) {
                            if (predictWeapon == 10) {
                                if (isBoughtHat(40, 0)) {
                                    currentHat = 40;
                                }
                            } else {
                                if (isBoughtHat(20, 0)) {
                                    currentHat = 20;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // ACC
                let currentAcc = 13;
                if (isBoughtHat(15, 1)) {
                    currentAcc = 15;
                }

                if (nearestEnemy && UTILS.getDistance(myPlayer.x2, myPlayer.y2, nearestEnemy.x2, nearestEnemy.y2) <= 400 && myPlayer.weapons[0] != 7 && myPlayer.weapons[0] != 6) {

                    currentAcc = 19;

                    if (isBoughtHat(19, 1)) {

                        currentAcc = 19;

                    }

                }

                if (nearestTrap && (myPlayer.weapons[0] == 7 || myPlayer.weapons[0] == 6)) {
                    currentAcc = currentAcc;
                    if (isBoughtHat(15, 1)) {
                        currentAcc = 15;
                    }
                }

                if (autogathering && predictWeapon < 9) {
                    currentAcc = currentAcc;
                    if (isBoughtHat(15, 1)) {
                        currentAcc = 15;
                    }
                }

                if (myPlayer.weapons[0] == 8 && isBoughtHat(11, 1)) {
                    currentAcc = 11;
                }

                // HAT & ACC

                if ((insta.primaryturret || insta.turret) && isBoughtHat(53, 0) && turretReload[myPlayer.sid] == 1) {
                    currentHat = 53;
                }

                if (insta.primary && isBoughtHat(7, 0)) {
                    currentHat = 7;
                }

                if (insta.secondary && isBoughtHat(40, 0)) {
                    currentHat = 40;
                }


                if ((ePress || autoaim || leftClick) && isBadTail()) {
                    currentHat = myPlayer.skinIndex;

                    currentAcc = 0;
                    if (isBoughtHat(19, 1)) {
                        currentAcc = 19;
                    }
                }

                if (isBoughtHat(6, 0)) {
                    if (soldierAnti) {
                        currentHat = 6;
                    }
                }

                // EQUIP
                hat(currentHat);
                acc(currentAcc);
            }

// === END HAT FUNCTION ===

// === MAIN MOD HAT LOGIC INTEGRATED ===
globalThis.hatSwitcher = function(returnHat) {
    // Call Main mod's hat logic
    try {
        hatFc();
        
        // Return the current hat if requested
        if (returnHat) {
            return currentHat;
        }
        
        // The hat() and acc() functions are called inside hatFc
        return null;
    } catch (e) {
        console.log("Hat switcher error:", e);
        return null;
    }
}


async function receiveChat(sid, message) {
    let tmpPlayer = findPlayerBySID(sid);
    if (!tmpPlayer) return;
    tmpPlayer.lastChat = performance.now();
    /*if (message == "haxs losers be legit" && tmpPlayer.name == "8:0:0") {
        packet("6", "Listen to 8:0:0's words.", "xd")
    }*/
    if (mutedSIDs.includes(sid)) return;
    const originalMessage = message;
    message = await translateMessageToEnglish(message);
    if (configs.dekuAI && sid != playerSID && tmpPlayer.name != "Moh:io") getDekuResponse(message)
    if (autoChat != null && !tmpPlayer.usingWhiteout && tmpPlayer.hacking && message == autoChat) tmpPlayer.happymod = true;
    else if (tmpPlayer.hacking && message.toLowerCase() == "By Minus v2") tmpPlayer.minusClient = true;
    else if (tmpPlayer.hacking && autoChat == "Happymod V4" && message == "maybe onetick mod v2") tmpPlayer.onetickMod = true;

// RENDER LEAF:
globalThis.renderLeaf = function(x, y, l, r, ctxt) {
    let endX = x + l * cos(r);
    let endY = y + l * sin(r);
    let width = l * 0.4;
    ctxt.moveTo(x, y);
    ctxt.beginPath();
    ctxt.quadraticCurveTo(
        (x + endX) * 0.5 + width * cos(r + PI * 0.5),
        (y + endY) * 0.5 + width * sin(r + PI * 0.5),
        endX,
        endY
    );
    ctxt.quadraticCurveTo(
        (x + endX) * 0.5 - width * cos(r + PI * 0.5),
        (y + endY) * 0.5 - width * sin(r + PI * 0.5),
        x,
        y
    );
    ctxt.closePath();
    ctxt.fill();
    ctxt.stroke();
}
// RENDER CIRCLE:
globalThis.renderCircle = function(x, y, scale, tmpContext, dontStroke, dontFill) {
    tmpContext = tmpContext || mainContext;
    tmpContext.beginPath();
    tmpContext.arc(x, y, scale, 0, PI2);
    if (!dontFill) tmpContext.fill();
    if (!dontStroke) tmpContext.stroke();
}
globalThis.renderHealthCircle = function(x, y, scale, tmpContext, dontStroke, dontFill) {
    tmpContext = tmpContext || mainContext;
    tmpContext.beginPath();
    tmpContext.arc(x, y, scale, 0, PI2);
    if (!dontFill) tmpContext.fill();
    if (!dontStroke) tmpContext.stroke();
}
// RENDER STAR SHAPE:
globalThis.renderStar = function(ctxt, spikes, outer, inner) {
    let rot = (PI * 0.5) * 3;
    let x, y;
    let step = PI / spikes;
    ctxt.beginPath();
    ctxt.moveTo(0, -outer - .0000250);
    for (let i = 0; i < spikes; i++) {
        x = cos(rot) * outer;
        y = sin(rot) * outer;
        ctxt.lineTo(x, y);
        rot += step;
        x = cos(rot) * inner;
        y = sin(rot) * inner;
        ctxt.lineTo(x, y);
        rot += step;
    }
    ctxt.lineTo(0, -outer);
    ctxt.closePath();
}
globalThis.renderHealthStar = function(ctxt, spikes, outer, inner) {
    let rot = (PI * 0.5) * 3;
    let x, y;
    let step = PI / spikes;
    ctxt.beginPath();
    ctxt.moveTo(0, -outer);
    for (let i = 0; i < spikes; i++) {
        x = cos(rot) * outer;
        y = sin(rot) * outer;
        ctxt.lineTo(x, y);
        rot += step;
        x = cos(rot) * inner;
        y = sin(rot) * inner;
        ctxt.lineTo(x, y);
        rot += step;
    }
    ctxt.lineTo(0, -outer);
    ctxt.closePath();
}
// RENDER RECTANGLE:
globalThis.renderRect = function(x, y, w, h, ctxt, dontStroke, dontFill) {
    if (!dontFill) ctxt.fillRect(x - w * 0.5, y - h * 0.5, w, h);
    if (!dontStroke) ctxt.strokeRect(x - w * 0.5, y - h * 0.5, w, h);
}
globalThis.renderHealthRect = function(x, y, w, h, ctxt, dontStroke, dontFill) {
    if (!dontFill) ctxt.fillRect(x - w * 0.5, y - h * 0.5, w, h);
    if (!dontStroke) ctxt.strokeRect(x - w * 0.5, y - h * 0.5, w, h);
}
// RENDER RECTCIRCLE:
globalThis.renderRectCircle = function(x, y, s, sw, seg, ctxt, dontStroke, dontFill) {
    ctxt.save();
    ctxt.translate(x, y);
    seg = ceil(seg * 0.5);
    for (let i = 0; i < seg; i++) {
        renderRect(0, 0, s * 2, sw, ctxt, dontStroke, dontFill);
        ctxt.rotate(PI / seg);
    }
    ctxt.restore();
}
// RENDER BLOB:
globalThis.renderBlob = function(ctxt, spikes, outer, inner) {
    let rot = (PI * 0.5) * 3;
    let x, y;
    let step = PI / spikes;
    let tmpOuter;
    ctxt.beginPath();
    ctxt.moveTo(0, -inner);
    for (let i = 0; i < spikes; i++) {
        tmpOuter = UWUTILS.randInt(outer + 0.9, outer * 1.2);
        ctxt.quadraticCurveTo(
            cos(rot + step) * tmpOuter,
            sin(rot + step) * tmpOuter,
            cos(rot + step * 2) * inner,
            sin(rot + step * 2) * inner
        );
        rot += step * 2;
    }
    ctxt.lineTo(0, -inner);
    ctxt.closePath();
}
// RENDER TRIANGLE:
globalThis.renderTriangle = function(s, ctx) {
    ctx = ctx || mainContext;
    let h = s * 0.86602540378;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(0, -h * 0.5);
    ctx.lineTo(-s * 0.5, h * 0.5);
    ctx.lineTo(s * 0.5, h * 0.5);
    ctx.lineTo(0, -h * 0.5);
    ctx.fill();
    ctx.closePath();
}

// RENDER PLAYERS:
globalThis.renderDeadPlayers = function(xOffset, yOffset) {
    mainContext.strokeStyle = outlineColor;
    const currentTime = performance.now();
    for (let opasdf = 0;opasdf < deadPlayers.length;opasdf++) {
        const dead = deadPlayers[opasdf];
        if (!dead.startTime) {
            dead.startTime = currentTime;
            dead.angle = (dead.dir + PI);
            dead.radius = 1;
        }
        const timeElapsed = currentTime - dead.startTime;
        dead.alpha = mathMax(0, 1 - timeElapsed * 0.003);
        dead.animate(delta);
        mainContext.globalAlpha = dead.alpha;
        mainContext.strokeStyle = outlineColor;
        mainContext.save();
        mainContext.translate(dead.x - xOffset, dead.y - yOffset);
        dead.radius -= 0.01;
        dead.angle += 0.04 * dead.alpha;
        const x = dead.radius * cos(dead.angle2);
        const y = dead.radius * sin(dead.angle2);
        dead.x += x;
        dead.y += y;
        mainContext.rotate(dead.angle);
        renderDeadPlayer(dead, mainContext);
        mainContext.restore();
        if (timeElapsed >= 3000) {
            dead.active = false;
            dead.startTime = null;
        }
    }
}
// RENDER PLAYERS:
globalThis.renderPlayers = function(xOffset, yOffset) {
    //const startTime = performance.now();
    const ctx = mainContext;
    const showDir = configs.showDir;
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    for (let i = 0, len = players.length; i < len; i++) {
        const obj = players[i];
        obj.animate(delta);
        if (!(obj.visible || obj.skinIndex == 56 || (inGame && obj.sid === player.sid))) continue;
        const isLocal = obj.sid === player.sid;
        const alpha = (!obj.visible) ? 0.5 : botSIDs.includes(obj.sid) ? 0.26666 : EightySix86 ? 0.75 : obj.opacity;
        obj.skinRot += 0.001 * delta;
        const dir = (obj === player && !globalThis.hacking || !showDir && obj === player && (!EightySix86 || realDir === 0)) ? getVisualDir() : (obj.dir || 0);
        const x = obj.x - xOffset;
        const y = obj.y - yOffset;
        const rot = dir + obj.dirPlus;
        const base = getPlayerBase(obj);
        ctx.save();
        ctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
        ctx.translate(x, y);
        ctx.rotate(rot);
        ctx.drawImage(
            base,
            -base.width * 0.5,
            -base.height * 0.5
        );
        ctx.restore();
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    //renderPlayersTime = performance.now() - startTime;
}
// RENDER DEAD PLAYER:
globalThis.renderDeadPlayer = function(obj, ctxt) {
    ctxt = ctxt || mainContext;
    ctxt.lineWidth = outlineWidth;
    ctxt.lineJoin = "miter";
    let handAngle = (PI / 4) * (obj.weapon.armS || 1);
    let oHandAngle =
        obj.buildIndex < 0 ? obj.weapon.hndS || 1 : 1;
    let oHandDist =
        obj.buildIndex < 0 ? obj.weapon.hndD || 1 : 1;
    // TAIL/CAPE:
    renderTail2(obj.tailIndex, ctxt, obj);
    // WEAPON BELLOW HANDS:
    if (obj.buildIndex < 0 && !obj.weapon.aboveHand) {
        renderTool(obj.weapon, sortVariantSrc(obj.weaponVariant), 35, 0, ctxt);
        if (obj.weapon.projectile != undefined &&!obj.weapon.hideProjectile) {
            renderProjectile(
                obj.scale,
                0,
                items.projectiles[obj.weapon.projectile],
                mainContext
            );
        }
    }
    // HANDS:
    ctxt.fillStyle = "#ececec";
    renderCircle(
        obj.scale * cos(handAngle),
        obj.scale * sin(handAngle),
        14
    );
    renderCircle(
        obj.scale * oHandDist * cos(-handAngle * oHandAngle),
        obj.scale * oHandDist * sin(-handAngle * oHandAngle),
        14
    );
    // WEAPON ABOVE HANDS:
    if (obj.buildIndex < 0 && obj.weapon.aboveHand) {
        renderTool(
            obj.weapon,
            sortVariantSrc(obj.weaponVariant),
            35,
            0,
            ctxt
        );
        if (
            obj.weapon.projectile != undefined &&
            !obj.weapon.hideProjectile
        ) {
            renderProjectile(obj.scale, 0, items.projectiles[obj.weapon.projectile], mainContext);
        }
    }
    // BUILD ITEM:
    if (obj.buildIndex >= 0) {
        var tmpSprite = getItemSprite(items.list[obj.buildIndex]);
        ctxt.drawImage(
            tmpSprite,
            obj.scale - items.list[obj.buildIndex].holdOffset,
            -tmpSprite.width * 0.5
        );
    }
    // BODY:
    renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, ctxt);
    // SKIN
    if (obj.skinIndex > 0) renderSkin(obj.skinIndex, ctxt, null, obj);
}
globalThis.playerBaseCache = new Map();
globalThis.handCache = new Map();
globalThis.getHandKey = function(obj, state, isIcon) {
    return obj.skinColor + "|" + state + "|" + (isIcon ? 1 : 0);
}
globalThis.getPlayerBaseKey = function(obj) {
    return `${obj.skinIndex}|${obj.tailIndex}|${(obj.buildIndex >= 0 ? `0|${obj.buildIndex}|${((obj.buildIndex < 6 || obj.buildIndex != 15 && obj.buildIndex > 9) || checkIsTeam(obj.sid)) ? "1" : "0"}` : (`${obj.weaponIndex}|${obj.weaponVariant}`))}|${obj.skinColor}|${obj.sid === player.sid && EightySix86 ? "1" : "0"}`;
}
globalThis.buildHandSprite = function(obj, state, scale, isIcon) {
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    const size = scale * 3;
    canvas.width = size;
    canvas.height = size;
    ctx.translate(size * 0.5, size * 0.5);
    ctx.fillStyle = config.skinColors[obj.skinColor];
    const s = state;
    // STATE 0 (0.65)
    if (s === 0) {
        renderCircle(scale * 0.8, scale * 0.3, isIcon ? 7 : 14, ctx);
        renderCircle(scale * 0.6, scale * -0.2, isIcon ? 7 : 14, ctx);
    }
    // STATE 1 (0.75)
    else if (s === 1) {
        renderCircle(scale * 0.5, scale * 0.4, isIcon ? 7 : 14, ctx);
        renderCircle(scale * 0.3, scale * -0.3, isIcon ? 7 : 14, ctx);
    }
    // STATE 2 (1.0)
    else {
        renderCircle(scale * 0.2, scale * 0.6, isIcon ? 7 : 14, ctx);
        renderCircle(scale * 0.1, scale * -0.4, isIcon ? 7 : 14, ctx);
    }
    return canvas;
}
globalThis.buildPlayerBase = function(obj, tailIndex = obj.tailIndex, weapon = obj.weapon, weaponVariant = obj.weaponVariant, skinIndex = obj.skinIndex, EightySix = EightySix86) {
    const scale = 35; // 35
    const color = obj.sid === player.sid && EightySix ? "rgb(199, 226, 255)" : config.skinColors[obj.skinColor];
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    if (weapon != -1) {
        if (obj.buildIndex < 0) {
            const weapon2 = items.weapons[weapon.id];
            const weaponW = weapon.id == 7 ? 5 : (weapon2?.width || 0);
            const weaponW2 = weapon.id == 15 ? 150 : weapon.id == 7 ? 40 : 0;
            // safe bounds around true center
            const pad = 50;
            const left = scale + pad;
            const top = scale + pad;
            const right = scale + pad + weaponW2;
            const bottom = scale + weaponW + pad;
            canvas.width = left + right;
            canvas.height = top + bottom;
        } else {
            const pad = 50;
            const left = scale + pad;
            const top = scale + pad;
            const right = scale + pad + 150;
            const bottom = scale + pad;
            canvas.width = left + right;
            canvas.height = top + bottom;
        }
    } else {
        const pad = 50;
        const left = scale + pad;
        const top = scale + pad;
        const right = scale + pad;
        const bottom = scale + pad;
        canvas.width = left + right;
        canvas.height = top + bottom;
    }
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    ctx.strokeStyle = outlineColor;
    ctx.lineWidth = outlineWidth;
    ctx.lineJoin = "miter";
    ctx.lineCap = "round";
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(canvas.width * 0.5, canvas.height * 0.5);
    if (tailIndex > 0) {
        const rendered = renderTail2(tailIndex, ctx, obj);
        if (!rendered) return `${skinIndex}|0|${(obj.buildIndex >= 0 ? obj.buildIndex : (`${weapon == -1 ? -1 : weapon.id}|${weaponVariant}`))}|${obj.skinColor}`;
    }
    if (weapon != -1 && weapon != undefined) {
        if (obj.buildIndex >= 0) {
            ctx.fillStyle = color;
            renderCircle(
                    scale * cos(-(PI / 4)),
                    scale * cos(-(PI / 4)),
                    14,
                    ctx
                );
                renderCircle(
                    scale * cos(-(PI / 4)),
                    scale * sin(-(PI / 4)),
                    14,
                    ctx
                );
                ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
            const item = items.list[obj.buildIndex];
            const sprite = (checkIsTeam(obj.sid) || !item.spike && !item.trap) ? getItemSprite(item, 0, EightySix) : getObjSprite(item, 0, EightySix);
            ctx.drawImage(
                sprite,
                scale - item.holdOffset,
                -sprite.width * 0.5
            );
        } else {
            if (!weapon.aboveHand) {
                ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
                const rendered = renderTool(weapon, sortVariantSrc(weaponVariant), 35, 0, ctx);
                if (!rendered) return `${skinIndex}|${tailIndex}|-1|0|${obj.skinColor}`;
                ctx.fillStyle = color;
                renderCircle(
                    35 * cos(-(PI / 4)),
                    35 * cos(-(PI / 4)),
                    14,
                    ctx
                );
                renderCircle(
                    35 * cos(-(PI / 4)),
                    35 * sin(-(PI / 4)),
                    14,
                    ctx
                );
            } else {
                let handAngle = (Math.PI / 4) * (weapon?.armS || 1);
                let oHandAngle = obj.buildIndex < 0 ? (weapon?.hndS || 1) : 1;
                let oHandDist = obj.buildIndex < 0 ? (weapon?.hndD || 1) : 1;
                renderCircle(
                    35 * cos(handAngle),
                    35 * sin(handAngle),
                    14
                );
                renderCircle(
                    35 * oHandDist * cos(-handAngle * oHandAngle),
                    35 * oHandDist * sin(-handAngle * oHandAngle),
                    14
                );
                ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
                const rendered = renderTool(weapon, sortVariantSrc(weaponVariant), 35, 0, ctx);
                if (!rendered) return `${skinIndex}|${tailIndex}|-1|0|${obj.skinColor}`;
                if (weapon.projectile != undefined &&!weapon.hideProjectile) {
                    const rendered2 = renderProjectile(
                        35,
                        0,
                        items.projectiles[weapon.projectile],
                        mainContext
                    );
                    if (!rendered2) return `${skinIndex}|${tailIndex}|-1|0|${obj.skinColor}`;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    ctx.fillStyle = color;
    renderCircle(0, 0, scale, ctx);
    if (skinIndex > 0) {
        ctx.globalAlpha = EightySix ? 0.83 : 1;
        ctx.rotate(PI * 0.5);
        const rendered = renderSkin(skinIndex, ctx, null, obj);
        if (!rendered) return `0|${tailIndex}|${(obj.buildIndex >= 0 ? obj.buildIndex : (`${weapon == -1 ? -1 : weapon.id}|${weaponVariant}`))}|${obj.skinColor}`
    }
    ctx.restore();
    return canvas;
}
globalThis.getHandSprite = function(obj, state, scale, isIcon) {
    const key = getHandKey(obj, state, isIcon);
    let sprite = handCache.get(key);
    if (!sprite) {
        sprite = buildHandSprite(obj, state, scale, isIcon);
        handCache.set(key, sprite);
    }
    return sprite;
}
globalThis.getPlayerBase = function(obj, key = getPlayerBaseKey(obj)) {
    let sprite = playerBaseCache.get(key);
    if (!sprite) {
        const split = key.split("|");
        sprite = buildPlayerBase(obj, Number(split[1]), split[2] == -1 ? -1 : items.weapons[Number(split[2])], Number(split[3]), Number(split[0]));
        if (typeof sprite === "string") return getPlayerBase(obj, sprite);
        playerBaseCache.set(key, sprite);
    }
    return sprite;
}
globalThis.getHandState = function(armS) {
    if (armS <= 0.65) return 0;
    if (armS <= 0.75) return 1;
    return 2;
}
// RENDER PLAYER:
globalThis.renderPlayer = function(obj, ctxt, asIcon) {
    ctxt = ctxt || mainContext;
    const scale = obj.scale;
    const isIcon = !!asIcon;
    ctxt.lineWidth = outlineWidth / (isIcon ? 2 : 1);
    ctxt.lineJoin = "miter";
    ctxt.shadowBlur = isIcon ? 0 : 15;
    ctxt.shadowColor = isIcon ? null : "rgba(0, 0, 0, .3)";
    const base = getPlayerBase(obj);
    ctxt.drawImage(
        base,
        -base.width * 0.5,
        -base.height * 0.5
    );
}
// RENDER NORMAL SKIN
var skinSprites2 = {};
var skinPointers2 = {};
globalThis.renderSkin2 = function(index, ctxt, parentSkin, owner) {
    tmpSkin = skinSprites2[index];
    if (!tmpSkin) {
        var tmpImage = new Image();
        tmpImage.onload = function () {
            this.isLoaded = true;
            this.onload = null;
        };
        //tmpImage.src = "https://moomoo.io/img/hats/hat_" + index + ".png";
        tmpImage.src = "https://moomoo.io/img/hats/hat_" + index + ".png";
        skinSprites2[index] = tmpImage;
        tmpSkin = tmpImage;
    }
    var tmpObj = parentSkin || skinPointers2[index];
    if (!tmpObj) {
        for (var i = 0; i < hats.length; ++i) {
            if (hats[i].id == index) {
                tmpObj = hats[i];
                break;
            }
        }
        skinPointers2[index] = tmpObj;
    }
    if (tmpSkin.isLoaded)
        ctxt.drawImage(
            tmpSkin,
            -tmpObj.scale * 0.5,
            -tmpObj.scale * 0.5,
            tmpObj.scale,
            tmpObj.scale
        );
    if (!parentSkin && tmpObj.topSprite) {
        ctxt.save();
        ctxt.rotate(owner.skinRot);
        renderSkin2(index + "_top", ctxt, tmpObj, owner);
        ctxt.restore();
    }
}

    samurai_1_r: "https://i.postimg.cc/VspfrChQ/samurai-1-r.png",

}
// RENDER SKINS:
globalThis.skinSprites = {};
globalThis.skinPointers = {};
globalThis.tmpSkin = null;
globalThis.getWeaponSrc = function(weaponName) {
    return /*configs.texturePack ? texturePackImages[weaponName] ?? */".././img/weapons/" + weaponName + ".png"// : ".././img/weapons/" + weaponName + ".png";
}
globalThis.renderSkin = function(index, ctxt, parentSkin, owner, asIcon) {
    tmpSkin = skinSprites[index];
    if (!tmpSkin) {
        let tmpImage = new Image();
        tmpImage.onload = function () {
            this.isLoaded = true;
            this.onload = null;
        };
        tmpImage.src = (index == 7 ? "https://i.postimg.cc/44MWN5Bp/hat-7.png" : "https://moomoo.io/img/hats/hat_" + index + ".png");
        skinSprites[index] = tmpImage;
        tmpSkin = tmpImage;
    }
    var tmpObj = parentSkin || skinPointers[index];
    if (!tmpObj) {
        for (let i = 0; i < hats.length; ++i) {
            if (hats[i].id != index) continue;
            tmpObj = hats[i];
            break;
        }
        skinPointers[index] = tmpObj;
    }
    if (tmpSkin.isLoaded) ctxt.drawImage(tmpSkin, -tmpObj.scale / (asIcon ? 4 : 2), -tmpObj.scale / (asIcon ? 4 : 2), tmpObj.scale / (asIcon ? 2 : 1), tmpObj.scale / (asIcon ? 2 : 1));
    else return false;
    if (!parentSkin && tmpObj.topSprite) {
        ctxt.save();
        ctxt.rotate(owner.skinRot);
        const rendered = renderSkin(index + "_top", ctxt, tmpObj, owner);
        ctxt.restore();
        if (!rendered) return false;
    }
    return true;
}
// RENDER TAIL:
var newAccImgs = {
    21: "https://i.imgur.com/4ddZert.png",
    19: "https://i.imgur.com/sULkUZT.png",
};
globalThis.setTailTextureImage = function(id, type, id2) {
        if (newAccImgs[id] && type == "acc") return newAccImgs[id];
        else {
            if (type == "acc") return ".././img/accessories/access_" + id + ".png";
            else if (type == "hat") return ".././img/hats/hat_" + id + ".png";
            else return ".././img/weapons/" + id + ".png";
        }
}
globalThis.renderTailTextureImage = function(index, ctxt, owner, asIcon) {
    let tmpObj = accessPointers[index];
    if (!(tmpSkin = accessSprites[index + (txt ? "lol" : 0)])) {
        var tmpImage = new Image();
        tmpImage.onload = function () {
            this.isLoaded = true;
            this.onload = null;
            if (index != 19) compositeImages.accessories[index] = generateCompositeImage("accessory", this, tmpObj.scale, tmpObj.scale);
        }
        tmpImage.src = setTailTextureImage(index, "acc") //".././img/accessories/access_" + index + ".png";
        accessSprites[index + (txt ? "lol" : 0)] = tmpImage
        tmpSkin = tmpImage;
    }
    if (!tmpObj) {
        for (let i = 0; i < accessories.length; ++i) {
            if (accessories[i].id == index) {
                tmpObj = accessories[i];
                break;
            }
        }
        accessPointers[index] = tmpObj;
    }
    if (tmpSkin.isLoaded) {
        ctxt.save();
        ctxt.translate((asIcon ? 0 : -20) - (tmpObj.xOff || 0), 0);
        if (tmpObj.spin) ctxt.rotate(owner.skinRot);
        ctxt.drawImage(
            tmpSkin,
            -(tmpObj.scale / (asIcon ? 4 : 2)),
            -(tmpObj.scale / (asIcon ? 4 : 2)),
            tmpObj.scale / (asIcon ? 2 : 1),
            tmpObj.scale / (asIcon ? 2 : 1)
        );
        ctxt.restore();
    }
}

// RENDER TOOL:
globalThis.toolSprites = {};
globalThis.renderTool = function(obj, variant, x, y, ctxt, asIcon) {
    let tmpSrc = obj.src + (variant || "");
    let tmpSprite = toolSprites[tmpSrc];
    if (!tmpSprite) {
        tmpSprite = new Image();
        tmpSprite.src = getWeaponSrc(tmpSrc);
        tmpSprite.onload = function () {
            this.isLoaded = true;
        };
        toolSprites[tmpSrc] = tmpSprite;
    }
    if (tmpSprite.isLoaded) {
        ctxt.drawImage(
            tmpSprite,
            x + obj.xOff - obj.length * 0.5,
            y + obj.yOff - obj.width * 0.5,
            obj.length * (asIcon ? 0.5 : 1),
            obj.width * (asIcon ? 0.5 : 1)
        );
    } else return false;
    return true;
}
// RENDER PROJECTILES:
globalThis.renderProjectiles = function(xOffset, yOffset) {
    let tmpObj;
    for (let i = 0; i < projectiles.length; i++) {
        tmpObj = projectiles[i];
        if (tmpObj.active && tmpObj.inWindow) {
            tmpObj.update(delta);
            mainContext.save();
            mainContext.translate(tmpObj.x - xOffset, tmpObj.y - yOffset);
            mainContext.rotate(tmpObj.dir);
            renderProjectile(0, 0, tmpObj, mainContext, 1);
            mainContext.restore();
        }
    }
}
// RENDER PROJECTILE:
globalThis.projectileSprites = {}; //fz iz zexy
globalThis.renderProjectile = function(x, y, obj, ctxt, asIcon, debug) {
    if (obj.src) {
        let tmpSrc = items.projectiles[obj.indx].src;
        let tmpSprite = projectileSprites[tmpSrc];
        if (!tmpSprite) {
            tmpSprite = new Image();
            tmpSprite.onload = function () {
                this.isLoaded = true;
                compositeImages.weapons[tmpSrc] = generateCompositeImage(1, this, obj.scale);
            };
            tmpSprite.src = "https://moomoo.io/img/weapons/" + tmpSrc + ".png";
            projectileSprites[tmpSrc] = tmpSprite;
        }
        if (tmpSprite.isLoaded)
            ctxt.drawImage(
                tmpSprite,
                x - obj.scale * 0.5,
                y - obj.scale * 0.5,
                obj.scale * (asIcon ? 0.5 : 1),
                obj.scale * (asIcon ? 0.5 : 1)
            );
        else return false;
    } else if (obj.indx == 1) {
        ctxt.fillStyle = "#939393";
        renderCircle(x, y, obj.scale, ctxt);
    }
    return true;
}
// RENDER AI:
globalThis.aiSprites = {};
globalThis.renderAI = function(obj, ctxt) {
    let tmpIndx = obj.index;
    let tmpSprite = aiSprites[tmpIndx];
    if (!tmpSprite) {
        let tmpImg = new Image();
        tmpImg.onload = function () {
            this.isLoaded = true;
            this.onload = null;
        };
        tmpImg.src = "https://moomoo.io/img/animals/" + obj.src + ".png";
        tmpSprite = tmpImg;
        aiSprites[tmpIndx] = tmpSprite;
    }
    if (tmpSprite.isLoaded) {
        let tmpScale = obj.scale * 1.2 * (obj.spriteMlt || 1);
        ctxt.drawImage(tmpSprite, -tmpScale, -tmpScale, tmpScale * 2, tmpScale * 2);
    }
}
globalThis.aiImages = {};
globalThis.drawAnimal = function(animal) {
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    canvas.height = 130;
    canvas.width = 90;
    parent.appendChild(canvas);
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d")
    ctx.strokeStyle = outlineColor;
    ctx.translate(40, 40)
    ctx.save()
    drawAnimalSprite(animal, ctx);
    ctx.restore()
    ctx.font = "15px Hammersmith One";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
    ctx.lineJoin = "round";
    ctx.fillStyle = "#fff";
    ctx.strokeStyle = outlineColor;
    ctx.strokeText(animal.name, 0, -30)
    ctx.fillText(animal.name, 0, -30)
}
globalThis.drawAnimalSprite = function(obj, ctxt) {
    let tmpSprite = aiImages[obj.src];
    if (!tmpSprite) {
        let tmpImg = new Image();
        tmpImg.onload = function () {
            this.isLoaded = true;
            this.onload = null;
        };
        tmpImg.src = "https://moomoo.io/img/animals/" + obj.src + ".png";
        tmpSprite = tmpImg;
        aiImages[obj.src] = tmpSprite;
    }
    if (tmpSprite.isLoaded) {
        const tmpScale = 50;
        ctxt.drawImage(tmpSprite, -tmpScale, -tmpScale, tmpScale * 2, tmpScale * 2);
    }
}
// RENDER WATER BODIES:
globalThis.renderWaterBodies = function(xOffset, yOffset, ctxt, padding) {
    // MIDDLE RIVER:
    let tmpW = config.riverWidth + padding;
    let tmpY = 7200 - yOffset - tmpW * 0.5;
    if (tmpY < maxScreenHeight && tmpY + tmpW > 0) ctxt.fillRect(0, tmpY, maxScreenWidth, tmpW);
}
// RENDER GAME OBJECTS:
globalThis.gameObjectSprites = {};
globalThis.getResSprite = function(obj) {
    let biomeID = (obj.y >= 14400 - config.snowBiomeTop) ? 2 : ((obj.y <= config.snowBiomeTop) ? 1 : 0);
    let tmpIndex = (obj.type + "_" + obj.scale + "_" + biomeID + "_" + configs.grayVis);
    let tmpSprite = gameObjectSprites[tmpIndex];
    if (!tmpSprite) {
        let blurScale = 6;
        let tmpCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        tmpCanvas.width = tmpCanvas.height = (obj.scale * 2.1) + outlineWidth + (obj.type == 1 ? 5 : obj.type != 0 ? 25 : 0);
        let tmpContext = tmpCanvas.getContext('2d');
        tmpContext.translate((tmpCanvas.width * 0.5), (tmpCanvas.height * 0.5));
        tmpContext.rotate(UWUTILS.randFloat(0, PI));
        tmpContext.strokeStyle = outlineColor;
        tmpContext.lineWidth = outlineWidth;
        // if (isNight) {
        //     tmpContext.shadowBlur = blurScale;
        //     tmpContext.shadowColor = `rgba(0, 0, 0, ${obj.alpha})`;
        // }
        if (obj.type == 0) {
            let tmpScale;
            let tmpCount = 8;
            for (let i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
                tmpContext.shadowBlur = i === 0 ? 27 : 0;
                tmpContext.shadowColor = i === 0 ? "rgba(0, 0, 0, .9)" : null;
                tmpScale = obj.scale * (!i ? 1 : 0.5);
                renderStar(tmpContext, tmpCount, tmpScale, tmpScale * 0.7);
                //"#d0dbb7"; //"#b6db66"; "#9ebf57" : "#b4db62"
                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? !biomeID ? (!i ? "#b7c29d" : "#d6e2b9") : (!i ? "#e3f1f4" : "#fff") : !biomeID ? (!i ? "#9ebf57" : "#b4db62") : (!i ? "#e3f1f4" : "#fff");
                tmpContext.fill();
                if (!i) {
                    tmpContext.stroke();
                    tmpContext.shadowBlur = null;
                    tmpContext.shadowColor = null;
                    tmpContext.globalAlpha = 1;
                }
            }
        } else if (obj.type == 1) {
            if (biomeID == 2) {
                tmpContext.fillStyle = "#606060";
                renderStar(tmpContext, 6, obj.scale * 0.3, obj.scale * 0.71);
                tmpContext.fill();
                tmpContext.stroke();
                //tmpContext.shadowBlur = null;
                //tmpContext.shadowColor = null;
                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#a3aa95" : "#89a54c";//"#89a54c"//"#d0dbb7"; //"#b6db66";
                renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.55, tmpContext);
                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#d0dbb7" : "#a5c65b";//"#a5c65b"//"#d0dbb7"; //"#b6db66";
                renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.3, tmpContext, true);
            } else {
                tmpContext.shadowBlur = 27;
                tmpContext.shadowColor = "rgba(0, 0, 0, .9)";
                renderBlob(tmpContext, 6, obj.scale, obj.scale * 0.7);
                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? biomeID ? "#f1f1f1" : "#9ea78b" : biomeID ? "#e3f1f4" : "#89a54c";//"#89a54c"//"#d0dbb7"; //"#b6db66";
                tmpContext.fill();
                tmpContext.stroke();
                //tmpContext.shadowBlur = null;
                //tmpContext.shadowColor = null;
                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? biomeID ? "#7c77b4" : "#b66363" : biomeID ? "#6a64af" : "#c15555"; // "#6a64af" "#c15555";
                let tmpRange;
                let berries = 4;
                let rotVal = (PI2) / berries;
                for (let i = 0; i < berries; ++i) {
                    tmpRange = UWUTILS.randInt(obj.scale * 0.2857, obj.scale * 0.4348);
                    renderCircle(tmpRange * cos(rotVal * i), tmpRange * sin(rotVal * i),
                        UWUTILS.randInt(10, 12), tmpContext);
                }
            }
        } else if (obj.type == 2 || obj.type == 3) {
            tmpContext.shadowBlur = 27;
            tmpContext.shadowColor = "rgba(0, 0, 0, .9)";
            // "#938d77" "#939393" "#e0c655"
            tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? (obj.type == 2) ? (biomeID == 2 ? "#969286" : "#b4b4b4") : "#d8cb97" : (obj.type == 2) ? (biomeID == 2 ? "#938d77" : "#939393") : "#e0c655";
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale, obj.scale);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.shadowBlur = null;
            tmpContext.shadowColor = null;
            //"#b2ab90" : "#bcbcbc" "#ebdca3"
            tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? (obj.type == 2) ? (biomeID == 2 ? "#c0bca8" : "#d3d3d3") : "#e0d7b3" : (obj.type == 2) ? (biomeID == 2 ? "#b2ab90" : "#bcbcbc") : "#ebdca3";;
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale * 0.55, obj.scale * 0.65);
            tmpContext.fill();
        }
        tmpSprite = tmpCanvas;
        gameObjectSprites[tmpIndex] = tmpSprite;
    }
    return tmpSprite;
}
globalThis.itemSpritesLARP = {};
globalThis.getItemSpriteLARP = function(obj, asIcon) {
    let tmpSprite = itemSpritesLARP[obj.id];
    if (!tmpSprite || asIcon) {
        let blurScale = !asIcon ? 25 : 5;
        let tmpCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        let reScale =
            !asIcon && obj.name == "windmill" ? items.list[4].scale : obj.scale;
        tmpCanvas.width = tmpCanvas.height = reScale * 2.5 + outlineWidth + (items.list[obj.id].spritePadding || 0) + blurScale;
        let tmpContext = tmpCanvas.getContext("2d");
        tmpContext.translate(tmpCanvas.width * 0.5, tmpCanvas.height * 0.5);
        tmpContext.rotate(asIcon ? 0 : PI * 0.5);
        tmpContext.strokeStyle = outlineColor;
        tmpContext.lineWidth = outlineWidth * (asIcon ? tmpCanvas.width / 81 : 1);
        if (!asIcon) {
            tmpContext.shadowBlur = 8;
            tmpContext.shadowColor = `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2)`;
        }
        tmpContext.shadowBlur = 17;
        tmpContext.shadowColor = `rgba(255, 255, 0, 10000)`;
        if (obj.name == "apple") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#c15555";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#89a54c";
            let leafDir = -(PI * 0.5);
            renderLeaf(
                obj.scale * cos(leafDir),
                obj.scale * sin(leafDir),
                25,
                leafDir + PI * 0.5,
                tmpContext
            );
        } else if (obj.name == "cookie") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#cca861";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#937c4b";
            let chips = 4;
            let rotVal = (PI2) / chips;
            let tmpRange;
            for (let i = 0; i < chips; ++i) {
                tmpRange = UWUTILS.randInt(obj.scale * 0.4, obj.scale / 1.7);
                renderCircle(
                    tmpRange * cos(rotVal * i),
                    tmpRange * sin(rotVal * i),
                    UWUTILS.randInt(4, 5),
                    tmpContext,
                    true
                );
            }
        } else if (obj.name == "cheese") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#f4f3ac";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#c3c28b";
            let chips = 4;
            let rotVal = (PI2) / chips;
            let tmpRange;
            for (let i = 0; i < chips; ++i) {
                tmpRange = UWUTILS.randInt(obj.scale * 0.4, obj.scale / 1.7);
                renderCircle(
                    tmpRange * cos(rotVal * i),
                    tmpRange * sin(rotVal * i),
                    UWUTILS.randInt(4, 5),
                    tmpContext,
                    true
                );
            }
        } else if (
            obj.name == "wood wall" ||
            obj.name == "stone wall" ||
            obj.name == "castle wall"
        ) {
            tmpContext.fillStyle =
                obj.name == "castle wall"
                    ? "#83898e"
                    : obj.name == "wood wall"
                        ? "#a5974c"
                        : "#939393";
            let sides = obj.name == "castle wall" ? 4 : 3;
            renderStar(tmpContext, sides, obj.scale * 1.1, obj.scale * 1.1);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle =
                obj.name == "castle wall"
                    ? "#9da4aa"
                    : obj.name == "wood wall"
                        ? "#c9b758"
                        : "#bcbcbc";
            renderStar(tmpContext, sides, obj.scale * 0.65, obj.scale * 0.65);
            tmpContext.fill();
        } else if (
            obj.name == "spikes" ||
            obj.name == "greater spikes" ||
            obj.name == "poison spikes" ||
            obj.name == "spinning spikes"
        ) {
                tmpContext.fillStyle =
                    obj.name == "poison spikes" ? "#ff0000" : "#ff0000";
                let tmpScale = obj.scale * 0.6;
                renderStar(tmpContext, obj.name == "spikes" ? 5 : 6, obj.scale);
                tmpContext.stroke();
                renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale, tmpContext);
                renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale * 0.5, tmpContext, true);
        } else if (
            obj.name == "windmill" ||
            obj.name == "faster windmill" ||
            obj.name == "power mill"
        ) {
            renderCircle(0, 0, reScale);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#ff0000";
            renderRectCircle(0, 0, reScale * 1.5, 29, 4, tmpContext);
            renderCircle(0, 0, reScale * 0.5, tmpContext);
        } else if (obj.name == "mine") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#939393";
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale, obj.scale);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#bcbcbc";
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale * 0.55, obj.scale * 0.65);
            tmpContext.fill();
        } else if (obj.name == "sapling") {
            for (let i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
                let tmpScale = obj.scale * (!i ? 1 : 0.5);
                renderStar(tmpContext, 7, tmpScale, tmpScale * 0.7);
                tmpContext.fillStyle = !i ? "#9ebf57" : "#b4db62";
                tmpContext.fill();
                if (!i) tmpContext.stroke();
            }
        } else if (obj.name == "pit trap") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#a5974c";
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale * 1.1, obj.scale * 1.1);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = outlineColor;
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale * 0.65, obj.scale * 0.65);
            tmpContext.fill();
        } else if (obj.name == "boost pad") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
            renderRect(0, 0, obj.scale * 2, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#dbd97d";
            renderTriangle(obj.scale * 1, tmpContext);
        } else if (obj.name == "turret") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#a5974c";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#939393";
            let tmpLen = 50;
            renderRect(0, -tmpLen * 0.5, obj.scale * 0.9, tmpLen, tmpContext);
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.6, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
        } else if (obj.name == "platform") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#cebd5f";
            let tmpCount = 4;
            let tmpS = obj.scale * 2;
            let tmpW = tmpS / tmpCount;
            let tmpX = -(obj.scale * 0.5);
            for (let i = 0; i < tmpCount; ++i) {
                renderRect(tmpX - tmpW * 0.5, 0, tmpW, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
                tmpContext.fill();
                tmpContext.stroke();
                tmpX += tmpS / tmpCount;
            }
        } else if (obj.name == "healing pad") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
            renderRect(0, 0, obj.scale * 2, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#db6e6e";
            renderRectCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.65, 20, 4, tmpContext, true);
        } else if (obj.name == "spawn pad") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
            renderRect(0, 0, obj.scale * 2, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#71aad6";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.6, tmpContext);
        } else if (obj.name == "blocker") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.rotate(PI / 4);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#db6e6e";
            renderRectCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.65, 20, 4, tmpContext, true);
        } else if (obj.name == "teleporter") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.rotate(PI / 4);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#d76edb";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.5, tmpContext, true);
        }
        tmpSprite = tmpCanvas;
        if (!asIcon) itemSpritesLARP[obj.id] = tmpSprite;
    }
    return tmpSprite;
}
globalThis.gameObjectSpritesLARP = {};
globalThis.getResSpriteLARP = function(obj) {
    console.log("el larpo")
    let biomeID = (obj.y >= 14400 - config.snowBiomeTop) ? 2 : ((obj.y <= config.snowBiomeTop) ? 1 : 0);
    let tmpIndex = (obj.type + "_" + obj.scale + "_" + biomeID);
    let tmpSprite = gameObjectSpritesLARP[tmpIndex];
    if (!tmpSprite) {
        let blurScale = 6;
        let tmpCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        tmpCanvas.width = tmpCanvas.height = (obj.scale * 2.1) + outlineWidth + (obj.type == 1 ? 5 : obj.type != 0 ? 25 : 0);
        let tmpContext = tmpCanvas.getContext('2d');
        tmpContext.translate((tmpCanvas.width * 0.5), (tmpCanvas.height * 0.5));
        tmpContext.rotate(UWUTILS.randFloat(0, PI));
        tmpContext.strokeStyle = outlineColor;
        tmpContext.lineWidth = outlineWidth;
        // if (isNight) {
        //     tmpContext.shadowBlur = blurScale;
        //     tmpContext.shadowColor = `rgba(0, 0, 0, ${obj.alpha})`;
        // }
        if (obj.type == 0) {
            tmpContext.shadowBlur = 27;
            tmpContext.shadowColor = "rgba(255, 255, 0, .9)";
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#e0c655";
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale, obj.scale);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.shadowBlur = null;
            tmpContext.shadowColor = null;
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#ebdca3";
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale * 0.55, obj.scale * 0.65);
            tmpContext.fill();
        } else if (obj.type == 1) {
            if (biomeID == 2) {
                tmpContext.fillStyle = "#606060";
                renderStar(tmpContext, 6, obj.scale * 0.3, obj.scale * 0.71);
                tmpContext.fill();
                tmpContext.stroke();

                //tmpContext.shadowBlur = null;
                //tmpContext.shadowColor = null;

                tmpContext.fillStyle = "#89a54c";
                renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.55, tmpContext);
                tmpContext.fillStyle = "#a5c65b";
                renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.3, tmpContext, true);
            } else {
                tmpContext.shadowBlur = 27;
                tmpContext.shadowColor = "rgba(255, 255, 0, 1)";
                renderBlob(tmpContext, 6, obj.scale, obj.scale * 0.7);
                tmpContext.fillStyle = biomeID ? "#e3f1f4" : "#89a54c";
                tmpContext.fill();
                tmpContext.stroke();

                //tmpContext.shadowBlur = null;
                //tmpContext.shadowColor = null;

                tmpContext.fillStyle = biomeID ? "#6a64af" : "#c15555";
                let tmpRange;
                let berries = 4;
                let rotVal = (PI2) / berries;
                for (let i = 0; i < berries; ++i) {
                    tmpRange = UWUTILS.randInt(obj.scale * 0.2857, obj.scale * 0.4348);
                    renderCircle(tmpRange * cos(rotVal * i), tmpRange * sin(rotVal * i),
                        UWUTILS.randInt(10, 12), tmpContext);
                }
            }
        } else if (obj.type == 2 || obj.type == 3) {
            let tmpScale;
            let tmpCount = 8;
            for (let i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
                tmpContext.shadowBlur = i === 0 ? 27 : 0;
                tmpContext.shadowColor = i === 0 ? "rgba(255, 255, 0, .9)" : null;
                tmpScale = obj.scale * (!i ? 1 : 0.5);
                renderStar(tmpContext, tmpCount, tmpScale, tmpScale * 0.7);
                tmpContext.fillStyle = !biomeID ? (!i ? "#9ebf57" : "#b4db62") : (!i ? "#e3f1f4" : "#fff");
                tmpContext.fill();
                if (!i) {
                    tmpContext.stroke();
                    tmpContext.shadowBlur = null;
                    tmpContext.shadowColor = null;
                    tmpContext.globalAlpha = 1;
                }
            }
        }
        tmpSprite = tmpCanvas;
        gameObjectSpritesLARP[tmpIndex] = tmpSprite;
        return tmpSprite;
    }
    return tmpSprite;
}
// GET ITEM SPRITE:
globalThis.itemSprites = {};
globalThis.getItemSprite = function(obj, asIcon, EightySix) {
    let tmpSprite = itemSprites[obj.id + configs.grayVis * 100];
    if (!tmpSprite || asIcon) {
        let blurScale = !asIcon ? 25 : 5;
        let tmpCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        let reScale = !asIcon && obj.name == "windmill" ? items.list[4].scale : obj.scale;
        tmpCanvas.width = tmpCanvas.height = reScale * 2.5 + outlineWidth + (items.list[obj.id].spritePadding || 0) + blurScale;
        let tmpContext = tmpCanvas.getContext("2d");
        tmpContext.translate(tmpCanvas.width * 0.5, tmpCanvas.height * 0.5);
        tmpContext.rotate(asIcon ? 0 : PI * 0.5);
        tmpContext.strokeStyle = outlineColor;
        tmpContext.lineWidth = outlineWidth * (asIcon ? tmpCanvas.width / 81 : 1);
        if (!asIcon) {
            tmpContext.shadowBlur = 8;
            tmpContext.shadowColor = `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2)`;
        }
        tmpContext.shadowBlur = 12;
        tmpContext.shadowColor = `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4)`
        if (obj.name == "apple") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#c15555";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#89a54c";
            let leafDir = -(PI * 0.5);
            renderLeaf(
                obj.scale * cos(leafDir),
                obj.scale * sin(leafDir),
                25,
                leafDir + PI * 0.5,
                tmpContext
            );
        } else if (obj.name == "cookie") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#cca861";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#937c4b";
            let chips = 4;
            let rotVal = (PI2) / chips;
            let tmpRange;
            for (let i = 0; i < chips; ++i) {
                tmpRange = UWUTILS.randInt(obj.scale * 0.4, obj.scale / 1.7);
                renderCircle(
                    tmpRange * cos(rotVal * i),
                    tmpRange * sin(rotVal * i),
                    UWUTILS.randInt(4, 5),
                    tmpContext,
                    true
                );
            }
        } else if (obj.name == "cheese") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#f4f3ac";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#c3c28b";
            let chips = 4;
            let rotVal = (PI2) / chips;
            let tmpRange;
            for (let i = 0; i < chips; ++i) {
                tmpRange = UWUTILS.randInt(obj.scale * 0.4, obj.scale / 1.7);
                renderCircle(tmpRange * cos(rotVal * i), tmpRange * sin(rotVal * i),UWUTILS.randInt(4, 5), tmpContext, true);
            }
        } else if (
            obj.name == "wood wall" ||
            obj.name == "stone wall" ||
            obj.name == "castle wall"
        ) {
            tmpContext.fillStyle =
                obj.name == "castle wall"
                    ? "#83898e"
                    : obj.name == "wood wall"
                        ? "#a5974c"
                        : "#939393";
            let sides = obj.name == "castle wall" ? 4 : 3;
            renderStar(tmpContext, sides, obj.scale * 1.1, obj.scale * 1.1);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle =
                obj.name == "castle wall"
                    ? "#9da4aa"
                    : obj.name == "wood wall"
                        ? "#c9b758"
                        : "#bcbcbc";
            renderStar(tmpContext, sides, obj.scale * 0.65, obj.scale * 0.65);
            tmpContext.fill();
        } else if (
            obj.name == "spikes" ||
            obj.name == "greater spikes" ||
            obj.name == "poison spikes" ||
            obj.name == "spinning spikes"
        ) {
            if (EightySix) {
                tmpContext.fillStyle = obj.name == "poison spikes" ? "#5d9372" : "#879bc0";
                let tmpScale = obj.scale * 0.6;
                renderStar(tmpContext, obj.name == "spikes" ? 5 : 6, obj.scale, tmpScale);
                tmpContext.fill();
                tmpContext.stroke();
                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#c0c0c0" : "#a5974c";
                renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale, tmpContext);
                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#d3d3d3" : "#c9b758";//"#d3d3d3";//"#a5974c";
                renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale * 0.5, tmpContext, true);
            } else {
                /*
                tmpContext.fillStyle =
                    obj.name == "poison spikes" ? "#7b935d" : "#939393";
                let tmpScale = obj.scale * 0.6;
                renderStar(tmpContext, obj.name == "spikes" ? 5 : 6, obj.scale, tmpScale);
                tmpContext.fill();
                tmpContext.stroke();
                tmpContext.fillStyle = "#d3d3d3";//"#a5974c";
                renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale, tmpContext);
                tmpContext.fillStyle = "#d3d3d3";//"#a5974c";
                renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale * 0.5, tmpContext, true);
                */
                tmpContext.fillStyle = obj.name == "poison spikes" ? "#7b935d" : "#939393";
                let tmpScale = obj.scale * 0.6;
                renderStar(tmpContext, obj.name == "spikes" ? 5 : 6, obj.scale, tmpScale);
                tmpContext.fill();
                tmpContext.stroke();
                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#c0c0c0" : "#a5974c";
                renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale, tmpContext);
                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#d3d3d3" : "#c9b758";
                renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale * 0.5, tmpContext, true);
            }
        } else if (
            obj.name == "windmill" ||
            obj.name == "faster windmill" ||
            obj.name == "power mill"
        ) {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#bdbdbd" : "#a5974c";//"#a5974c";
            renderCircle(0, 0, reScale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#d3d3d3" : "#c9b758";//"#a5974c";
            renderRectCircle(0, 0, reScale * 1.5, 29, 4, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#bdbdbd" : "#a5974c";//"#a5974c";
            renderCircle(0, 0, reScale * 0.5, tmpContext);
        } else if (obj.name == "mine") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#939393";
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale, obj.scale);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#bcbcbc";
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale * 0.55, obj.scale * 0.65);
            tmpContext.fill();
        } else if (obj.name == "sapling") {
            for (let i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
                let tmpScale = obj.scale * (!i ? 1 : 0.5);
                renderStar(tmpContext, 7, tmpScale, tmpScale * 0.7);
                tmpContext.fillStyle = !configs.grayVis ? (!i ? "#9ebf57" : "#b4db62") : (!i ? "#b7c29d" : "#d6e2b9");
                tmpContext.fill();
                if (!i) tmpContext.stroke();
            }
        } else if (obj.name == "pit trap") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#d3d3d3" : "#a5974c";//"#a5974c";
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale * 1.1, obj.scale * 1.1);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = outlineColor;
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale * 0.65, obj.scale * 0.65);
            tmpContext.fill();
        } else if (obj.name == "boost pad") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
            renderRect(0, 0, obj.scale * 2, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#dfdd97" : "#dbd97d";
            renderTriangle(obj.scale * 1, tmpContext);
        } else if (obj.name == "turret") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#d3d3d3" : "#a5974c";//"#a5974c";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#939393"
            let tmpLen = 50;
            renderRect(0, -tmpLen * 0.5, obj.scale * 0.9, tmpLen, tmpContext);
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.6, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
        } else if (obj.name == "platform") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#cebd5f";
            let tmpCount = 4;
            let tmpS = obj.scale * 2;
            let tmpW = tmpS / tmpCount;
            let tmpX = -(obj.scale * 0.5);
            for (let i = 0; i < tmpCount; ++i) {
                renderRect(tmpX - tmpW * 0.5, 0, tmpW, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
                tmpContext.fill();
                tmpContext.stroke();
                tmpX += tmpS / tmpCount;
            }
        } else if (obj.name == "healing pad") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
            renderRect(0, 0, obj.scale * 2, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#db6e6e";
            renderRectCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.65, 20, 4, tmpContext, true);
        } else if (obj.name == "spawn pad") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
            renderRect(0, 0, obj.scale * 2, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#71aad6";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.6, tmpContext);
        } else if (obj.name == "blocker") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.rotate(PI / 4);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#db6e6e";
            renderRectCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.65, 20, 4, tmpContext, true);
        } else if (obj.name == "teleporter") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.rotate(PI / 4);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#d76edb";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.5, tmpContext, true);
        }
        tmpSprite = tmpCanvas;
        if (!asIcon) itemSprites[obj.id + configs.grayVis * 100] = tmpSprite;
    }
    return tmpSprite;
}
globalThis.getItemSprite2 = function(obj, tmpX, tmpY) {
    let tmpContext = mainContext;
    let reScale = obj.name == "windmill" ? items.list[4].scale : obj.scale;
    tmpContext.save();
    tmpContext.translate(tmpX, tmpY);
    tmpContext.rotate(obj.dir);
    tmpContext.strokeStyle = outlineColor;
    tmpContext.lineWidth = outlineWidth;
    tmpContext.shadowBlur = 12;
    tmpContext.shadowColor = `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4)`
    if (obj.name == "apple") {
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#c15555";
        renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#89a54c";
        let leafDir = -(PI * 0.5);
        renderLeaf(
            obj.scale * cos(leafDir),
            obj.scale * sin(leafDir),
            25,
            leafDir + PI * 0.5,

        tmpContext.fillStyle = obj.name == "poison spikes" ? "#7b935d" : "#939393";

        renderStar(tmpContext, obj.name == "spikes" ? 5 : 6, obj.scale, tmpScale);

    } else if (obj.name == "sapling") {
        for (let i = 0; i < 2; ++i) {
            let tmpScale = obj.scale * (!i ? 1 : 0.5);
            renderStar(tmpContext, 7, tmpScale, tmpScale * 0.7);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = !configs.grayVis ? (!i ? "#9ebf57" : "#b4db62") : (!i ? "#b7c29d" : "#d6e2b9");
            tmpContext.fill();
            if (!i) tmpContext.stroke();
        }
    } else if (obj.name == "pit trap") {
        tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#d3d3d3" : "#a5974c";//"#a5974c";
        renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale * 1.1, obj.scale * 1.1);
        tmpContext.fill();
        tmpContext.stroke();
        tmpContext.fillStyle = outlineColor;
        renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale * 0.65, obj.scale * 0.65);
        tmpContext.fill();
    } else if (obj.name == "boost pad") {
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
        renderRect(0, 0, obj.scale * 2, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
        tmpContext.fill();
        tmpContext.stroke();
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#dbd97d";
        renderTriangle(obj.scale * 1, tmpContext);
    } else if (obj.name == "turret") {
        tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#d3d3d3" : "#a5974c";//"#a5974c";
        renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
        tmpContext.fill();
        tmpContext.stroke();
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#939393"
        let tmpLen = 50;
        renderRect(0, -tmpLen * 0.5, obj.scale * 0.9, tmpLen, tmpContext);
        renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.6, tmpContext);
        tmpContext.fill();
        tmpContext.stroke();
    } else if (obj.name == "platform") {
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#cebd5f";
        let tmpCount = 4;
        let tmpS = obj.scale * 2;
        let tmpW = tmpS / tmpCount;
        let tmpX = -(obj.scale * 0.5);
        for (let i = 0; i < tmpCount; ++i) {
            renderRect(tmpX - tmpW * 0.5, 0, tmpW, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpX += tmpS / tmpCount;
        }
    } else if (obj.name == "healing pad") {
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
        renderRect(0, 0, obj.scale * 2, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
        tmpContext.fill();
        tmpContext.stroke();
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#db6e6e";
        renderRectCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.65, 20, 4, tmpContext, true);
    } else if (obj.name == "spawn pad") {
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
        renderRect(0, 0, obj.scale * 2, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
        tmpContext.fill();
        tmpContext.stroke();
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#71aad6";
        renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.6, tmpContext);
    } else if (obj.name == "blocker") {
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
        renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
        tmpContext.fill();
        tmpContext.stroke();
        tmpContext.rotate(PI / 4);
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#db6e6e";
        renderRectCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.65, 20, 4, tmpContext, true);
    } else if (obj.name == "teleporter") {
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
        renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
        tmpContext.fill();
        tmpContext.stroke();
        tmpContext.rotate(PI / 4);
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#d76edb";
        renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.5, tmpContext, true);
    }
    tmpContext.restore();
}
globalThis.objSprites = {};
globalThis.markSprites = {};
globalThis.getObjSprite = function(obj, EightySix) {
    let tmpSprite = objSprites[obj.id + configs.grayVis * 100];
    if (!tmpSprite) {
        // let blurScale = isNight ? 20 : 0;
        let tmpCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        tmpCanvas.width = tmpCanvas.height = obj.scale * 2.5 + outlineWidth + (items.list[obj.id].spritePadding || 0) + 15;
        let tmpContext = tmpCanvas.getContext("2d");
        tmpContext.translate(tmpCanvas.width * 0.5, tmpCanvas.height * 0.5);
        tmpContext.rotate(PI * 0.5);
        tmpContext.strokeStyle = outlineColor;
        tmpContext.lineWidth = outlineWidth;
        tmpContext.shadowBlur = 12;
        tmpContext.shadowColor = `rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.4)`
        // if (isNight) {
        //     tmpContext.shadowBlur = 20;
        //     tmpContext.shadowColor = `rgba(0, 0, 0, ${mathMin(0.3, obj.alpha)})`;
        // }
        if (
            obj.name == "spikes" ||
            obj.name == "greater spikes" ||
            obj.name == "poison spikes" ||
            obj.name == "spinning spikes"
        ) {
            if (EightySix) {
                tmpContext.fillStyle = obj.name == "poison spikes" ? "#663a00" : "#a55a4d";
                let tmpScale = obj.scale * 0.6;
                renderStar(tmpContext, obj.name == "spikes" ? 5 : 6, obj.scale, tmpScale);
                tmpContext.fill();
                tmpContext.stroke();
                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#c0c0c0" : "#a5974c";
                renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale, tmpContext);
                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#d3d3d3" : "#c9b758";//"#d3d3d3";//"#a5974c";
                renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale * 0.5, tmpContext, true);
            } else {
                tmpContext.fillStyle = obj.name == "poison spikes" ? "#7b935d" : "#939393";
                let tmpScale = obj.scale * 0.6;
                renderStar(tmpContext, obj.name == "spikes" ? 5 : 6, obj.scale, tmpScale);
                tmpContext.fill();
                tmpContext.stroke();
                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#c0c0c0" : "#a5974c";
                renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale, tmpContext);
                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#d3d3d3" : "#c9b758";//"#d3d3d3";//"#a5974c";
                renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale * 0.5, tmpContext, true);
            }
        } else if (obj.name == "pit trap") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#d3d3d3" : "#a5974c";
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale * 1.1, obj.scale * 1.1);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#cc5151";
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale * 0.65, obj.scale * 0.65);
            tmpContext.fill();
        }
        tmpSprite = tmpCanvas;
        objSprites[obj.id + configs.grayVis * 100] = tmpSprite;
    }
    return tmpSprite;
}
// GET MARK SPRITE:
globalThis.getMarkSprite = function(obj) {
    let tmpSprite = markSprites[obj.id + configs.grayVis * 100];
    if (!tmpSprite) {
        let tmpCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        tmpCanvas.width = tmpCanvas.height = obj.scale * 2.5 + outlineWidth + (items.list[obj.id].spritePadding || 0) + 15;
        let tmpContext = tmpCanvas.getContext("2d");
        tmpContext.translate(tmpCanvas.width * 0.5, tmpCanvas.height * 0.5);
        tmpContext.lineWidth = outlineWidth;
        mainContext.globalAlpha = 0.2;
        tmpContext.strokeStyle = outlineColor;
        if (
            obj.name == "spikes" ||
            obj.name == "greater spikes" ||
            obj.name == "poison spikes" ||
            obj.name == "spinning spikes"
        ) {
            /*
            tmpContext.fillStyle = obj.name == "poison spikes" ? "#7b935d" : "#939393";
            let tmpScale = obj.scale * 0.6;
            renderStar(tmpContext, obj.name == "spikes" ? 5 : 6, obj.scale, tmpScale);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#d3d3d3";//"#a5974c";
            renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#d3d3d3";//"#a5974c";
            renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale * 0.5, tmpContext, true);
            */
        configs.grayVis ? "#d3d3d3" : "#a5974c";"#c9b758";
        tmpContext.fillStyle = obj.name == "poison spikes" ? "#7b935d" : "#939393";
                let tmpScale = obj.scale * 0.6;
                renderStar(tmpContext, obj.name == "spikes" ? 5 : 6, obj.scale, tmpScale);
                tmpContext.fill();
                tmpContext.stroke();
                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#c0c0c0" : "#a5974c";

                tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#d3d3d3" : "#c9b758";//"#d3d3d3";//"#a5974c";
                renderCircle(0, 0, tmpScale * 0.5, tmpContext, true);
        } else if (obj.name == "turret") {
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#939393"
            let tmpLen = 50;
            renderRect(0, -tmpLen * 0.5, obj.scale * 0.9, tmpLen, tmpContext);
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.6, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
        } else if (obj.name == "teleporter") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.rotate(PI / 4);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#d76edb";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.5, tmpContext, true);
        } else if (obj.name == "platform") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#cebd5f";
            let tmpCount = 4;
            let tmpS = obj.scale * 2;
            let tmpW = tmpS / tmpCount;
            let tmpX = -(obj.scale * 0.5);
            for (let i = 0; i < tmpCount; ++i) {
                renderRect(tmpX - tmpW * 0.5, 0, tmpW, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
                tmpContext.fill();
                tmpContext.stroke();
                tmpX += tmpS / tmpCount;
            }
        } else if (obj.name == "healing pad") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
            renderRect(0, 0, obj.scale * 2, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#db6e6e";
            renderRectCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.65, 20, 4, tmpContext, true);
        } else if (obj.name == "spawn pad") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
            renderRect(0, 0, obj.scale * 2, obj.scale * 2, tmpContext);
            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();

        } else if (obj.name == "blocker") {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#7e7f82";
            renderCircle(0, 0, obj.scale, tmpContext);

            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.rotate(PI / 4);
            tmpContext.fillStyle = "#db6e6e";
            renderRectCircle(0, 0, obj.scale * 0.65, 20, 4, tmpContext, true);
        } else if (
            obj.name == "windmill" ||
            obj.name == "faster windmill" ||
            obj.name == "power mill"
        ) {
            tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#bdbdbd" : "#a5974c";//"#a5974c";

            tmpContext.fillStyle = configs.grayVis ? "#d3d3d3" : "#c9b758";//"#a5974c";

        } else if (obj.name == "pit trap") {
