            tmpContext.fill();
            tmpContext.stroke();
            tmpContext.fillStyle = outlineColor;
            renderStar(tmpContext, 3, obj.scale * 0.65, obj.scale * 0.65);
            tmpContext.fill();
        }
        tmpSprite = tmpCanvas;
        markSprites[obj.id + configs.grayVis * 100] = tmpSprite;
    }
    return tmpSprite;
}
// Autoplace sprite
globalThis.aPlaceSprites = {};
globalThis.aPlaceSprite = function(obj) {
    let tmpSprite = aPlaceSprites[obj.id + configs.grayVis * 100];
    if (!tmpSprite) {
        let tmpCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        tmpCanvas.width = tmpCanvas.height = obj.scale * 2.5 + outlineWidth + (items.list[obj.id].spritePadding || 0) + 15;
        let tmpContext = tmpCanvas.getContext("2d");
        tmpContext.drawImage(getMarkSprite(obj), 0, 0);
        tmpContext.globalCompositeOperation = "source-atop";
        tmpContext.globalAlpha = 0.5;
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#002574";
        tmpContext.fillRect(0, 0, tmpCanvas.width, tmpCanvas.height);
        tmpSprite = tmpCanvas;
        aPlaceSprites[obj.id + configs.grayVis * 100] = tmpSprite;
    }
    return tmpSprite;
}
// Replace sprite
globalThis.replaceSprites = {};
globalThis.replaceSprite = function(obj) {
    let tmpSprite = replaceSprites[obj.id + configs.grayVis * 100];
    if (!tmpSprite) {
        let tmpCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        tmpCanvas.width = tmpCanvas.height = obj.scale * 2.5 + outlineWidth + (items.list[obj.id].spritePadding || 0) + 15;
        let tmpContext = tmpCanvas.getContext("2d");
        tmpContext.drawImage(getMarkSprite(obj), 0, 0);
        tmpContext.globalCompositeOperation = "source-atop";
        tmpContext.globalAlpha = 0.6;
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#c3fff7";
        tmpContext.fillRect(0, 0, tmpCanvas.width, tmpCanvas.height);
        tmpSprite = tmpCanvas;
        replaceSprites[obj.id + configs.grayVis * 100] = tmpSprite;
    }
    return tmpSprite;
}
globalThis.preSprites = {};
// Preplace sprite
globalThis.preSprite = function(obj) {
    let tmpSprite = preSprites[obj.id + configs.grayVis * 100];
    if (!tmpSprite) {
        let tmpCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        tmpCanvas.width = tmpCanvas.height = obj.scale * 2.5 + outlineWidth + (items.list[obj.id].spritePadding || 0) + 15;
        let tmpContext = tmpCanvas.getContext("2d");
        tmpContext.drawImage(getMarkSprite(obj), 0, 0);
        tmpContext.globalCompositeOperation = "source-atop";
        tmpContext.globalAlpha = 0.5;
        tmpContext.fillStyle = "#8b3939";
        tmpContext.fillRect(0, 0, tmpCanvas.width, tmpCanvas.height);
        tmpSprite = tmpCanvas;
        preSprites[obj.id + configs.grayVis * 100] = tmpSprite;
    }
    return tmpSprite;
}
// RENDER GAME OBJECTS:
globalThis.fadeInSpeed = 0.05;
globalThis.fadeOutSpeed = 0.025;
globalThis.minOpacity = 0.1;
globalThis.maxOpacity = 1.0;
globalThis.fader = function(obj) {
    if (!obj.fadingOut) {
        obj.opacity2 += fadeInSpeed;
        if (obj.opacity2 >= maxOpacity) {
            obj.opacity2 = maxOpacity;
            obj.fadingOut = true;
        }
    } else {
        obj.opacity2 -= fadeOutSpeed;
        if (obj.opacity2 <= minOpacity) {
            obj.opacity2 = minOpacity;
            obj.fadingOut = false;
        }
    }
    return obj.opacity2;
}
globalThis.renderGameObjects = function(xOffset, yOffset, EightySix) {
    //const startTime = performance.now();
    let tmpSprite;
    let tmpX;
    let tmpY;
    const render = renderObj
    for (let i = 0; i < render.length; i++) {
        const tmpObj = render[i];
        tmpX = tmpObj.x + tmpObj.xWiggle - xOffset;
        tmpY = tmpObj.y + tmpObj.yWiggle - yOffset;
        tmpObj.update(delta);
        if (tmpObj.isVisible) mainContext.globalAlpha = 0.9;
        else mainContext.globalAlpha = tmpObj.alpha;
        if (tmpObj.isItem) {
            if ((tmpObj.dmg || tmpObj.trap) && !tmpObj.inClan) tmpSprite = getObjSprite(tmpObj, EightySix);
            else tmpSprite = larp ? getItemSpriteLARP(tmpObj, false) : getItemSprite(tmpObj, false, EightySix);
            mainContext.save();
            mainContext.translate(tmpX, tmpY);
            mainContext.rotate(tmpObj.dir);
            mainContext.drawImage(
                tmpSprite,
                -(tmpSprite.width * 0.5),
                -(tmpSprite.height * 0.5)
            );
            if (tmpObj.blocker) {
                mainContext.strokeStyle = "#db6e6e";
                mainContext.globalAlpha = 0.3;
                mainContext.lineWidth = 6;
                renderCircle(0, 0, tmpObj.blocker, mainContext, false, true);
            }
            mainContext.restore();
        } else {
            if (tmpObj.type == 0) {
                if (UWUTILS.getDist(tmpObj, player, 0, 0) <= 400 && tmpObj.alpha > 0.5) tmpObj.alpha -= 0.02
                else if (tmpObj.alpha < 1) tmpObj.alpha += 0.02
            }
            mainContext.globalAlpha = tmpObj.alpha;
            tmpSprite = larp ? getResSpriteLARP(tmpObj) : getResSprite(tmpObj);
            if (tmpObj.type === 4) drawVolcano(mainContext, tmpX, tmpY);
            else {
                mainContext.drawImage(
                    tmpSprite,
                    tmpX - tmpSprite.width * 0.5,
                    tmpY - tmpSprite.height * 0.5
                );
            }
        }
        if (tmpObj.type === null || tmpObj.invincible) {
            // HEALTH HOLDER:
            if ((tmpObj.health < tmpObj.maxHealth) && globalThis.hacking && !tmpObj.invincible && tmpObj.dist2 <= 500) {
                mainContext.fillStyle = darkOutlineColor;
                mainContext.roundRect(
                    tmpX - 50 * 0.5 - 4.5,
                    tmpY - 4.5,
                    50 + 4.5 * 2,
                    17,
                    8
                );
                mainContext.fill();
                // HEALTH BAR:
                mainContext.fillStyle =
                    tmpObj.owner && tmpObj.owner.sid == player.sid
                        ? "#05b97d"
                        : tmpObj.isTeamObject(player)
                            ? "#fceb65"
                            : "#cc5151";
                mainContext.roundRect(
                    tmpX - 50 * 0.5,
                    tmpY,
                    50 * (tmpObj.health / tmpObj.maxHealth),
                    17 - 4.5 * 2,
                    7
                );
                mainContext.fill();
            }
            let owner = findPlayerBySID(tmpObj.owner.sid);
            if (owner && tmpObj.dist2 <= 500) {
                let tmpText = owner.name;
                mainContext.font = "20px Hammersmith One";
                mainContext.fillStyle = "#fff";
                mainContext.textBaseline = "middle";
                mainContext.textAlign = "center";
                mainContext.lineWidth = 5.5;
                mainContext.lineJoin = "round";
                mainContext.strokeText(
                    tmpText,
                    tmpX,
                    tmpY - 7
                );
                mainContext.fillText(
                    tmpText,
                    tmpX,
                    tmpY - 7
                );
            }
        }
    }
    // PLACE VISIBLE:
    mainContext.globalAlpha = .45;
    for (let i = 0; i < placeVisible.length; i++) {
        let places = placeVisible[i];
        tmpX = places.x - xOffset;
        tmpY = places.y - yOffset;
        const sprite = places.auto ? aPlaceSprite(places) : places.pre ? preSprite(places) : places.re ? replaceSprite(places) : getMarkSprite(places);
        mainContext.save();
        mainContext.translate(tmpX, tmpY);
        mainContext.rotate(places.dir);
        mainContext.drawImage(sprite, -(sprite.width * 0.5), -(sprite.height * 0.5));
        mainContext.restore();
    }
    mainContext.globalAlpha = .45;
    for (let i = 0; i < breakObjs.length; i++) {
        const e = breakObjs[i];
        mainContext.fillStyle = e.assumeBreak ? "#c9346b" : "#2693c7";
        mainContext.strokeStyle = e.assumeBreak ? "#c9346b" : "#2693c7",
        mainContext.beginPath();
        mainContext.arc(e.x - xOffset, e.y - yOffset, e.scale * .35 , 0, PI2)
        mainContext.fill();
        mainContext.stroke();
    }
    //renderObjectsTime = performance.now() - startTime;
}
// RENDER MINIMAP:
class MapPing {
    constructor(color, scale) {
        this.init = function (x, y) {
            this.scale = 0;
            this.x = x;
            this.y = y;
            this.active = true;
        };
        this.update = function (ctxt, dlta) {
            if (this.active) {
                this.scale += 0.05 * dlta;
                if (this.scale >= scale) this.active = false;
                else {
                    ctxt.globalAlpha = 1 - mathMax(0, this.scale / scale);
                    ctxt.beginPath();
                    ctxt.arc(
                        (this.x / 14400) * mapDisplay.width,
                        (this.y / 14400) * mapDisplay.width,
                        this.scale,
                        0,
                        PI2
                    );
                    ctxt.stroke();
                }
            }
        };
        this.color = color;
    }
}
globalThis.getBorderColor = function() {
    if (configs.pinkUI) return "#ff9cfb";
    else return "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.8)";
}
globalThis.getBackgroundColor = function() {
    if (configs.pinkUI) return "rgb(93 0 131 / 40%)";
    else return "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7)";
}
globalThis.pingMap = function(x, y) {
    //hitBull(near.aim2)
    tmpPing = mapPings.find2((pings) => !pings.active);
    if (!tmpPing) {
        tmpPing = new MapPing("#fff", config.mapPingScale);
        mapPings.push(tmpPing);
    }
    tmpPing.init(x, y);
}
globalThis.updateMapMarker = function() {
    mapMarker.x = player.x;
    mapMarker.y = player.y;
}
globalThis.renderMinimap = function(dlta) {
    if (player && player.alive) {
        mapContext.clearRect(0, 0, mapDisplay.width, mapDisplay.height);
        // RENDER PINGS:
        mapContext.lineWidth = 4;
        for (let i = 0; i < mapPings.length; ++i) {
            tmpPing = mapPings[i];
            mapContext.strokeStyle = tmpPing.color;
            tmpPing.update(mapContext, dlta);
        }
        // RENDER BREAK TRACKS:
        mapContext.globalAlpha = 1;
        mapContext.fillStyle = "#ff0000";
        if (breakTrackers.length) {
            mapContext.fillStyle = "#abcdef";
            mapContext.font = "34px Lilita One";
            mapContext.textBaseline = "middle";
            mapContext.textAlign = "center";
            for (let i = 0; i < breakTrackers.length;) {
                mapContext.fillText(
                    "!",
                    (breakTrackers[i].x / 14400) * mapDisplay.width,
                    (breakTrackers[i].y / 14400) * mapDisplay.height
                );
                i += 2;
            }
        }
        // RENDER PLAYERS:
        mapContext.globalAlpha = 1;
        mapContext.fillStyle = configs.pinkUI ? "#ff82c1" : "rgb(200, 200, 200)";
        renderCircle(
            (player.x / 14400) * mapDisplay.width,
            (player.y / 14400) * mapDisplay.height,
            7,
            mapContext,
            true
        );
        mapContext.fillStyle = configs.pinkUI ? "rgba(255, 130, 193, 0.35)" : "rgb(200, 200, 200, 0.35)";
        if (player.team && minimapData) {
            for (let i = 0; i < minimapData.length;) {
                renderCircle(
                    (minimapData[i] / 14400) * mapDisplay.width,
                    (minimapData[i + 1] / 14400) * mapDisplay.height,
                    7,
                    mapContext,
                    true
                );
                i += 2;
            }
        }
        // DEATH LOCATION:
        if (lastDeath) {
            mapContext.fillStyle = "#bd0d5f";
            mapContext.font = "34px Hammersmith One";
            mapContext.textBaseline = "middle";
            mapContext.textAlign = "center";
            mapContext.fillText(
                "x",
                (lastDeath.x / 14400) * mapDisplay.width,
                (lastDeath.y / 14400) * mapDisplay.height
            );
        }
        /*
        // MAP MARKER:
        if (mapMarker) {
            mapContext.fillStyle = configs.pinkUI ? "#ffadd3" : "rgb(200, 200, 200)";
            mapContext.font = "34px Hammersmith One";
            mapContext.textBaseline = "middle";
            mapContext.textAlign = "center";
            mapContext.fillText(
                "x",
                (mapMarker.x / 14400) * mapDisplay.width,
                (mapMarker.y / 14400) * mapDisplay.height
            );
        }*/
    }
}
// ICONS:
globalThis.crossHair =
    "https://i.postimg.cc/Hn5QKCDZ/wmremove-transformed-removebg-preview-1.png";
globalThis.crossHairSprite = {};
globalThis.iconSprites = {};
globalThis.icons = ["crown", "skull"];
globalThis.loadIcons = function() {
    for (let i = 0; i < icons.length; ++i) {
        let tmpSprite = new Image();
        tmpSprite.onload = function () {
            this.isLoaded = true;
        };
        tmpSprite.src = "./../img/icons/" + icons[i] + ".png";
        iconSprites[icons[i]] = tmpSprite;
    }
    let tmpSprite = new Image();
    tmpSprite.onload = function () {
        crossHairSprite.isLoaded = true;
    };
    tmpSprite.src = crossHair;
    crossHairSprite = tmpSprite;
}
loadIcons();
globalThis.cdf = function(e, t) {
    try {
        return hypot(
            (t.y2 || t.y) - (e.y2 || e.y),
            (t.x2 || t.x) - (e.x2 || e.x)
        );
    } catch (e) {
        return Infinity;
    }
}
globalThis.squareCdf = function(e, t) {
    try {
        return ((t.y2 || t.y) - (e.y2 || e.y)) ** 2 + ((t.x2 || t.x) - (e.x2 || e.x)) ** 2;
    } catch (e) {
        return Infinity;
    }
}
globalThis.squareDist = function(e, t) {
    try {
        return e * e + t * t;
    } catch (e) {
        return Infinity;
    }
}
globalThis.moveCamera = function() {
    if (player) {
        if ((abs(player.oldPos.x2 - player.x2) + abs(player.oldPos.y2 - player.y2)) > 300) {
            camX = player.x2;
            camY = player.y2;
            return;
        }
        const tmpDist = squareDist(camX - player.x, camY - player.y);
        if (tmpDist > 0.01) {
            const tmpDir = fastAtan2(player.y - camY, player.x - camX);
            const realDist = sqrt(tmpDist);
            const camSpd = mathMin(realDist * (realDist > 200 ? 1 : 0.01167) * delta, realDist);
            camX += camSpd * cos(tmpDir);
            camY += camSpd * sin(tmpDir);
        } else {
            camX = player.x;
            camY = player.y;
        }
    } else camX = camY = 7200;
}
globalThis.interPolate = function(xOffset, yOffset) {
    //const startTime = performance.now();
    let lastTime = now - game.tickSpeed;
    let tmpDiff;
    for (let i = 0; i < players.length + ais.length; ++i) {
        let tmpObj = players[i] || ais[i - players.length];
        if (tmpObj && tmpObj.visible) {
            if (tmpObj.forcePos) {
                tmpObj.x = tmpObj.x2;
                tmpObj.y = tmpObj.y2;
                tmpObj.dir = tmpObj.d2;
            } else {
                let total = tmpObj.t2 - tmpObj.t1;
                let fraction = lastTime - tmpObj.t1;
                let ratio = fraction / total;
                tmpObj.dt += delta;
                let tmpRate = mathMin(1.7, tmpObj.dt / 170);
                tmpDiff = tmpObj.x2 - tmpObj.x1;
                tmpObj.x = tmpObj.x1 + tmpDiff * tmpRate;
                let tmpDiff2 = tmpObj.y2 - tmpObj.y1;
                tmpObj.y = tmpObj.y1 + tmpDiff2 * tmpRate;
                if (tmpObj.sid == player.sid && realDir > 0 && EightySix86) {
                    //lerpAngle: newDir, oldDir, ratio
                    //tmpObj.dir = lerpAngle(tmpObj.d2, tmpObj.d1, mathMin(1.2, ratio));
                    //tmpObj.dir = lerpAngle(visDir, tmpObj.d2, mathMin(1.2, ratio));
                    tmpObj.dir = lerpAngle(tmpObj.d2, tmpObj.d1, mathMin(1.2, ratio));
                } else tmpObj.dir = lerpAngle(tmpObj.d2, tmpObj.d1, mathMin(1.2, ratio));
            }
            tmpObj.relativeX = tmpObj.x - xOffset;
            tmpObj.relativeY = tmpObj.y - yOffset;
        }
    }
    //interPolateTime = performance.now() - startTime;
}
globalThis.elFiller = "#b6db66";
globalThis.elFiller2 = "#dbc666";
globalThis.elFiller3 = "#fff";
globalThis.elFiller4 = "#91b2db";
globalThis.enableLarpMode = function() {
    larp = true;
    larpModeActivated = true;
    elFiller = "#ffff00";
    elFiller2 = "#ff00ff";
    elFiller3 = "#00ff00";
    elFiller4 = "#441f00";
}
globalThis.disableLarpMode = function() {
    larp = false;
    larpModeActivated = false;
    elFiller3 = "#fff";
    if (configs.grayVis) filler2 = "rgba(13, 6, 60, 0.71)", filler = "rgba(12, 0, 60, 0.67)", elFiller = "#d0dbb7", elFiller2 = "#ddd19b", elFiller4 = "#bac8da";
    else elFiller = "#b6db66", elFiller2 = "#dbc666", elFiller4 = "#91b2db";
}
globalThis.drawBackground = function(xOffset, yOffset) {
    //const startTime = performance.now();
    /*if (configs.customBackground) {
        mainContext.drawImage(backgroundVideo, 0, 0, maxScreenWidth, maxScreenHeight);
        backgroundVideo.play();
    } else {*/
        if (config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset <= 0 && 14400 - config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset >= maxScreenHeight) {
            mainContext.fillStyle = elFiller;
            mainContext.fillRect(0, 0, maxScreenWidth, maxScreenHeight);
        } else if (14400 - config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset <= 0) {
            mainContext.fillStyle = elFiller2; // "#dbc666";
            mainContext.fillRect(0, 0, maxScreenWidth, maxScreenHeight);
        } else if (config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset >= maxScreenHeight) {
            mainContext.fillStyle = elFiller3;
            mainContext.fillRect(0, 0, maxScreenWidth, maxScreenHeight);
        } else if (config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset >= 0) {
            mainContext.fillStyle = elFiller3;
            mainContext.fillRect(0, 0, maxScreenWidth, config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset);
            if (config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset > 14400 - config.snowBiomeTop - maxScreenHeight) {
                mainContext.fillStyle = elFiller; //"#b6db66";
                mainContext.fillRect(0, config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset, maxScreenWidth, (14400 - config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset));
                mainContext.fillStyle = elFiller2; // "#dbc666";
                mainContext.fillRect(0, 14400 - config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset, maxScreenWidth, maxScreenHeight - (14400 - config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset));
            } else {
                mainContext.fillStyle = elFiller; //"#b6db66";
                mainContext.fillRect(0, config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset, maxScreenWidth, maxScreenHeight - (config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset));
            }
        } else {
            mainContext.fillStyle = elFiller; //"#b6db66";
            mainContext.fillRect(0, 0, maxScreenWidth, 14400 - config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset);
            mainContext.fillStyle = elFiller2; // "#dbc666";
            mainContext.fillRect(0, 14400 - config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset, maxScreenWidth, maxScreenHeight - (14400 - config.snowBiomeTop - yOffset));
        }
        // RENDER WATER AREAS:
        if (!firstSetup) {
            waterMult += waterPlus * config.waveSpeed * delta;
            if (waterMult >= config.waveMax) {
                waterMult = config.waveMax;
                waterPlus = -1;
            } else if (waterMult <= 1) waterMult = waterPlus = 1;
            mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
            mainContext.fillStyle = elFiller2; // "#dbc666";
            renderWaterBodies(xOffset, yOffset, mainContext, config.riverPadding);
            mainContext.fillStyle = elFiller4; //"#91b2db";
            renderWaterBodies(xOffset, yOffset, mainContext, (waterMult - 1) * 250);
        }
//}
    //drawBackgroundTime = performance.now() - startTime;
}
globalThis.renderVolcanoDamageZone = function(xOffset, yOffset) {
    const centerX = 14400 - xOffset;
    const centerY = 14400 - yOffset;
    const arcX = 13960 - xOffset;
    const arcY = 13960 - yOffset;
    mainContext.beginPath();
    mainContext.rect(12520 - xOffset, 12520 - yOffset, 1880, 1880);
    mainContext.moveTo(centerX, centerY);
    mainContext.arc(arcX, arcY, 1440, PI * 0.901, PI * 1.599);
    mainContext.closePath();
    mainContext.fillStyle = "rgba(0, 0, 0, .05)";
    mainContext.fill("evenodd");
    mainContext.strokeStyle = "rgba(0, 0, 0, .1)";
    mainContext.lineWidth = 4;
    mainContext.strokeRect(
        12520 - xOffset,
        12520 - yOffset,
        1880,
        1880
    );
    mainContext.beginPath();
    mainContext.moveTo(centerX, centerY);
    mainContext.arc(arcX, arcY, 1440, PI * 0.901, PI * 1.599);
    mainContext.closePath();
    mainContext.fillStyle = "rgba(255, 100, 60, 0.2)";
    mainContext.fill();
    mainContext.strokeStyle = "rgba(255, 100, 60, 0.3)";
    mainContext.lineWidth = 4;
    mainContext.stroke();
}
globalThis.renderAIs = function(xOffset, yOffset) {
    //const startTime = performance.now();
    for (let i = 0; i < ais.length; ++i) {
        let tmpObj = ais[i];
        if (tmpObj.active && tmpObj.visible) {
            tmpObj.animate(delta);
            mainContext.save();
            mainContext.translate(tmpObj.relativeX, tmpObj.relativeY);
            mainContext.rotate(tmpObj.dir + tmpObj.dirPlus - PI * 0.5);
            renderAI(tmpObj, mainContext);
            mainContext.restore();
        }
    }
    //renderAIsTime = performance.now() - startTime;
}
globalThis.drawGrid = function(xOffset, yOffset) {
    //const startTime = performance.now();
    if (!configs.showGrid) return;
    mainContext.lineWidth = 3;
    mainContext.strokeStyle = "#000";
    mainContext.globalAlpha = 0.05;
    mainContext.beginPath();
    let ratfrr = 60;
    for (var x = -xOffset % ratfrr; x < maxScreenWidth; x += ratfrr) {
        if (x > 0) {
            mainContext.moveTo(x, 0);
            mainContext.lineTo(x, maxScreenHeight);
        }
    }
    for (var y = -yOffset % ratfrr; y < maxScreenHeight; y += ratfrr) {
        if (y > 0) {
            mainContext.moveTo(0, y);
            mainContext.lineTo(maxScreenWidth, y);
        }
    }
    mainContext.stroke();
    //drawGridTime = performance.now() - startTime;
}
globalThis.renderMapBoundaries = function(xOffset, yOffset) {
    //const startTime = performance.now();
    mainContext.fillStyle = "#000";
    mainContext.globalAlpha = 0.09;
    if (xOffset <= 0) mainContext.fillRect(0, 0, -xOffset, maxScreenHeight);
    if (14400 - xOffset <= maxScreenWidth) {
        let tmpY = mathMax(0, -yOffset);
        mainContext.fillRect(
            14400 - xOffset,
            tmpY,
            maxScreenWidth - (14400 - xOffset),
            maxScreenHeight - tmpY
        );
    }
    if (yOffset <= 0) mainContext.fillRect(-xOffset, 0, maxScreenWidth + xOffset, -yOffset);
    if (14400 - yOffset <= maxScreenHeight) {
        let tmpX = mathMax(0, -xOffset);
        let tmpMin = 0;
        if (14400 - xOffset <= maxScreenWidth)
            tmpMin = maxScreenWidth - (14400 - xOffset);
        mainContext.fillRect(
            tmpX,
            14400 - yOffset,
            maxScreenWidth - tmpX - tmpMin,
            maxScreenHeight - (14400 - yOffset)
        );
    }
    //mapBoundariesTime = performance.now() - startTime;
}
let filler = (configs.grayVis ? "rgba(12, 0, 60, 0.67)" : configs.themeColor);
globalThis.filler2 = configs.grayVis ? "rgba(13, 6, 60, 0.71)" : configs.themeColor;
globalThis.renderShaders = function() {
    //const startTime = performance.now();
    mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
    if (larpModeActivated) {
        mainContext.fillStyle = "rgba(15, 0, 70, 0.2)";
        mainContext.fillRect(0, 0, maxScreenWidth, maxScreenHeight);
    } else if (configs.nightMode) {
        mainContext.fillStyle = filler2;
        mainContext.fillRect(0, 0, maxScreenWidth, maxScreenHeight);
    } else if (configs.darkMode) {
        mainContext.fillStyle = filler + "ba";
        mainContext.fillRect(0, 0, maxScreenWidth, maxScreenHeight);
    } else {
        mainContext.fillStyle = "rgba(15, 0, 70, 0.39)";
        mainContext.fillRect(0, 0, maxScreenWidth, maxScreenHeight);
    }
    //renderShaderTime = performance.now() - startTime;
}
globalThis.textWidthCache = new Map();
globalThis.getTextWidth = function(ctx, text, font) {
    if (!textWidthCache.has(text)) {
        if (font) ctx.font = font;
        textWidthCache.set(text, ctx.measureText(text).width);
    }
    return textWidthCache.get(text);
}
globalThis.textWidthCache2 = new Map();
globalThis.getTextWidth2 = function(ctx, text) {
    if (!textWidthCache2.has(text)) textWidthCache2.set(text, ctx.measureText(text).width);
    return textWidthCache2.get(text);
}
globalThis.buildNameTexture = function(obj) {
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    const fontSize = obj.nameScale || (configs.bigNames ? 55 : 30);
    ctx.font = fontSize + "px Lilita One";
    const text = obj.displayText;
    const width = ctx.measureText(text).width;
    canvas.width = width + 20;
    canvas.height = fontSize + 20;
    ctx.font = fontSize + "px Lilita One";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.lineWidth = 5.5;
    ctx.lineJoin = "round";
    const cx = canvas.width * 0.5;
    const cy = canvas.height * 0.5;
    ctx.strokeText(text, cx, cy);
    ctx.fillText(text, cx, cy);
    obj.nameCanvas = canvas;
    obj.nameDirty = false;
}
globalThis.nameplateCache = new Map();
globalThis.buildNameplate = function(name, color, team, EightySix, isPlayer) {
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
    const NAME_FONT = (configs.bigNames ? 55 : 30) + "px Lilita One";
    const TEAM_FONT = (configs.bigNames ? 25 : 20) + "px Lilita One";
    const nameText = name || "";
    const teamText = team ? team + "  " : "";
    ctx.font = NAME_FONT;
    const nameW = nameText ? ctx.measureText(nameText).width : 0;
    ctx.font = TEAM_FONT;
    const teamW = teamText ? ctx.measureText(teamText).width : 0;
    const width = nameW + teamW + 10;
    const height = 80;
    canvas.width = width;
    canvas.height = height;
    const cx = width * 0.5;
    const baseY = height * 0.5;
    ctx.strokeStyle = darkOutlineColor;
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.lineWidth = 5.5;
    ctx.lineJoin = "round";
    ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = "source-over";
    ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    if (nameText) {
        ctx.font = NAME_FONT;
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(185, 185, 185, 0.67)";
        ctx.strokeText(nameText, cx + teamW * 0.5, baseY);
        ctx.fillText(nameText, cx + teamW * 0.5, baseY);
        if (EightySix) {
            ctx.globalAlpha = 0.35;
            ctx.fillStyle = color == undefined ? "rgb(255, 255, 255)" : isPlayer ? "rgb(158, 205, 255)" : config.skinColors[color];
            ctx.fillText(nameText, cx + teamW * 0.5, baseY);
            ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
        }
    }
    if (teamText) {
        ctx.font = TEAM_FONT;
        ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(185, 185, 185, 0.67)";
        const offsetX = -nameW * 0.5;
        ctx.strokeText(teamText, cx + offsetX, baseY);
        ctx.fillText(teamText, cx + offsetX, baseY);
        if (EightySix) {
            ctx.fillStyle = color == undefined ? "rgb(255, 255, 255)" : isPlayer ? "rgb(199, 226, 255)" : config.skinColors[color];
            ctx.globalAlpha = 0.35;
            ctx.fillText(teamText, cx + offsetX, baseY);
            ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
        }
    }
    return canvas;
}
globalThis.getNameplateKey = function(name, color, team, isPlayer) {
    return name + (EightySix86 ? ("|" + isPlayer + "|" + color) : "") + "|" + (team || "");
}
globalThis.getNameplate = function(name, color, team, isPlayer) {
    const key = getNameplateKey(name, color, team, isPlayer);
    let sprite = nameplateCache.get(key);
    if (!sprite) {
        sprite = buildNameplate(name, color, team, EightySix86, isPlayer);
        nameplateCache.set(key, sprite);
    }
    return sprite;
}
globalThis.generateRandomColor = function(existingColors) {
    const hue = Math.floor(Math.random() * 360);
    const saturation = Math.floor(Math.random() * 81) + 20; // Saturation range: 20-100
    const lightness = Math.floor(Math.random() * 61) + 20; // Lightness range: 20-80
    const newColor = `hsl(${hue}, ${saturation}%, ${lightness}%)`;
    if (!existingColors) return newColor;
    if (existingColors.some(({color}) => color &&isSimilarColor(color, newColor))) {
        // If a similar color exists in the array, recursively generate a new color
        return generateRandomColor(existingColors);
    }
    return newColor;
}
globalThis.renderEntityExtras = function(EightySix) {
    //const startTime = performance.now();
    const ctx = mainContext;
    const localPlayer = player;
    const cfg = config;
    const cfgs = configs;
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    ctx.strokeStyle = darkOutlineColor;
    if (localPlayer && autoQuadSpike && nearestTeammate) {
        ctx.save();
        ctx.strokeStyle = "rgb(92, 0, 0)";
        renderCircle(
            nearestTeammate.relativeX,
            nearestTeammate.relativeY,
            40,
            ctx,
            false,
            true
        );
        ctx.restore();
    }
    ctx.textBaseline = "middle";
    ctx.textAlign = "center";
    for (let i = 0, len = players.length; i < len; i++) {
        const obj = players[i];
        if (!obj.visible) continue;
        const isBot = botSIDs.includes(obj.sid);
        const x = obj.relativeX;
        const y = obj.relativeY;
        const scale = obj.scale;
        const isLocal = obj.sid === localPlayer.sid;
        ctx.globalAlpha = (isLocal && !obj.visible) ? EightySix ? 0.45 : 0.5 : isBot ? 0.3 : EightySix ? 0.95 : obj.opacity;
        if ((obj.sid === realPlayerSid || !isBot) && obj.displayText) {
            const plate = getNameplate(EightySix ? obj.name : obj.displayText, obj.skinColor, obj.team, isLocal);
            const iconSize = cfg.crownIconScale;
            const px = x - plate.width * 0.5 - iconSprites.skull.isLoaded * config.iconPad;
            const py = y - scale - cfg.nameY - plate.height * 0.5;
            ctx.drawImage(plate, px, py);
            const baseY = y - scale - cfg.nameY;
            if (obj.isLeader && iconSprites.crown.isLoaded) {
                ctx.drawImage(
                    iconSprites.crown,
                    x - iconSize * 0.5,
                    baseY - iconSize * 0.5 - 35,
                    iconSize,
                    iconSize
                );
            }
            if (obj.iconIndex === 1 && iconSprites.skull.isLoaded) {
                ctx.drawImage(
                    iconSprites.skull,
                    x + plate.width * 0.5 - config.iconPad,
                    baseY - iconSize * 0.5 - 2,
                    iconSize,
                    iconSize
                );
            }
            if (
                obj.isPlayer &&
                (shameInsta || waitInsta) &&
                nearHacker === obj &&
                crossHairSprite.isLoaded &&
                enemy.length
            ) {
                ctx.drawImage(crossHairSprite, x - 60, y - 58, 120, 120);
            }
        }
        if (obj.health > 0) {
            const hpRatio = obj.health / obj.maxHealth;
            ctx.fillRect(
                x,
                y - scale - cfg.nameY - scale - 10,
                obj.width * hpRatio,
                10
            );
            ctx.fillStyle = darkOutlineColor;
            ctx.roundRect(
                x - (EightySix  ? 0.75 : 1) * (50) - cfg.healthBarPad,
                y + scale + cfg.nameY - EightySix * 10,
                EightySix  ? 50 * 1.5 + cfg.healthBarPad * 2 : 50 * 2 + cfg.healthBarPad * 2,
                17,
                8
            );
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.fillStyle = (obj.sid === player.sid || (obj.team && obj.team === localPlayer.team)) ? EightySix ? "#4bb6c4" : "#8ecc51" : EightySix ? "#cc517a" : "#cc5151";
            ctx.roundRect(
                x - (EightySix  ? 0.75 : 1) * (50),
                y + scale + cfg.nameY + cfg.healthBarPad - EightySix * 10,
                EightySix  ? 50 * 1.5 * hpRatio : 50 * 2 * hpRatio,
                17 - cfg.healthBarPad * 2,
                7
            );
            ctx.fill();
        }
        if (obj.isPlayer && EightySix) {
                ctx.globalAlpha = !obj.visible ? 0.15 : 0.3;
                ctx.lineWidth = 2;
                ctx.font = "20px Lilita One";
                ctx.shadowColor = ctx.fillStyle = (obj.sid === player.sid ? "#168eff" : obj.realColor);
                ctx.shadowBlur = 2;
                ctx.strokeText(obj.sid, x, y);
                ctx.fillText(obj.sid, x, y);
                ctx.lineWidth = 5.5;
        }
        ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
        if (obj.isPlayer && (obj.sid === realPlayerSid || !isBot)) {
            ctx.globalAlpha = isBot ? 0.3 : (isLocal && !obj.visible) ? 0.5 : obj.opacity;
            obj.updateShameTimer();
            ctx.font = "35px Starjedi One";
            let gegagedigedagedago = obj.shameCount;
            ctx.shadowBlur = 0;
            if (obj.shameTimer === 0) ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${gegagedigedagedago > 5 ? "100" : "255"}, ${gegagedigedagedago > 4 ? "0, 0" : "255, 255"})`;
            else {
                gegagedigedagedago = (round(obj.shameTimer * 10)) / 10;
                ctx.fillStyle = configs.pinkUI ? "pink" : "white";
            }
            ctx.strokeText(gegagedigedagedago, x, y - 45);
            ctx.fillText(gegagedigedagedago, x, y - 45);
            if (!isLocal && obj.hacker) {
                ctx.font = "25px Lilita One";
                ctx.strokeText(Math.round(obj.pinge), x, y + scale + cfg.nameY + 15);
                ctx.fillText(Math.round(obj.pinge), x, y + scale + cfg.nameY + 15);
                ctx.font = "35px Lilita One";
                ctx.fillStyle = cfgs.pinkUI ? "pink" : "rgb(200,200,200)";
                const text = obj.isTurk ? "Turk" : obj.guessHack ?? "Hacker";
                ctx.strokeText(
                    text,
                    x,
                    y + scale + cfg.nameY + 35
                );
                ctx.fillText(
                    text,
                    x,
                    y + scale + cfg.nameY + 35
                );
            }
        }
    }
    mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
    for (let i = 0, len = ais.length; i < len; i++) {
        const obj = ais[i];
        if (!obj.visible) continue;
        const x = obj.relativeX;
        const y = obj.relativeY;
        const scale = obj.scale;
        ctx.globalAlpha = obj.opacity;
        if (obj.displayText) {
            const plate = getNameplate(
                obj.displayText,
                null,
                0,
                obj.skinColor
            );
            ctx.drawImage(
                plate,
                x - plate.width * 0.5,
                y - scale - cfg.nameY - plate.height * 0.5
            );
        }
        if (obj.health > 0) {
            const hpRatio = obj.health / obj.maxHealth;
            ctx.fillRect(
                x,
                y - scale - cfg.nameY - scale - 10,
                obj.width * hpRatio,
                10
            );
            ctx.fillStyle = darkOutlineColor;
            ctx.roundRect(
                x - 50 - cfg.healthBarPad,
                y + scale + cfg.nameY,
                50 * 2 + cfg.healthBarPad * 2,
                17,
                8
            );
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.fillStyle = EightySix ? "#cc517a" : "#cc5151";
            ctx.roundRect(
                x - 50,
                y + scale + cfg.nameY + cfg.healthBarPad,
                50 * 2 * hpRatio,
                17 - cfg.healthBarPad * 2,
                7
            );
            ctx.fill();
            ctx.font = "20px Lilita One";
            ctx.fillStyle = "rgba(185, 185, 185, 0.67)";
            ctx.strokeText(obj.health, x, y + scale + cfg.nameY + cfg.healthBarPad + 30);
            ctx.fillText(obj.health, x, y + scale + cfg.nameY + cfg.healthBarPad + 30);
        }
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    //renderExtrasTime = performance.now() - startTime;
}
globalThis.renderChats = function() {
    //const startTime = performance.now();
    for (let i = 0; i < players.length; ++i) {
        let tmpObj = players[i];
        if (tmpObj.visible) {
            if (tmpObj.chatMessages.length > 0) {
                tmpObj.chatCountdown -= delta;
                if (tmpObj.chatCountdown <= 0) tmpObj.chatCountdown = 0;
                mainContext.font = "32px Lilita One";
                for (let x = tmpObj.chatMessages.length;x > 0;) {
                    x--;
                    const chatMessage = tmpObj.chatMessages[x];
                    let tmpSize = mainContext.measureText(chatMessage);
                    mainContext.textBaseline = "middle";
                    mainContext.textAlign = "center";
                    let tmpX = tmpObj.relativeX;
                    let tmpY = tmpObj.relativeY - 125 - 55 * x;
                    let tmpH = 47;
                    let tmpW = tmpSize.width + 17;
                    mainContext.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.2)";
                    mainContext.roundRect(tmpX - tmpW * 0.5, tmpY - tmpH * 0.5, tmpW, tmpH, 6);
                    mainContext.fill();
                    mainContext.fillStyle = "#fff";
                    mainContext.fillText(chatMessage, tmpX, tmpY);
                }
            }
            if (tmpObj.chat.count > 0) {
                tmpObj.chat.count -= delta;
                if (tmpObj.chat.count <= 0) tmpObj.chat.count = 0;
                mainContext.font = "32px Lilita One";
                let tmpSize = mainContext.measureText(tmpObj.chat.message);
                mainContext.textBaseline = "middle";
                mainContext.textAlign = "center";
                let tmpX = tmpObj.relativeX;
                let tmpY = tmpObj.relativeY + 55 * 2;
                let tmpH = 47;
                let tmpW = tmpSize.width + 17;
                mainContext.fillStyle = "rgba(0,0,0,0.2)";
                mainContext.roundRect(tmpX - tmpW * 0.5, tmpY - tmpH * 0.5, tmpW, tmpH, 6);
                mainContext.fill();
                mainContext.fillStyle = tmpObj.chat.color;//"#ffffff99";
                mainContext.fillText(tmpObj.chat.message, tmpX, tmpY);
            } else tmpObj.chat.count = 0;
        }
    }
    //renderChatsTime = performance.now() - startTime;
}
globalThis.renderFrameCircle = function() {
    //const startTime = performance.now();
    if (circle1.length) {
        for (let i = 0; i < circle1.length; i++) {
            const tmpPlayer = circle1[i];
            mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
            mainContext.beginPath();
            mainContext.lineWidth = 5;
            const gradient = mainContext.createRadialGradient(tmpPlayer.relativeX, tmpPlayer.relativeY, 25, tmpPlayer.relativeX, tmpPlayer.relativeY, 80);
            gradient.addColorStop(0, "red");
            gradient.addColorStop(0.4, "rgba(0, 0, 0, 1)");
            gradient.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)");
            mainContext.strokeStyle = gradient;
            mainContext.arc(player.relativeX, player.relativeY, player.frameDist, 0, PI2);
            mainContext.stroke();
        }
    }
    if (circle2.length) {
        for (let i = 0; i < circle2.length; i++) {
            let tmpPlayer = circle2[i];
            mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
            mainContext.beginPath();
            mainContext.lineWidth = 6;
            const gradient = mainContext.createRadialGradient(tmpPlayer.relativeX, tmpPlayer.relativeY, 25, tmpPlayer.relativeX, tmpPlayer.relativeY, 80);
            gradient.addColorStop(0, configs.pinkUI ? "pink" : "rgb(200, 200, 200");
            gradient.addColorStop(1, "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)");
            mainContext.strokeStyle = gradient;
            mainContext.arc(player.relativeX, player.relativeY, 238, 0, PI2);
            mainContext.stroke();
        }
    }
    //frameCircleTime = performance.now() - startTime;
}
globalThis.renderCursor = function(xOffset, yOffset) {
    mainContext.globalAlpha = .8;
    mainContext.beginPath();
    mainContext.shadowBlur = 9;
    mainContext.shadowColor = "#2d92e4";
    mainContext.fillStyle = "#2d92e4";
    const outerRadius = wbe * 10
    const innerRadius = wbe * 2.5;
    const x = camX + (mouseX - window.innerWidth * 0.5) * window.devicePixelRatio * wbe - xOffset, y = camY + (mouseY - window.innerHeight * 0.5) * window.devicePixelRatio * wbe - yOffset;
    const gradient = mainContext.createRadialGradient(x, y, innerRadius, x, y, outerRadius);
    gradient.addColorStop(0, "rgba(45,146,228,0.6)");
    gradient.addColorStop(0.1, "rgba(45,146,228,0.4)");
    gradient.addColorStop(0.7, "rgba(45,146,228,0.01)");
    gradient.addColorStop(1, "rgba(45,146,228,0)");
    mainContext.fillStyle = gradient;
    mainContext.beginPath();
    mainContext.arc(x, y, outerRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    mainContext.fill();
    mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
    mainContext.shadowBlur = 0;
}
// UPDATE GAME:
globalThis.rotation = 0;
globalThis.decreasing = false;
globalThis.shittingItself = false;
globalThis.updateGame = function() {
    //const startTime = performance.now();
    mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
    if (larpModeActivated) {
        mainContext.translate(maxScreenWidth * 0.5, maxScreenHeight * 0.5);
        if (!shittingItself) {
            if (Math.random() < 0.0001) shittingItself = true;
        } else if (shittingItself && Math.random() < 0.005) {
            shittingItself = false;
            rotation = 0;
        }
        if (shittingItself) rotation += Math.random() - 0.5, mainContext.rotate(rotation);
        if (rotation >= 0.5) decreasing = true;
        if (decreasing) rotation -= 0.0001, mainContext.rotate(-0.0001);
        else rotation += 0.0001, mainContext.rotate(0.0001);
        mainContext.translate(-maxScreenWidth * 0.5, -maxScreenHeight * 0.5);
    }
    // MOVE CAMERA:
    moveCamera()
    const xOffset = camX - maxScreenWidth * 0.5;
    const yOffset = camY - maxScreenHeight * 0.5;
    // INTERPOLATE PLAYERS AND AI:
    interPolate(xOffset, yOffset)
    if (player && Tach?.goal?.pathing){
        mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
        mainContext.save();
        mainContext.translate(-l, -c);
        Pathfinder?.drawPath(mainContext, '#ffffff', player, "#00FF00");
        Tach?.drawWaypoints(mainContext, player.skinRot);
        mainContext.restore();
    }
    // RENDER CORDS:
    // RENDER BACKGROUND
    drawBackground(xOffset, yOffset)
    // VOLCANO DAMAGE ZONE:
    // no longer needed since volc is gone
    //renderVolcanoDamageZone(xOffset, yOffset);
    // RENDER DEAD PLAYERS:
    //renderDeadPlayers(xOffset, yOffset);
    // RENDER PROJECTILES:
    mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
    mainContext.lineWidth = outlineWidth;
    // RENDER PLAYERS:
    // RENDER AI:
    mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
    renderAIs(xOffset, yOffset);
    // RENDER GRID:
    drawGrid(xOffset, yOffset)
    renderGameObjects(xOffset, yOffset, EightySix86);
    renderPlayers(xOffset, yOffset);
    mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
    renderProjectiles(xOffset, yOffset);
    renderConnectionLines(xOffset, yOffset);
    // MAP BOUNDARIES:
    renderMapBoundaries(xOffset, yOffset);
    // RENDER DAY/NIGHT TIME:
    renderShaders();
    // RENDER PLAYER AND AI UI:
    renderEntityExtras(EightySix86);
    mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
    // RENDER ANIM TEXTS:
    textManager.update(delta, mainContext, xOffset, yOffset);
    // RENDER CHAT MESSAGES:
    renderChats(xOffset, yOffset);
    mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
    // RENDER MINIMAP:
    renderMinimap(delta);
    // FRAME CIRCLE:
    renderFrameCircle(xOffset, yOffset);
    renderCursor(xOffset, yOffset);
    if (drawPos) {
        mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
        mainContext.beginPath();
        mainContext.shadowBlur = 2;
        mainContext.shadowColor = "#1657b996";
        mainContext.fillStyle = "#1657b996";
        mainContext.arc(drawPos.x - xOffset, drawPos.y - yOffset, 17, 0, PI2);
        mainContext.fill();
        mainContext.shadowBlur = 0;
    }
    /*
    mainContext.lineWidth = 3;
    if (drawDir != null) {
        mainContext.save();
        mainContext.beginPath();
        mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
        mainContext.strokeStyle = "#ffffff"
        const d1 = drawPosition.x - xOffset, d2 = drawPosition.y - yOffset;
        mainContext.moveTo(d1, d2);
        mainContext.lineTo(drawPosition.toX - xOffset, drawPosition.toY - yOffset);
        mainContext.stroke();
        mainContext.restore();
        if (drawDir2 != null) {
            mainContext.save();
            mainContext.beginPath();
            mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
            mainContext.strokeStyle = "#ffffff"
            const d3 = drawPosition2.x - xOffset, d4 = drawPosition2.y - yOffset;
            mainContext.moveTo(d3, d4);
            mainContext.lineTo(drawPosition2.toX - xOffset, drawPosition2.toY - yOffset);
            mainContext.stroke();
            mainContext.restore();
        }
    }
    if (drawDir3 != null) {
        mainContext.save();
        mainContext.beginPath();
        mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
        mainContext.strokeStyle = "#000000"
        const d5 = drawPosition3.x - xOffset, d6 = drawPosition3.y - yOffset;
        mainContext.moveTo(d5, d6);
        mainContext.lineTo(drawPosition3.toX - xOffset, drawPosition3.toY - yOffset);
        mainContext.stroke();
        mainContext.restore();
        if (drawDir4 != null) {
            mainContext.save();
            mainContext.beginPath();
            mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
            mainContext.strokeStyle = "#000000"
            const d7 = drawPosition4.x - xOffset, d8 = drawPosition4.y - yOffset;
            mainContext.moveTo(d7, d8);
            mainContext.lineTo(drawPosition4.toX - xOffset, drawPosition4.toY - yOffset);
            mainContext.stroke();
            mainContext.restore();
        }
    }*/
    if (drawLine) {
        mainContext.save();
        mainContext.beginPath();
        mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
        mainContext.strokeStyle = "#ffffff"
        mainContext.moveTo(player.x - xOffset, player.y - yOffset);
        mainContext.lineTo(drawLine.x + player.x - xOffset, player.y - yOffset + drawLine.y)
        mainContext.stroke();
        mainContext.restore();
    }
    /*
    mainContext.save();
    mainContext.globalAlpha = 1;
    mainContext.translate(player.x - xOffset, player.y - yOffset - 200);
    const img = playerBaseCache.get('40|11|10|0|0');
    mainContext.drawImage(img, -img.width * 0.5, -img.height * 0.5);
    mainContext.restore();
    //gameUpdateTime = performance.now() - startTime;*/
}

globalThis.fpsEl = getEl("pingFps");

globalThis.lastFPS = 0;
globalThis.lastFrameTime = 0;
globalThis.lastTopRightUiSync = 0;
globalThis.updateUpdate = function() {
    //rape modulus
    const startTime = performance.now();
    now = performance.now();
    delta = now - lastUpdate;
    lastUpdate = now;
    window.requestAnimationFrame(updateUpdate);
    const diff = now - fpsTimer.last;
    if (diff >= 1000) {
        fpsTimer.ltime = fpsTimer.time * (1000 / diff);
        fpsTimer.last = now;
        fpsTimer.time = 0;
    }
    fpsTimer.time++;
    if (!document.hidden) {
        const FPS = Math.round(fpsTimer.ltime);
        if (FPS !== lastFPS) {
            lastFPS = FPS;
            fpsEl.textContent = `${Math.round(window.pingTime * 10) / 5}ms | Fps: ${FPS}`;
        }
        if (lastSecPacket !== secPacket) {
            lastSecPacket = secPacket;
            packetEl.textContent = secPacket + " / 0." + Math.round(secPacketRatio * 1000);
        }
    }
    updateGame();
    ms.avg = round((ms.min + ms.max) * 0.5);
}
globalThis.$n = 0;
globalThis.cf = function(e) {
    $n != e && ($n = e,
                createStore())
}
globalThis.createStore = function() {
    if (player) {
        UWUTILS.removeAllChildren(storeHolder);
        const e = $n, t = e ? store.accessories : store.hats;

        for (let i = 0; i < t.length; ++i)
            t[i].dontSell || (function(s) {
                if (t[s].id == 13) return;
                const n = UWUTILS.generateElement({
                    id: "storeDisplay" + s,
                    class: "storeItem",
                    onmouseout: showItemInfo(),
                    onmouseover: () => showItemInfo(t[s], !1, !0),
                    parent: storeHolder
                });

                UWUTILS.hookTouchEvents(n, !0);

                const imgSrc = "./img/" + (e ? "accessories/access_" : "hats/hat_") + t[s].id + (t[s].topSprite ? "_p" : "") + ".png";
                const imgElement = UWUTILS.generateElement({ tag: "img", class: "hatPreview smallImage", src: imgSrc, parent: n });

                const textElement = UWUTILS.generateElement({
                    tag: "span",
                    style: "font-size: 85%; vertical-align: middle; line-height: 1.2;",
                    text: `[${t[s].id}] ${t[s].name}`,
                    parent: n
                });

                if ((e ? !player.tails[t[s].id] : !player.skins[t[s].id])) {
                    UWUTILS.generateElement({
                        class: "joinAlBtn",
                        style: "margin-top: 5px; font-size: 85%; vertical-align: middle; line-height: 1.51;",
                        text: "Buy",
                        onclick: () => storeBuy(t[s].id, e),
                        hookTouch: !0,
                        parent: n
                    });
                    UWUTILS.generateElement({ tag: "span", class: "itemPrice", style: "font-size: 85%; vertical-align: middle; line-height: 1.51;", text: t[s].price/1000>=1?t[s].price/1000+"k":t[s].price, parent: n });
                } else {
                    const equipText = (e ? player.tailIndex : lastHat) == t[s].id ? "Unequip" : "Equip";
                    UWUTILS.generateElement({
                        class: "joinAlBtn",
                        style: "margin-top: 5px; font-size: 85%; vertical-align: middle; line-height: 1.51;",
                        text: equipText,
                        onclick: () => (equipText == "Unequip" ? storeEquip2(0, e) : storeEquip2(t[s].id, e)),
                        hookTouch: !0,
                        parent: n
                    });
                }
            })(i);
    }
}

globalThis.closeChat = function() {
    chatBox.value = "";
    chatHolder.style.display = "none";

globalThis.toggleChat = function() {
    //console.log("toggle")
    if (chatHolder.style.display == "block") {
        if (chatBox.value) {
            if (globalThis.hacking) packet("6", chatBox.value);
            else packet2("6", chatBox.value);
        }
        chatHolder.style.display = "none";
    } else {
        storeMenu.style.display = "none";
        allianceMenu.style.display = "none";
        chatHolder.style.display = "block";
        chatBox.focus();
    }
    chatBox.value = "";
}
window.prepareUI = function (tmpObj) {
    resize();
    // CHAT STUFF:
    var chatBox = document.getElementById("chatBox");
    var chatHolder = document.getElementById("chatHolder");
    var suggestBox = document.createElement("div");
    suggestBox.id = "suggestBox";
    var prevChats = [];
    var prevChatsIndex = 0;
    // ACTION BAR:
    UWUTILS.removeAllChildren(actionBar);
    for (let i = 0; i < 64; i++) {
        const id = i & 3;
        UWUTILS.generateElement({
            id: "actionBarItem" + Math.floor(i * 0.25) + (id >= 2 ? (id == 3 ? "_r" : "_d") : (id == 1 ? "_g" : "")),
            class: "actionBarItem",
            style: "display:none; box-shadow: 2px 2px 5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)",
            onmouseout: function () {
                showItemInfo();
            },
            parent: actionBar,
        });
    }
    for (let i = 16; i < 39; i++) {
        UWUTILS.generateElement({
            id: "actionBarItem" + i,
            class: "actionBarItem",
            style: "display:none; box-shadow: 2px 2px 5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5)",
            onmouseout: function () {
                showItemInfo();
            },
            parent: actionBar,
        });
    }
    for (let j = 0; j < 87; j++) {
        (function (i) {
            const id = i & 3;
            const variant = id >= 2 ? (id == 3 ? "_r" : "_d") : (id == 1 ? "_g" : "");
            let tmpI = Math.floor(i * 0.25);
            let tmpCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
            tmpCanvas.width = tmpCanvas.height = 66;
            let tmpContext = tmpCanvas.getContext("2d");
            tmpContext.translate(tmpCanvas.width * 0.5, tmpCanvas.height * 0.5);
            tmpContext.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
            tmpContext.webkitImageSmoothingEnabled = false;
            tmpContext.mozImageSmoothingEnabled = false;
            if (items.weapons[tmpI]) {
                tmpContext.rotate(PI * 1.25);
                let tmpSprite = new Image();
                toolSprites[items.weapons[tmpI].src + variant] = tmpSprite;
                tmpSprite.onload = function () {
                    this.isLoaded = true;
                    let tmpPad = 1 / (this.height / this.width);
                    let tmpMlt = items.weapons[tmpI].iPad || 1;
                    tmpContext.drawImage(
                        this,
                        -(tmpCanvas.width * tmpMlt * config.iconPad * tmpPad) * 0.5,
                        -(tmpCanvas.height * tmpMlt * config.iconPad) * 0.5,
                        tmpCanvas.width * tmpMlt * tmpPad * config.iconPad,
                        tmpCanvas.height * tmpMlt * config.iconPad
                    );
                    tmpContext.fillStyle = "rgba(0, 0, 70, 0.2)";
                    tmpContext.globalCompositeOperation = "source-atop";
                    tmpContext.fillRect(
                        -tmpCanvas.width * 0.5,
                        -tmpCanvas.height * 0.5,
                        tmpCanvas.width,
                        tmpCanvas.height
                    );
                    getEl("actionBarItem" + tmpI + variant).style.backgroundImage = "url(" + tmpCanvas.toDataURL() + ")";
                };
                tmpSprite.src = "./../img/weapons/" + items.weapons[tmpI].src + variant + ".png";
                let tmpUnit = getEl("actionBarItem" + tmpI + variant);
                // tmpUnit.onmouseover = UWUTILS.checkTrusted(function () {
                //     showItemInfo(items.weapons[tmpI], true);
                // });
                tmpUnit.onclick = UWUTILS.checkTrusted(function () {
                    selectWeapon(tmpI < 9 ? tmpObj.primaryIndex : tmpObj.secondaryIndex);
                });
            } else {
                tmpI = i - 48;
                let tmpSprite = larp ? getItemSpriteLARP(tmpObj, true) : getItemSprite(items.list[tmpI - 16], true, EightySix86);
                let tmpScale = mathMin(
                    tmpCanvas.width - config.iconPadding,
                    tmpSprite.width
                );
                tmpContext.globalAlpha = 1;
                tmpContext.drawImage(
                    tmpSprite,
                    -tmpScale * 0.5,
                    -tmpScale * 0.5,
                    tmpScale,
                    tmpScale
                );
                tmpContext.fillStyle = "rgba(0, 0, 70, 0.1)";
                tmpContext.globalCompositeOperation = "source-atop";
                tmpContext.fillRect(-tmpScale * 0.5, -tmpScale * 0.5, tmpScale, tmpScale);
                getEl("actionBarItem" + tmpI).style.backgroundImage =
                    "url(" + tmpCanvas.toDataURL() + ")";
                let tmpUnit = getEl("actionBarItem" + tmpI);
                // tmpUnit.onmouseover = UWUTILS.checkTrusted(function () {
                //     showItemInfo(items.list[tmpI - 16]);
                // });
                tmpUnit.onclick = UWUTILS.checkTrusted(function () {
                    selectToBuild(tmpObj.items[tmpObj.getItemType(tmpI - 16)]);
                });
            }
        })(j);
    }
};
globalThis.isAnimating = false;
globalThis.menuVisible = false;
globalThis.toggleMenu = function(homeScreenVisible) {
    if (isAnimating) return;
    isAnimating = true;
    if (homeScreenVisible || menuVisible) {
        container.style.opacity = "0";
        if (imageOverlay) imageOverlay.style.display = "none";
        setTimeout(() => {
            container.style.visibility = "hidden";
            menuVisible = false;
            isAnimating = false;
        }, 300);
    } else {
        Object.assign(container.style, { visibility: "visible", opacity: "1" });
        if (imageOverlay) imageOverlay.style.display = "flex";
        menuVisible = true;
        isAnimating = false;
    }
}
document.addEventListener("keydown", ({ key, target }) => {
    if (
        key === "Escape" &&
        !isAnimating &&
        !["allianceMenu", "storeMenu", "mainMenu"].some(
            (id) => document.querySelector(`#${id}`).style.display !== "none"
        ) &&
        target.id.toLowerCase() !== "chatbox"
    ) {
        toggleMenu(false);
    }
});

globalThis.injectStyles = function() {
    const existingStyle = document.getElementById("remedy-theme-styles");
    if (existingStyle) existingStyle.remove();
    const styleElement = document.createElement("style");
    styleElement.id = "remedy-theme-styles";
    styleElement.innerHTML = `
    :root {
        --primary-color: ${configs.accentColor || (configs.pinkUI ? "#ff9cfb" : "#50afef")};
        --outline-color: ${configs.outlineColor || "#e0e0e0"};
        --secondary-color: var(--outline-color);

globalThis.injectScrollbarStyles = function() {
    const style = document.createElement("style");
    style.textContent = `
                #menu-container {
                    max-height: 100vh;
                    backdrop-filter: blur(5px);
                }
                #menu-container::-webkit-scrollbar {
                    width: 8px;
                }
                #menu-container::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
                    background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
                    border-radius: 10px;
                }
                #menu-container::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
                    background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.2);
                    border-radius: 10px;
                    transition: background 0.3s;
                }
                #menu-container::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
                    background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.5);
                }
            `;
    document.head.appendChild(style);
}
globalThis.createMenu = function() {
    injectStyles();
    injectScrollbarStyles();
    container = document.createElement("div");
    container.id = "menu-container";
    container.style.visibility = "hidden";
    container.style.opacity = "0";
    document.body.appendChild(container);
    imageOverlay = document.createElement("div");
    imageOverlay.className = "menu-image-overlay";
    imageOverlay.style.display = "none";
    document.body.appendChild(imageOverlay);
    function addMenuImage(src, alt, goldAmt, onClick) {
        const item = document.createElement("div");
        item.className = "menu-image-item";
        const img = document.createElement("img");
        img.className = "menu-image";
        img.src = src;
        img.alt = alt;
        const label = document.createElement("span");
        label.className = "menu-image-label";
        img.addEventListener("click", () => {
            if (player.points >= goldAmt && onClick) {
                item.style.backgroundColor = "#76ad79";
                onClick();
            }
        });
        item.appendChild(img);
        item.appendChild(label);
        imageOverlay.appendChild(item);
    }
    addMenuImage("https://moomoo.io/img/hats/hat_15.png", "image 1", 600, () => {

    const leftPanel = document.createElement("div");
    leftPanel.className = "left-panel";
    container.appendChild(leftPanel);
    const featurePanel = document.createElement("div");
    featurePanel.className = "feature-panel";
    featurePanel.id = "feature";
    container.appendChild(featurePanel);
    panels.forEach((panel) => {
        const panelElement = document.createElement("div");

        panelElement.style.display = "none";
        featurePanel.appendChild(panelElement);
        panelElements[panel] = panelElement;

    function createInfoTooltip() {
        let tooltip = document.getElementById("menu-info-tooltip");
        if (tooltip) return tooltip;
        tooltip = document.createElement("div");
        tooltip.id = "menu-info-tooltip";
        tooltip.style.position = "absolute";
        tooltip.style.overflow = "hidden";
        tooltip.style.width = "300px";
        tooltip.style.height = "80px";
        tooltip.style.color = "white";
        tooltip.style.backgroundColor = "rgba(54, 50, 67, 0.93)";
        tooltip.style.boxShadow = "rgb(60, 60, 60) 0px 0px 10px";
        tooltip.style.borderRadius = "8px";
        tooltip.style.backdropFilter = "blur(2px)";
        const title = document.createElement("div");
        title.className = "menu-info-tooltip-title";
        title.style.position = "relative";
        title.style.top = "5px";
        const body = document.createElement("div");
        body.className = "menu-info-tooltip-body";
        tooltip.appendChild(title);
        tooltip.appendChild(body);
        document.body.appendChild(tooltip);
        return tooltip;
    }

    function attachInfoTooltip(target, header, description) {
        if (!description) return;
        const tooltip = createInfoTooltip();
        const title = tooltip.querySelector(".menu-info-tooltip-title");
        const body = tooltip.querySelector(".menu-info-tooltip-body");
        target.classList.add("has-info-tooltip");
        let hoverTimer, hideTimer;
        const showTooltip = () => {
            title.textContent = header;
            body.textContent = description;
            tooltip.style.display = "block";
            const targetRect = target.getBoundingClientRect();
            const tooltipRect = tooltip.getBoundingClientRect();
            tooltip.style.left = `${Math.max(5, Math.min(targetRect.left + targetRect.width / 2 - tooltipRect.width / 2, window.innerWidth - tooltipRect.width - 5))}px`;
            tooltip.style.top = `${targetRect.top - tooltipRect.height - 10 + (top < 5) * 20}px`;
            tooltip.style.display = "block";
            tooltip.style.opacity = "0";
            requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                tooltip.style.opacity = "1";
            });
        };
        target.onmouseenter = (event) => {
            clearTimeout(hoverTimer);
            clearTimeout(hideTimer);
            hoverTimer = setTimeout(showTooltip, 100);
        };
        //target.addEventListener("mousemove", moveTooltip);
        target.onmouseleave = () => {
            clearTimeout(hoverTimer);
            clearTimeout(hideTimer);
            tooltip.style.opacity = "0";
            hideTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                tooltip.style.display = "none";
            }, 180);
        };
    }
    function createSwitch(labelText, configKey, parentPanel, defaultChecked = false, changeHandler = null, infoDescription = null, infoHeader = null, alignRight = false) {
        const switchContainer = document.createElement("div");
        switchContainer.className = "switch-container";
        if (alignRight) switchContainer.classList.add("switch-right");
        const labelSpan = document.createElement("span");
        labelSpan.className = "switch-label-text";
        labelSpan.textContent = ` ${labelText} `;
        const checkbox = document.createElement("input");
        checkbox.type = "checkbox";
        checkbox.className = "switch-checkbox";
        checkbox.id = `${configKey}-checkbox`;
        checkbox.checked = defaultChecked;
        const label = document.createElement("label");
        label.className = "switch-label";
        label.setAttribute("for", `${configKey}-checkbox`);
        switchContainer.appendChild(labelSpan);
        switchContainer.appendChild(checkbox);
        switchContainer.appendChild(label);
        panelElements[parentPanel].appendChild(switchContainer);
        if (infoDescription) attachInfoTooltip(switchContainer, infoHeader || labelText, infoDescription);
        if (changeHandler) checkbox.addEventListener("change", changeHandler);
        return checkbox;
    }
    function customColor(labelText, configKey, parentPanel, defaultColor, infoDescription = null, infoHeader = null) {
        const switchContainer = document.createElement("div");
        switchContainer.className = "switch-container";
        const labelSpan = document.createElement("span");
        labelSpan.className = "switch-label-text";
        labelSpan.textContent = ` ${labelText} `;
        const colorInput = document.createElement("input");
        colorInput.type = "color";
        colorInput.id = `${configKey}-color`;
        colorInput.value = defaultColor;
        colorInput.style.width = "50px";
        colorInput.style.height = "30px";
        colorInput.style.cursor = "pointer";
        colorInput.style.border = "none";
        colorInput.style.borderRadius = "4px";
        switchContainer.appendChild(labelSpan);
        switchContainer.appendChild(colorInput);
        panelElements[parentPanel].appendChild(switchContainer);
        if (infoDescription) attachInfoTooltip(switchContainer, infoHeader || labelText, infoDescription);
        return colorInput;
    }

    function customNumber(labelText, configKey, parentPanel, defaultValue, width, height, min = 0, max = 1, step = "0.01", infoDescription = null, infoHeader = null) {
        const switchContainer = document.createElement("div");
        switchContainer.className = "switch-container";
        const labelSpan = document.createElement("span");
        labelSpan.className = "switch-label-text";
        labelSpan.textContent = ` ${labelText} `;
        const numberInput = document.createElement("input");
        numberInput.type = "text";
        numberInput.id = `${configKey}-number`;
        numberInput.value = defaultValue;
        numberInput.style.width = width;
        numberInput.style.height = height;
        numberInput.style.cursor = "text";
        numberInput.style.border = "1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04)";
        numberInput.style.borderRadius = "10px";
        numberInput.style.background = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05)";
        numberInput.style.color = "var(--foreground-color)";
        numberInput.style.outline = "none";
        switchContainer.appendChild(labelSpan);
        switchContainer.appendChild(numberInput);
        panelElements[parentPanel].appendChild(switchContainer);
        if (infoDescription) attachInfoTooltip(switchContainer, infoHeader || labelText, infoDescription);
        return numberInput;
    }
    function updateSliderAccent(slider) {
        const min = Number(slider.min) || 0;
        const max = Number(slider.max) || 100;
        const value = Number(slider.value) || 0;
        const percent = max === min ? 0 : ((value - min) / (max - min)) * 100;
        slider.style.setProperty("--slider-fill", `${Math.max(0, Math.min(100, percent))}%`);
    }
    function sliderNumber(labelText, configKey, parentPanel, defaultValue, width1, height1, width2, height2, min = 0, max = 1, step = "0.01", infoDescription = null, infoHeader = null) {
        const switchContainer = document.createElement("div");
        switchContainer.className = "switch-container";
        const labelSpan = document.createElement("span");
        labelSpan.className = "switch-label-text";
        labelSpan.textContent = ` ${labelText} `;
        const slider = document.createElement("input");
        slider.type = "range";
        slider.id = `${configKey}-slider`;
        slider.min = min;
        slider.max = max;
        slider.step = step;
        slider.style.width = width2;
        slider.style.height = height2;
        slider.style.borderRadius = "2px";
        slider.style.cursor = "pointer";
        slider.style.border = "1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0)";
        slider.style.background = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05)";
        slider.value = defaultValue;
        slider.style.setProperty("--slider-accent", "var(--toggle-color)");
        slider.style.outline = "none";
        const numberInput = document.createElement("input");
        numberInput.type = "text";
        numberInput.id = `${configKey}-number`;
        textAreas.push(numberInput.id);
        numberInput.value = defaultValue;
        numberInput.style.textAlign = "center";
        numberInput.style.width = width1;
        numberInput.style.height = height1;
        numberInput.style.cursor = "text";
        numberInput.style.border = "1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04)";
        numberInput.style.borderRadius = "8px";
        numberInput.style.background = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05)";
        numberInput.style.color = "var(--foreground-color)";
        numberInput.style.outline = "none";
        switchContainer.appendChild(labelSpan);
        switchContainer.appendChild(slider);
        switchContainer.appendChild(numberInput);
        (typeof parentPanel == "string" ? panelElements[parentPanel] : parentPanel).appendChild(switchContainer);
        if (infoDescription) attachInfoTooltip(switchContainer, infoHeader || labelText, infoDescription);
        return { slider, numberInput };
    }
    function sectionHeader(labelText, parentPanel) {
        const header = document.createElement("div");
        header.className = "section-header";
        header.textContent = labelText;
        panelElements[parentPanel].appendChild(header);
    }
    function createDropdown(labelText, configKey, parentPanel, options = [], defaultValue = null, changeHandler = null, infoDescription = null, infoHeader = null) {
        const switchContainer = document.createElement("div");
        switchContainer.className = "switch-container";
        const labelSpan = document.createElement("span");
        labelSpan.className = "switch-label-text";
        labelSpan.textContent = ` ${labelText} `;
        const select = document.createElement("select");
        select.className = "dropdown-select";
        select.id = `${configKey}-dropdown`;
        for (const option of options) {
            const optionElement = document.createElement("option");
            optionElement.value = String(option.value);
            optionElement.textContent = option.label;
            select.appendChild(optionElement);
        }
        if (defaultValue !== null) select.value = String(defaultValue);
        if (changeHandler) select.addEventListener("change", changeHandler);
        switchContainer.appendChild(labelSpan);
        switchContainer.appendChild(select);
        panelElements[parentPanel].appendChild(switchContainer);
        if (infoDescription) attachInfoTooltip(switchContainer, infoHeader || labelText, infoDescription);
        return select;
    }
    // HOME PANEL - MAIN
    sectionHeader("MAIN", "home");
    function toHex(n) {
        return Math.round(n * 255).toString(16).padStart(2, "0");
    }
    function customText(labelText, configKey, parentPanel, defaultText, infoDescription = null, infoHeader = null) {
        textAreas.push(configKey + "-text");
        const switchContainer = document.createElement("div");
        switchContainer.className = "switch-container";
        const labelSpan = document.createElement("span");
        labelSpan.className = "switch-label-text";
        labelSpan.textContent = ` ${labelText} `;
        const text = document.createElement("input");
        text.type = "text";
        text.id = `${configKey}-text`;
        text.value = defaultText;
        switchContainer.appendChild(labelSpan);
        switchContainer.appendChild(text);
        panelElements[parentPanel].appendChild(switchContainer);
        if (infoDescription) attachInfoTooltip(switchContainer, infoHeader || labelText, infoDescription);
        return text;
    }
    function bindSliderToConfig(control, configKey, defaultValue, min, max) {
        const setValue = (rawValue) => {
            let value = parseFloat(rawValue);
            if (Number.isNaN(value)) value = defaultValue;
            value = Math.max(min, Math.min(max, value));
            control.slider.value = value;
            control.numberInput.value = value;
            configs[configKey] = value;
            localStorage.setItem(configKey, value);
        };

        control.slider.addEventListener("input", () => setValue(control.slider.value));
        control.slider.addEventListener("change", () => setValue(control.slider.value));

        control.numberInput.addEventListener("input", () => setValue(control.numberInput.value));
        control.numberInput.addEventListener("change", () => setValue(control.numberInput.value));

        setValue(configs[configKey] ?? defaultValue);
    }

    function createAdvancedPlacersWindow() {
        let overlay = document.getElementById("placers-advanced-overlay");
        if (overlay) {
            container.style.visibility = "hidden";
            overlay.style.display = "flex";
            return;
        }
        overlay = document.createElement("div");
        overlay.id = "placers-advanced-overlay";
        overlay.className = "advanced-popup-overlay";
        const popup = document.createElement("div");
        popup.className = "advanced-popup-window";
        const topBar = document.createElement("div");
        topBar.className = "advanced-popup-topbar";
        const title = document.createElement("div");
        title.className = "advanced-popup-title";
        title.textContent = "Placer Configuration";
        const disclaimer = document.createElement("div");
        disclaimer.className = "advanced-popup-disclaimer";

        const closeButton = document.createElement("button");
        closeButton.className = "advanced-popup-close";
        closeButton.textContent = "×";
        closeButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
            overlay.style.display = "none";
            container.style.visibility = "visible";
        });
        topBar.appendChild(title);
        topBar.appendChild(disclaimer);
        topBar.appendChild(closeButton);
        const content = document.createElement("div");
        content.className = "advanced-popup-content";
        popup.appendChild(topBar);
        popup.appendChild(content);
        overlay.appendChild(popup);
        document.body.appendChild(overlay);
        overlay.addEventListener("mousedown", (event) => {
            if (event.target === overlay) overlay.style.display = "none";
        });
        const weightGroups = [{

                { label: "Spike Bounce Weight", key: "spikeBounceWeight", defaultValue: 1, description: "Adjusts preplacer priority for placing spikes that bounce enemies into other spikes" }
            ]
        }];

        for (let i = 0;i < weightGroups.length;i++) {
            const group = weightGroups[i];
            //console.log(i, group);
            if (group.newLine) {
                const lineBreak = document.createElement("br");
                content.appendChild(lineBreak);
            }
            const groupElement = document.createElement("div");
            groupElement.className = "advanced-weight-group";
            const header = document.createElement("div");
            header.className = "section-header";
            header.textContent = group.header;

            groupElement.appendChild(header);
            const subKey = group.header + "_";
            for (const weight of group.weights) {
                const key = subKey + weight.key;
                const control = sliderNumber(
                    weight.label,
                    weight.key,
                    groupElement,
                    String(configs[key] ?? weight.defaultValue),
                    "42px",
                    "20px",
                    "130px",
                    "2px",
                    weight.min ?? 0,
                    weight.max ?? 10,
                    weight.step ?? "0.01",
                    weight.description,
                    weight.header
                );
                bindSliderToConfig(
                    control,
                    key,
                    weight.defaultValue,
                    weight.min ?? 0,
                    weight.max ?? 10
                );
            }

            content.appendChild(groupElement);
        }
    }

    }
