    const sectionIcons = [
    "https://i.imgur.com/Da9LKoE.png",
    "https://i.imgur.com/sR5JnTE.png",
    "https://i.imgur.com/0fz1qiE.png",
    "https://i.imgur.com/cJOwD3n.png",
    "https://i.imgur.com/g6p10wB.png",
    "https://i.imgur.com/XWv7qI9.png",
    "https://i.postimg.cc/yghs87Tk/image.png",
    "https://i.postimg.cc/BnvcMYFS/image.png",
    "https://i.imgur.com/9fbjRuw.png"
    ];
    sections.forEach((section, index) => {
        const sectionButton = document.createElement("button");
        sectionButton.textContent = section;
        sectionButton.className = "menu-button";
        const icon = document.createElement("div");
        icon.style.setProperty("--icon", `url(${sectionIcons[index]})`);
        icon.alt = `${section} Icon`;
        icon.style.width = "20px";
        icon.style.height = "20px";
        icon.style.marginRight = "10px";
        icon.className = "colored-icon"
        sectionButton.prepend(icon);
        leftPanel.appendChild(sectionButton);
        sectionButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
            Object.keys(panelElements).forEach(p => {
                panelElements[p].style.display = 'none';
                panelElements[p].classList.remove('active-panel');
            });
            if (panel.style) panel.style.background = "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1)";
            sectionButton.style.background = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.2)";
            panel = sectionButton
            const panelKey = section.replace(" ", "").toLowerCase();
            const blockLayoutPanels = ['placers', 'clans', 'joinrequests'];
            panelElements[panelKey].style.display = blockLayoutPanels.includes(panelKey) ? "block" : "grid";
            panelElements[panelKey].classList.add("active-panel");
            if (section === "Join Requests") renderJoinRequests();
        });
    });
    panelElements["home"].style.display = "grid";
    panelElements["home"].classList.add("active-panel");
    featurePanel.style.overflow = "auto";
    featurePanel.style.scrollbarWidth = "none";
    ensureJoinRequestsPanel();
    renderJoinRequests();
}
createMenu();

// Load saved color configs
globalThis.savedAccentColor = configs.accentColor;
globalThis.savedMainColor = configs.mainColor;
globalThis.savedTextColor = configs.textColor;
//const savedOutlineColor = localStorage.getItem("outlineColor");

if (savedAccentColor) {
    const accentInput = document.getElementById("accentColor-color");
    if (accentInput) {
        accentInput.value = savedAccentColor;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--primary-color", savedAccentColor);
        configs.accentColor = savedAccentColor;
    }
}
if (savedMainColor) {
    const mainInput = document.getElementById("mainColor-color");
    if (mainInput) {
        mainInput.value = savedMainColor;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--background-color", savedMainColor);
        configs.mainColor = savedMainColor;
    }
}
if (savedTextColor) {
    const textInput = document.getElementById("textColor-color");
    if (textInput) {
        textInput.value = savedTextColor;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--foreground-color", savedTextColor);
        configs.textColor = savedTextColor;
    }
}
/*
if (savedOutlineColor) {
    const outlineInput = document.getElementById("outlineColor-color");
    if (outlineInput) {
        outlineInput.value = savedOutlineColor;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--outline-color", savedOutlineColor);
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--secondary-color", savedOutlineColor);
        configs.outlineColor = savedOutlineColor;
    }
}
*/
globalThis.savedToggleColor = configs.toggleColor;
if (savedToggleColor) {
    const toggleInput = document.getElementById("toggleColor-color");
    if (toggleInput) {
        toggleInput.value = savedToggleColor;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--toggle-color", savedToggleColor);
        configs.toggleColor = savedToggleColor;
    }
}
globalThis.savedMenuOpacity = configs.menuOpacity;
if (savedMenuOpacity) {
    const opacityInput = document.getElementById("menuOpacity-number");
    if (opacityInput) {
        opacityInput.value = savedMenuOpacity;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--menu-opacity", savedMenuOpacity);
        configs.menuOpacity = parseFloat(savedMenuOpacity);
    }
}
globalThis.savedElementOpacity = configs.elementOpacity;
if (savedElementOpacity) {
    const opacityInput = document.getElementById("backgroundOpacity-number");
    if (opacityInput) {
        opacityInput.value = savedElementOpacity;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--element-opacity", savedElementOpacity);
        configs.elementOpacity = parseFloat(savedElementOpacity);
    }
}
globalThis.savedMenuColor = configs.menuColor;
if (savedMenuColor) {
    const menuColorInput = document.getElementById("menuColor-color");
    if (menuColorInput) {
        configs.menuColor = menuColorInput.value = savedMenuColor;
        document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--menu-background-color", savedMenuColor);
    }
}

globalThis.selectors = [
    "#pre-content-container",
    "#chatButton",
    "#partyButton",
    "#promoImgHolder",
    "#linksContainer2",
    "#partyJoinButton",
    "#joinPartyButton",
    "span",
];
document.querySelectorAll(selectors.join(",")).forEach((element) => {
    if (
        element.tagName === "SPAN" &&
        element.textContent.trim() === "Join Party"
    ) {
        element.remove();
    } else if (element.tagName !== "SPAN") element.remove();
});
rangeElement = getEl("apRange-text");
chMainDiv = document.querySelector(".chMainDiv");
chMainDiv.style.scrollbarWidth = "none";
menuDiv.style.display = "none";
menuDiv.style.visibility = "hidden";
